# Ligou — design critique of the live site (landing + dashboard `#ligou`)

**Date:** 2026-09-01 · **Scope:** design only, copy untouched · **Status:** findings for RJ to decide; nothing applied.

## What was reviewed (source identity, proven)

- Live URL: https://client-nine-taupe-24.vercel.app (landing) and `/dashboard/#ligou`.
- Deployed source: worktree `/Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI`, branch `codex/v0.2-browser-pilot`, commit `2df576e` (2026-09-01 01:24 PDT). Its `dist/client/` (built 01:25) holds the exact live bundle `dashboard/assets/index-BD5I2sSu.js`; Vercel serves that asset with `last-modified 2026-09-01 08:26 UTC`. Deploy record: `docs/release/V0.2-M2-ONBOARDING-FINALIZATION.md` → Vercel `dpl_3uU9FQWQ3hG7G3n4gHGxcDHWcbVY`.
- Landing files (`index.html`, `assets/js/ligou-app9.js`) are byte-identical to commits from 2026-08-20 on that branch and unchanged since; this is the Claude Design v9 export.
- The newer worktree `openclaw-company-discovery-stage0` (commits later on 2026-09-01) is NOT live: its dashboard builds to 588 KB with a Discovery view; the live bundle is 553 KB with zero "discovery" strings.
- `/Users/d1f/Desktop/Ligou-MVP/dist/client` is stale (2026-08-22 build).

## Method

Two fleets, 7 critic lenses each (typography, layout/responsive, colour/brand, interaction/motion/states, IA + Nielsen + personas, accessibility WCAG 2.2 AA, fidelity to `.impeccable.md` / brand guide / approved mock), measured live in the internal Browser pane at 390×844, 768×1024 and 1440×900. Findings merged, then the top-ranked ones adversarially verified by independent agents (verdict CONFIRMED / ADJUSTED / REFUTED). Copy was declared frozen.

- **Landing:** Find: 7/7 lenses returned, 120 raw findings · Merge: 67 consolidated findings (53 dropped as duplicates) · Verify: capping at 24; 43 lowest-ranked findings left unverified · Verify: 23 survived, 1 refuted, 43 unverified

- **Dashboard:** Find: 7/7 lenses returned, 82 raw findings · Merge: 47 consolidated findings (35 dropped as duplicates) · Verify: capping at 22; 25 lowest-ranked findings not verified (listed as unverified) · Verify: 22 survived, 0 refuted, 25 unverified


## Decisions that are RJ's (the fleets flagged, did not decide)


**Landing**

- Three CTAs say 'Falar com o Ligou' and all three deliver a transcript to read. Until a demo number exists, is a demo you read the proof the contract means — and should the console at least visibly play when he arrives, or is the static picture the intended product?
- The v9 intro prints 'Ligou? Atendido.' at 110px and then the hero prints it again 0.8s later. Does the splash earn 2.45s on every load, or is it the 'deslumbramento tecnológico' the brand guide rules out — would you keep it at 0.9s once per session, or drop it and lose 'ligou.ai · agente operacional'?
- At 768–1199 the proof board renders a 4–7px conversation and a 6px 'Aprovar encaixe'. Is the tablet/split-view band a device you care about, or should the console simply own everything under 1200 as .impeccable.md line 15 already promises?
- 'Quero aproveitar a oferta' is the only orange control in the price block and it scrolls to itself. Until checkout exists, what should the highest-intent click on the page do — open the demo, open mailto:suporte@ligou.ai, or nothing?

**Dashboard**

- If the decision card is the reason this tab exists, why does the heading block (badge, h1, rule, date) get 115px above the fold before the first bubble — what would break if the date joined the badge on one row and the h1 lost its rule?
- The mock was drawn on a 922px-tall canvas with 35px buttons and ~10px text; the build correctly grew the touch targets and type, but kept the mock's vertical rhythm. Which one is the approved artefact — the pixels, or the promise 'decide without scrolling'?
- The Supabase mapper never sets urgencyLabel, isNewClient or location, so in production every case wears 'Pedido urgente · mesmo dia', 'Novo cliente' and 'San Rafael, CA'. How many other 'fixture-only' defaults are silently shipping as invented state?
- Orange is spent on the CTA, the badge, the rail, the node, the kicker and the active nav — all on-brand — yet the one orange the owner must read ('Aprovar') is the only pair the brand guide's contrast table would reject. Is white-on-orange a decision RJ made, or a default nobody questioned?


---

# Landing page (Claude Design v9)


## Overall

The bones are right: a specific voice, a real design system underneath, a mobile hero that does one job, and a proof fold whose mobile console is the best-read block on the page. What fails is execution at the seams — the proof fold that every CTA points to arrives finished and static, its captions sit at 2:1 contrast, it collapses to 4–7px type across 768–1199, and the one sentence that explains the product model ('02 Suas regras') is invisible on a phone because a percentage gradient lands under it. Six button-shaped things do nothing (including the only orange primary in the price block, which scrolls to itself), and a 2.45s splash gates the primary action on every load. None of this needs new copy or touches a frozen decision; it is CSS, a few attributes, and one visibility gate.


## AI-slop verdict

Not slop in substance: roofer, caixa postal, an English call summarised in Portuguese, a hand-composed proof board with an SVG causal trail, real tokens, no invented metrics or testimonials. The wrapper, however, is the template kit — cinematic splash with progress bar, two-row industry marquee, backdrop-blur sticky nav, reveal-on-scroll on everything, a 7%-alpha watermark word — exactly what the contract excludes ('a linha telefônica laranja é o único elemento decorativo recorrente'). The decisive tell is where effort went: motion is spent on ceremony while the one animation that carries meaning (the console sequence) fires at mount where nobody sees it, and the desktop board has zero motion; orange, the one semantic colour, is spent on a 92px non-clickable kicker and a headline fragment. Verdict: authored content in a generated wrapper — strip the kit, keep the board.


## Nielsen heuristics — 20/50


| # | Heuristic | Score | Key issue |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Visibility of system status | 2 | Progress bar, FAQ state and active-nav dot exist, but five mockup buttons and the desktop 'Ver de novo' span give no feedback (L06, L16); the console sequence finishes off-screen so the reader never sees the system 'work' (L04). |
| 2 | Match between system and the real world | 3 | Language of work is strong; English dialogue is read in a Portuguese voice by screen readers (L11) and the mobile console header ('Ligou em chamada', static 00:12) asserts a live call it is not (L15). |
| 3 | User control and freedom | 1 | 2.45s splash on every load with click-only skip and no keyboard/scroll dismissal (L08); pricing primary scrolls the user ~300px back to its own heading (L10); Termos/Privacidade throw the reader to the top (L24); no mobile section navigation (L23). |
| 4 | Consistency and standards | 2 | Same-looking buttons are live or inert at random (L06); 'Ver de novo' is a button on mobile, a span on desktop (L16); 7 h2 sizes, 14 label specs, two orange-button colour pairs (L13, L14, L07). |
| 5 | Error prevention | 2 | Orange 'Aprovar encaixe' is the largest action-coloured surface inside the proof and does nothing; the mobile console carries no visible 'Exemplo' label to frame it (L06, L15). |
| 6 | Recognition rather than recall | 2 | Speaker/language labels at 2:1 make the reader infer who is talking (L02); step '02 Suas regras' disappears on phones (L03); mobile replay is an unlabelled icon (L04); tab title and share preview are a dev artefact (L12). |
| 7 | Flexibility and efficiency of use | 1 | 9.4 viewports to the price with no jump links below 920px (L23); primary CTA readable only ~3.7s after load and the splash replays on back-navigation (L08); six purposeless tab stops (L06). |
| 8 | Aesthetic and minimalist design | 2 | Hero and proof are strong, but orange is spent on a 92px kicker and a headline fragment against Principle 1 (L09), h2/lede/label sizes drift across 22 values (L13, L14), and Preço+CTA+Footer stack 2.4 viewports of continuous dark before the closing fold (L17). |
| 9 | Help users recognize, diagnose, recover from errors | 2 | Noscript fallback exists but covers only two sections and a failed bundle still yields a white page (L22); dead controls fail silently (L06, L10, L24). |
| 10 | Help and documentation | 3 | FAQ answers the real objections and the langnote explains the EN example, but the mobile console itself never says 'Exemplo' and both summary asides are unnamed landmarks (L15). |

## Cognitive load — 6 failed

- 1. One primary action per decision point — the price block's only orange primary links to its own section (L10); inside the proof, orange 'Aprovar encaixe' competes with the real CTA colour and does nothing (L06, L07); desktop hero adds a peer-weight ghost CTA that skips the proof.
- 4. Consistent patterns — inert controls styled as live ones (L06); span vs button vs unlabelled icon for the same 'Ver de novo' (L04, L16); two colour pairs for the orange button (L07); four eyebrow/kicker treatments and 14 label specs (L14).
- 5. Clear visual hierarchy — #diferenca's 44–58px orange kicker outranks its 30–38px h2 and A dor spends the accent on a headline fragment (L09); silent sections outsize the price h2 on desktop (L13); a fixed 10px 'Decisão de exceção' becomes the largest drawer label at 768 (L01).
- 6. Recognition over recall — speaker/language captions at 2:1 (L02); '02 Suas regras' invisible on phones (L03); replay icon-only on mobile (L04); no visible 'Exemplo' on the mobile console (L15).
- 7. Reading burden — 9911px / 11.7 viewports with no wayfinding (L23); proof labels at 9–11px on desktop and 4–7px on tablets (L01, L14); nav primary at 11.5px (L20); 11 running-text sizes between 12 and 17px (L13).
- 8. Motion and distraction — mandatory 2.45s splash with the frozen signature printed twice (L08); the one animation that explains (console sequence) plays 2.5 viewports off-screen and is finished before arrival (L04); desktop board has no motion but shows a fake replay control (L16).

## Working well (keep)

- Mobile hero fold does exactly one job: eyebrow → 50.7px h1 with calibrated .lmask (0.18px descender clip) → 3-line lede → single 346x53 orange CTA at y≈392 → trust line, all above y 513, ghost CTA removed below 768, dedicated vertical loop with matching poster.
- Mobile console (.lc-*) is the best-read block on the page and the right model for the tablet band: 16px/600 bubbles at ~30 ch/line, speaker carried by alignment + avatar, event rows between turns, real 44px replay and 'Ver relato completo' with aria-expanded/aria-controls, PT sheet attached below — the frozen EN→PT visual done properly.
- Token discipline where the DS is actually used: .lg-btn--accent pairs ink-950 on orange (5.59:1, the certified brand pair) on hero/nav/pricing/final; small orange text on paper uses #b83216; teal marks 'Regra consultada' and 'Resumo em português' as confirmation; reduced-motion and noscript paths are complete end to end.

## Priority issues


### [P1] The primary action is gated behind a 2.45s navy splash on every load and back-navigation: CTA readable at ~3.7s, a ~700ms blank hero frame at hand-off, the frozen 'Ligou? Atendido.' printed twice, skip is an undocumented click only (no keyboard, no scroll), caption at 4.36:1.

**Why:** The user is a phone user with a missed call on his mind; four seconds of ceremony before 'Falar com o Ligou' reads as latency and is the 'deslumbramento tecnológico' brand p.8 forbids. Nielsen H3/H7 fail; keyboard users cannot dismiss it. Reduced-motion users skip it entirely, proving the page works without it.

**Fix:** Keep Intro4 (approved v9 element, keeps the string 'ligou.ai · agente operacional'). In ligou-app9.jsx:17-40: sessionStorage guard 'ligou-intro' (LGFX_REDUCED || seen → fire() immediately, return null); timers t1 450ms → setTxt(1), t2 900ms → setOut(true)+fire() in one hand-off so .play lands while the veil slides (drop t3); root becomes <button className='intro intro4…' onClick={skip} onKeyDown Enter/Space/Escape aria-label='Pular introdução' autoFocus> with index.html:28 `.intro{appearance:none;border:0;padding:0;background:none}`, plus passive wheel/touchmove → skip; render intro 'Atendido.' without <span class='acc'> (app9:35) so the splash stops being a fourth large orange surface; index.html:40 .intro__cap alpha .45 → .62 (7.2:1); :41 .intro__bar 1.75s → .9s. Scope hero stagger to .hero4 only (`.play .hero4 .hd/.ln{transition-duration:.5s}`), --d 0/60/160/260/380/480ms in app9:177-189. Result: first-visit CTA ≈1.9s, repeat ≈1.0s. Separate yes/no for RJ: remove <Intro4/> entirely (drops one string).

Findings: L08 · skill: `/quieter`


### [P1] The proof fold — destination of every CTA — is illegible on tablets and split-view laptops and its captions are ghosts everywhere: cqw type with no floor gives 4.3–10px labels/bubbles at 768–1100 (a 6px 'Aprovar encaixe', 5px EN→PT conversation) while the fixed-px mobile console is hidden until 767px; speaker/language labels and timestamps use --text-muted at 2.0–2.2:1 on paper in both consoles.

**Why:** Principle 5: the demo is the proof and the primary action. .impeccable.md line 15 promises an own composition for 768–1199 and the hero already has that 'mid' band (useHeroBand) — CallDemo has none. Who-is-speaking and in-which-language are the caption of the frozen EN→PT visual; at 2:1 in daylight the console reads as an ordinary chat. Five lenses measured this independently.

**Fix:** (1) Tablet band = the console: create @media (max-width:1199px) before the 767 block and MOVE into it index.html:501 (.p7-cq{display:none}), :504 (.p7m{display:block;margin-top:28px}) and the whole .lc-* block :505-550 (incl. @keyframes lcin and .p7-foot override); leave :497-500 and :502-503 in the ≤767 block; add @media (min-width:768px) and (max-width:1199px){.lc-shell{max-width:480px}}. (2) ≥1200: no px floors (they desync the %-positioned poster); raise the smallest multipliers — .p7-blbl .72→.82cqw (:489), .p7-clabel/.p7-cmuted/.p7-stpill .77→.80cqw (:448,:454,:458), .p7-dlab .82→.86cqw (:477), .p7-dhead .88→.90cqw (:472), .p7-decision 10px→.86cqw (:483), .p7-b3 .p7-bub .97→1.05cqw (:491) after checking the nowrap third line clears .p7-c3; verify 'Urgência identificada' fits the 17.3% card or set .p7-clabel{white-space:normal}; optional .p7 .ct padding 68→40px for +56px board. (3) Contrast, colour only: `.lc-who,.lc-htime,.p7-blbl,.p7-cmuted,.p7-ts,.p7-time,.p7-foot{color:var(--text-secondary)}` (5.7–6.3:1) and `.lc-row--cust .lc-who{color:var(--lg-teal-700)}` mirroring the desktop mint rule at :490; .lc-who 12px; .p7-blbl font-size:max(11px,.85cqw). --text-muted stays for hairlines and the marquee.

Findings: L01, L02 · skill: `/adapt`


### [P1] Colour is used off-contract in three places that carry meaning: step '02 Suas regras' sits on the #40595a band of a percentage gradient at 1.05–1.2:1 on every phone and tablet (the middle of the frozen three-step chain vanishes); '03 Em operação' lands on the desktop 96° ramp at 3.1–3.4:1 at every 3-column width; the demo's approve buttons are white on #ff5a36 at 3.10:1 while every real CTA uses the certified ink-on-orange pair; and orange is spent on the 92px 'Em português.' kicker and the 'não fica esperando.' fragment against Principle 1 ('Headline interna não gasta acento').

**Why:** 'Suas respostas viram regras de operação' is the one sentence that turns the product from answering machine into hired agent; outdoors on a phone it is gone, and the failure moves with copy wrap because the stops are percentages of a content-sized section. #40595a/#d9decf/#dfe3d6 exist in neither colors.css nor the brand guide. When a non-clickable 92px phrase wears action-orange, the eye stops reading orange as 'press here'.

**Fix:** L03 (≤1024, index.html:397): replace the 180deg gradient with `.iv{background:var(--surface-page);padding-top:0}` and anchor the dark head to the DOM: `.iv-head{margin:0 calc(50% - 50vw);padding:clamp(64px,8vw,104px) max(20px,calc(50vw - 50%)) clamp(28px,3vw,48px);background:linear-gradient(180deg,#041a2b,#07222f)}`; mirror the col-2/3 rules for step 01 now on paper: `.iv-col--1 .iv-lab{color:var(--lg-teal-700)} .iv-col--1 .iv-line{color:var(--lg-ink-950)}`; replace raw #0b655a at :346/:403 with var(--lg-teal-700). Verify .iv-col--1/2 .iv-lab ≥4.5:1 at 360/390/430/768/1024 after the Reveal settles (≥900ms). L18 (desktop): index.html:346 `.iv-col--3 .iv-lab{color:var(--lg-ink-950)}` (7.3–7.9:1 from 1100 to 1440); do NOT retune the 96deg stops (they drop col-2's tail to 2.2:1) and do NOT add max-width:24ch (misaligns the connectors); longer term replace :335 with a hard seam dark | var(--surface-page). L07: edit in place :487 `.p7-apr{…color:var(--on-accent)}`, :547 `.lc-apr{…color:var(--on-accent)}`, :568 `.nojs-v9__button{…color:var(--on-accent)}` → 5.59:1, same pair as hero/nav/pricing/final. L09: :340 `.iv-word{color:var(--lg-paper)}` keeping its clamp (scale is the sanctioned hierarchy tool; legible on both gradients), :132 `.dorbig .acc{color:inherit}`; optional: wrap only the terminal '.' in <span class='acc'> as the brand-board idiom (#b83216 on paper). Leave .iv-hang, .lg-btn--accent, .p7-apr/.lc-apr fills alone.

Findings: L03, L18, L07, L09 · skill: `/colorize`


### [P1] Controls that lie: the price block's only orange primary 'Quero aproveitar a oferta' links to #preco, its own section (a ~300px scroll back to the heading on mobile); five mockup <button>s ('Aprovar encaixe', 'Ajustar resposta' ×2, 'Aprovar e ativar') have pointer/hover/active states and no handler; desktop 'Ver de novo' is a decorated <span>; Termos/Privacidade are href='#' and smooth-scroll the reader 9600px back to the hero; the mobile console carries no visible 'Exemplo' label and reads as a live call ('Ligou em chamada', static 00:12).

**Why:** Brand p.23: the primary CTA must say exactly what happens; a dead primary at the money moment fails that and the contract's 'checkout só domina no bloco de preço'. Principle 3: every mockup carries a visible example label, 'aria-label sozinho não resolve'; 'Aprovar encaixe' is the largest orange surface inside the proof and tapping it does nothing. The skeptic's legitimacy check (Terms/Privacy) dead-ends. Six extra tab stops with no outcome.

**Fix:** L10: ligou-app9.jsx:483 href='#preco' → href='#prova' (rebuild so assets/js/ligou-app9.js follows); keep label, variant='accent', marginTop 8; swap for the real checkout URL when it exists, never a self-anchor. L06: jsx:256, :347, :394 `<button className='iv-approve|p7-apr|p7-adj|lc-apr|lc-adj' type='button'>` → `<span className='…'>` (no aria-hidden, text stays AT-readable); index.html:380 drop cursor:pointer+transition, delete :381-382 (:hover/:active); :486 and :546 drop cursor:pointer. L16/L06(c): jsx:298 `<button className='p7-again' type='button' onClick={() => { setMrel(false); setMrun(m => m + 1); }}>` keeping visible 'Ver de novo'; move key={mrun} to .p7-cq (jsx:290); :437 add cursor:pointer;font:inherit and a :focus-visible ring like .lc-rel (:542). L24: jsx:525-526 render 'Termos' and 'Privacidade' as <span style={a}> (same inline style object, visually identical) until pages exist; drop the mailto alternative. L15: in .lc-idsub (jsx:354) add `<i className='lc-en lc-ex'>Chamada · Exemplo</i>` (verbatim desktop string, fits 138.9–160px columns at 360–390); in the ≤767 block `.lc-idsub{flex-wrap:wrap;row-gap:4px}` and move `.lc-wf{display:none}` from the ≤359 rule (:554) into ≤767; add aria-label='Resumo em português' to both asides (jsx:336, :384).

Findings: L10, L06, L24, L15 · skill: `/harden`


### [P2] The demo never plays for the reader: the mobile choreography (.lc-i, --lcd 0–2.85s) runs at mount, 2.5 viewports off-screen, and reports playState 'finished' before anyone taps the hero CTA; the desktop board has zero animations, all three timestamps at once, and a fake replay control; the CTA lands on 519px of heading (a 216px paper seam and a hard <br/> forcing a 4-line title) with the first Ligou reply cut by the fold.

**Why:** Brand motion rule: 'Movimento que explica'. Every CTA that promises a conversation (nav, hero, final) delivers a finished transcript, and the causal unfolding cliente → regra → aviso → resumo em português is the one thing motion was supposed to carry. The handoff flags the 0.45s/step cadence as an open decision.

**Fix:** L04: in CallDemo add `const [shellRef, shellOn] = useInView({threshold:.3})` (window.useInView from ligou-fx2.js:212); `ref={shellRef}` + `className={'lc-shell'+(shellOn?' on':'')}` on jsx:351; index.html:506 → `.lc-shell.on .lc-i{animation:lcin .2s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1) var(--lcd,0s) both}` + `.lc-shell:not(.on) .lc-i{opacity:0}`, and inside the reduced-motion block `.lc-shell:not(.on) .lc-i{opacity:1}`. Replay from CTAs: hashchange does not fire when already at #prova, so give the three href='#prova' Buttons (jsx:78, 186, 507) an onClick dispatching `new CustomEvent('ligou:replay')` and in CallDemo listen → setMrel(false); setMrun(m=>m+1). Cadence: present RJ the option --lcd 0/.9/1.7/2.6/3.4/4.3/5.1/6.0s + animation-duration .35s (~6s cycle) vs current 2.85s — do not change silently. Surface 'Ver de novo' as visible text inside .lc-again (pill: height 44px;padding 0 12px;border-radius var(--radius-pill);font 700 12.5px var(--font-display)). L16: desktop board gets the same staging — useInView on .p7-board, class p7-i with inline --lcd 0/.45/.9/1.35/1.8/2.25/2.7s on .p7-b1/.p7-c1/.p7-b2/.p7-c2/.p7-b3/.p7-c3/.p7-drawer, `.p7-board.on .p7-i{animation:lcin .2s … var(--lcd) both}`, draw only the solid teal trail path (pathLength='1', stroke-dashoffset transition 1.6s) and keep it teal; append `.p7-i{opacity:1;animation:none}` and `.p7-trail path{stroke-dashoffset:0}` to index.html:230-236. L05 (≤767 only): jsx:287 `decisão,<br/>ele` → `decisão, <br/>ele` then `.p7-title br{display:none}` (3-line wrap, string unchanged); `.p7{margin-top:0!important;padding-top:24px}` (seam ~110px like the others); `.p7-bridge{padding:10px 14px}`; `.p7m{margin-top:16px}`; `#prova{scroll-margin-top:69px}`; reorder via `.p7 .ct{display:flex;flex-direction:column}` `.p7m{order:1}` `.p7-langnote{order:2;margin-top:14px}` so console head, customer bubble, urgency, Ligou reply and 'Regra consultada' all sit on the arrival screen.

Findings: L04, L16, L05 · skill: `/animate`


### [P2] No type system: 7 ad-hoc h2 sizes (Dor/Prova/Diferença outsize the Preço h2 by 4–8px on desktop, inverting 'Hero e preço recebem escala editorial; o restante é silencioso'), 4 lede sizes, 11 running-text sizes between 12 and 17px, 14 uppercase-label specs across two typefaces (.iv-lab in Familjen breaks 'caixa-alta em Archivo com tracking'), hero eyebrow floored at 10px under 1282px, and the sticky primary 'Falar com o Ligou' at 11.5–12px on phones — the smallest button text on the page.

**Why:** In daylight the reader relies on size steps to know heading / lede / fact / footnote; half-pixel differences cost nothing in hierarchy and everything in rhythm, every future edit becomes a new clamp, and the one action a prospect can take from any scroll position is set at 72% of body size though there is room (133px pill fits at 320).

**Fix:** L13: :root tokens `--t-h2-quiet:clamp(30px,calc(20px + 1.7vw),44px); --t-h2-price:clamp(34px,calc(24px + 2vw),52px); --t-h3:clamp(21px,calc(16px + .6vw),24px); --t-lede:clamp(17px,1.35vw,19px); --t-body:16px; --t-small:15px; --t-caption:13px`. .dorbig (:131), .p7-title (:423), .iv h2 (:337) and jsx:12 SectionHead → var(--t-h2-quiet); jsx:471 price h2 → var(--t-h2-price); delete :413; jsx:408/:491 → var(--t-h3) only then drop :191's --size-display (keep --size-h3 otherwise). Ledes (.h4-lede :272, .p7-sub :424, .iv-sub :339, .iv-q, jsx:13, jsx:407) → var(--t-lede); .p7-bridge weight 600. Small tier 15px: .startcard p, .incitem, .iv-row p, .price-mobile-normal, .p7-langnote, footer links, jsx:492, .p7-foot, .iv-own b, .iv-approve, .lc-rel, .lc-apr/.lc-adj. Caption 13px: .lc-ev em, .iv-pill, .lc-htime, .iv-own i; keep .h4-trust 13, .lc-ts inheriting 11px, hero h1 and .cta h2 untouched. L14: `:root{--t-label:var(--size-caption);--t-label-track:var(--track-caps);--t-label-weight:var(--weight-bold)}` (12px/.16em/700 — DS values, not a new .14em) and one shared rule for .h4-eyebrow,.pt-cap__label,.iv-lab,.iv-railitem,.iv-memory__label,.iv-memory__approval,.lc-who,.lc-ev b,.lc-dhead,.p7-decision,.intro__cap {font-family:var(--font-body);font-size/weight/letter-spacing from tokens;text-transform:uppercase}; `.iv-lab b{font-family:var(--font-display)}` for the numeral; jsx:466 and :501 → same tokens; .h4-eyebrow :266 drop the clamp (verify hero CTA stays ~y392 at 390); .lc-en :513 10→11px; leave cqw board labels to the L01 fix; delete dead .trustline/.scrollhint/.callchip CSS fully (:66,:68-71,:115-117,:172,:203,:232) after grep. L20: index.html:205 drop font-size:12.5px, :207 drop font-size:12px!important, :332 drop font-size:11.5px!important (keep paddings) → .lg-btn--sm 13.5px everywhere, 44px min-height unchanged, no label swap moved.

Findings: L13, L14, L20 · skill: `/typeset`


## Single biggest opportunity

Make the proof fold do its job on arrival. Every CTA on the page — nav, hero, final, and (after L10) pricing — lands on #prova, so this one section carries conversion: gate the console sequence on visibility and replay it from every CTA (L04), give tablets the console instead of a 4px poster (L01), lift the captions from 2:1 to 6:1 (L02), tighten the arrival composition so the customer bubble and 'Regra consultada' sit on the first screen (L05), and stage the desktop board with the same cadence (L16). No copy changes, no frozen decision touched; the demo becomes something the reader watches happen instead of a screenshot he scrolls past.


## Minor observations

- L11 — English demo bubbles (.lc-bub ×3 on mobile, .p7-bub ×3 on desktop) and the FAQ 'Hi, you've reached…' quote have no lang='en-US'; attribute-only fix, FAQ entry becomes a JSX fragment.
- L12 — Live <title> is 'Ligou 2026 v9 — Responsive Hero Video', no og:/twitter: tags, favicon and footer use the handset channel badge the brand guide says is not the corporate mark; regenerate the existing og-card.svg → og-ligou.png (1200x630, <300 KB, absolute URL) with AgentNodeMark; Agent Node vs A Linha is still DECISÃO PENDENTE — name it to RJ.
- L17 (P3) — Preço + CTA + Footer stack 2.4 viewports of continuous dark (#082f3d → #041d27 is the only seam); pricing surface is inline JSX style (jsx:468) so the fix is there, not CSS; a theme decision for RJ, not silent polish.
- L21 — Only #diferenca/#prova get scroll-margin-top and only ≤767; on desktop #faq/#comecar/#preco headings land under the 69px sticky nav; `#prova,#diferenca,#comecar,#faq,#preco{scroll-margin-top:93px}` at all widths.
- L22 — noscript keeps only hero + 4-step proof (Principle 4 promises the three scenes stacked) and a failed bundle yields a white page; extend <noscript> additively with the scene/pricing strings verbatim and add a capture-phase window 'error' handler that injects noscript.textContent into #root — do not rewrite the pinned 'Ver demonstração ilustrativa' string (tests/frontend-contract.test.mjs:15).
- L23 — .nav-links hidden below 920px on a 9911px page; a wrapped chip row under the nav (nav4 paper-on-dark pill language, not .lg-tabs) restores the four existing labels at ~104px header height; RJ must accept the taller sticky bar.
- L19 — checked and fine: nav pill and hero CTA are 384px apart, .prog is 0px wide at scrollY 0, and Principle 5 explicitly sanctions the header repeating the voice action; at most P3 taste.

## Personas — red flags


**Casey — distracted, one-handed mobile (390x844)**

- 2.45s navy splash on arrival and again on every back-navigation; tapping skips it but nothing says so; primary CTA fully readable only at ~3.7s (L08)
- 'Falar com o Ligou' drops her on 519px of heading with a 216px blank seam; the console is already finished, one static bubble, first Ligou reply cut by the fold (L04, L05)
- She taps the orange 'Aprovar encaixe' — the biggest button since the hero — and nothing happens (L06)
- Pricing 'Quero aproveitar a oferta' scrolls her ~300px back up to the heading; she reads it as a misfire (L10)
- No section links below 920px; price is 9.4 viewports of thumb away (L23)
- Sticky 'Falar com o Ligou' is 11.5px in a 113px pill — smaller than 'Área' beside it (L20)
- Footer 'Termos' throws her back to the hero from y 9600 (L24)

**Jordan — first-timer, never used an AI phone agent**

- Mobile console header reads 'Ligou em chamada · EN · 00:12' with no visible 'Exemplo' chip — live? recorded? his? (L15)
- '02 Suas regras — Suas respostas viram regras de operação', the sentence that explains the model, is invisible on his phone (1.05:1 on the gradient band) (L03)
- The sheet's 'Aprovar encaixe / Ajustar resposta' addresses him as the owner and tapping does nothing → 'the demo is broken' (L06)
- #diferenca 'Aprovar e ativar' (full width on mobile) is another approve that does nothing (L06)
- Every CTA that promises a conversation delivers a finished transcript; the causal unfolding he needs to see never plays in view (L04)
- Desktop: 'Ver de novo' pill is a <span>; clicking does nothing, and the board has no motion to replay (L16)
- Speaker/language labels at 2:1 — he infers who is talking from bubble alignment alone (L02)

**Rafael — Brazilian roofer in the sun, one missed call on his mind, skeptical of tech promises**

- First 912px are #04182a after 2.45s of dark splash — the hardest surface to read under direct sun, before any light section (L08)
- In #prova the .lc-who labels are 11px #9db3b8 on #f7f8f1 (2.05:1) and 'Decisão de exceção' is 10px; in glare he cannot tell Cliente from Ligou by text (L02)
- 'Aprovar encaixe' is white on #ff5a36 at 3.10:1 — the weakest contrast on the one control that says 'you stay in control' (L07)
- Step 02 of the three-step chain vanishes on his phone; on his laptop '03 Em operação' sits at 3.1:1 (L03, L18)
- Termos / Privacidade are '#' — his legitimacy check dead-ends and scrolls him to the top (L24)
- Preço → CTA → Footer is 2.4 viewports of continuous dark; the closing 'Ligou? Atendido.' has no pause to land on (L17)
- The WhatsApp link he receives previews 'Ligou 2026 v9 — Responsive Hero Video' with no image and the handset badge (L12)

## Verified findings (detail)


### L01 · P1 · ADJUSTED — Proof board type collapses to 4.3–10px across 768–1440: cqw sizes have no floor and the mobile console is hidden until 767px

**Where:** index.html:427-429 (.p7-cq container-type / .p7-board), :432 .p7-blab, :448 .p7-clabel .77cqw, :452 .p7-crule .92cqw, :454 .p7-cmuted .77cqw, :458 .p7-stpill, :466 .p7-bub 1.25cqw, :477 .p7-dlab .82cqw, :480 .p7-dval, :486 .p7-apr 1.08cqw, :489 .p7-blbl .72cqw, :491 .p7-b3 .p7-bub .97cqw; switch to .p7m only at :495-504 @media (max-width:767px)

**Viewports:** 768x1024, 1024x768, 1280x800, 1440x900

**Evidence:** Computed font sizes. 768x1024: board 597px (1cqw=5.97px) → .p7-blbl 4.30px, .p7-clabel/.p7-cmuted 4.60px, .p7-dlab 4.90px, .p7-dhead 5.25px, .p7-crule 5.49px, .p7-b3 .p7-bub 5.79px, .p7-again 5.91px, .p7-apr 'Aprovar encaixe' 6.45px, .p7-dval 6.87px, .p7-bub 7.46px; .p7m display:none. 1024x768: board 853px → .p7-blbl 6.14px, .p7-clabel 6.57px, .p7-dlab 6.99px, .p7-b3 bubble 8.27px, .p7-apr 9.21px, .p7-bub 10.66px. 1280x800: bubble 13.86px, .p7-clabel 8.54px, .p7-blbl 7.98px, .p7-dlab 9.09px. 1440x900: board 1269px → .p7-blbl 9.14px, .p7-clabel/.p7-cmuted 9.77px, .p7-dlab 10.4px, .p7-b3 .p7-bub 12.31px vs 15.86px siblings, .p7-apr 13.7px; fixed-px .p7-decision (10px) becomes the LARGEST label in the drawer at 768 (siblings 5.25px) — inverted hierarchy. Screenshots at 768 and 1024 show the board as grey texture.

**Why:** The demo is the primary proof and the primary action (Principle 5). .impeccable.md promises an own composition for 768–1199; instead an iPad or split-view laptop gets a 4–7px EN→PT conversation, an unreadable rule card and a 6px 'Aprovar encaixe'. Three lenses measured this independently (typography, layout, contract-fidelity); all rate it the top defect.

**Fix:** Copy untouched; light proof fold, EN/ES->PT visual and orange-only-on-Aprovar all preserved. (1) Tablet/mid band = the console, aligned with the existing 768-1199 'mid' band and the .impeccable.md promise: create @media (max-width:1199px){...} placed before the current 767 block and MOVE into it index.html:501 (.p7-cq{display:none}), :504 (.p7m{display:block;margin-top:28px}) and the whole .lc-* block :505-550 (lc-shell, lc-i + @keyframes lcin, lc-head ... lc-adj, the .p7-foot override); leave :497-499 (#diferenca/#prova scroll-margin, .mq-claims/.iv-rail/.price-* hides, .price-mobile-normal), :500 (.p7 .ct 20px padding) and :502-503 (.iv-avatar) in the <=767 block. Add @media (min-width:768px) and (max-width:1199px){.lc-shell{max-width:480px}} next to :551. Result: 768-1199 shows the console with fixed 16px bubbles / 11px labels / 48px buttons, centered on the light fold. (2) >=1200 (board >=1029px, 1cqw>=10.29px): do NOT add px floors or min card widths (they desync the %-positioned composition). Raise the smallest multipliers so proportional scaling stays intact: .p7-blbl .72->.82cqw (:489); .p7-clabel/.p7-cmuted/.p7-stpill .77->.80cqw (:448,:454,:458); .p7-dlab .82->.86cqw (:477); .p7-dhead .88->.90cqw (:472); .p7-decision 10px->.86cqw (:483, fixes the inverted hierarchy at every width); .p7-b3 .p7-bub .97->1.05cqw (:491) only after checking the nowrap third line clears .p7-c3 at 54.9%. Yields labels 8.2-8.9px at 1200, 9.6-10.3px at 1366, 10.4-11.1px at 1440. Verify .p7-clabel 'Urgência identificada' (nowrap) still fits the 17.3% card at .80cqw (current slack ~5%); if it wraps/overflows, set .p7-clabel{white-space:normal}. Optional extra headroom: .p7 .ct padding 68->40px (:428) gives the board +56px at every desktop width (+0.55px per cqw).

**Verifier:** Evidence is REAL and reproduces exactly. index.html:427-491 sizes every board label/bubble/button in cqw with no floor, .p7-cq is a container-type:inline-size, and .p7m/.p7-cq swap only at @media (max-width:767px) (index.html:496,501,504). Board width = viewport - 15px scrollbar - 136px (.p7 .ct padding 68+68, :428) - 20px (.p7-cq calc(100% - 20px), :427): 768->597, 1024->853, 1280->1109, 1440->1269 (1269 also matches tmp/qa-notes/landing-geometry.json p7board.w). Recomputed: .p7-blbl .72cqw = 4.30/6.14/7.98/9.14px; .p7-clabel .77cqw = 4.60/6.57/8.54/9.77px (json: 9.77); .p7-dlab .82cqw = 4.90/.../10.4px (json: 10.4); .p7-apr 1.08cqw = 6.45/9.21/.../13.7px (json: 13.7); .p7-bub 1.25cqw = 7.46/10.66/13.86/15.86px (json: 15.86); .p7-decision fixed 10px (:483) is indeed larger than every cqw drawer label at 768 (dhead 5.25px). .impeccable.md line 15 does promise 'composição intermediária própria entre 768 e 1199 px', and useHeroBand (ligou-app9.jsx:150) already has a 'mid' band for exactly that range while CallDemo has none. So the defect and the contract breach stand. Severity is inflated: the brief's primary user (phone, <=767) gets the fixed-px console at 16px bubbles / 11px labels, and at the reference desktop 1440 the conversation (15.9px), drawer values (14.6px) and 'Aprovar encaixe' (13.7px) are readable; only the micro-labels (9.1-10.4px) dip below an 11px floor. The board is truly unreadable (4-9px) only at 768-~1100 (tablets, split-view, unmaximized windows) - a minority segment for this audience. That is a major defect plus a contract breach, not a conversion blocker for the primary path -> P1 (P0-grade inside 768-1099 only). The proposed fix is also flawed in two places: (1) switching just the .p7-cq/.p7m lines to 1099 leaves the console unstyled, because every .lc-* rule (index.html:505-550, incl. lc-i keyframes) sits inside the same <=767 block; (2) px floors and width:max(17.3%,220px) break a poster whose positions are all percentages: at a 929px board (1100 vp) .p7-c3 (left 54.9%) + 220px ends at 730px while the z-index:3 drawer starts at 74.3% = 690px, hiding ~40px of the card ('Aguardando decisão'); still ~5px overlap at 1280. The drawer (board height ~451px at 1100) also cannot hold 12-15px floored content in 5 rows + relato + buttons without overflow; the finding only checked bubble 1 vs the drawer.


### L02 · P1 · ADJUSTED — Speaker/language labels and timestamps in both demo consoles use --text-muted on paper: 2.0–2.2:1 at 9–12.5px

**Where:** index.html:525 .lc-who 11px, :526 .lc-ts, :515 .lc-htime 12.5px, :532 .lc-ev b (mobile console); :489 .p7-blbl .72cqw, :454/:457 .p7-cmuted, :468 .p7-ts, :436 .p7-time, :492 .p7-foot (desktop board); colors.css:9 --text-muted = --lg-ink-300 #9db3b8

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** Computed: .lc-who rgb(157,179,184) on .lc-shell rgb(247,248,241) → 2.05:1 at 11px/800 uppercase +.12em ('CLIENTE · INGLÊS 00:02', 'LIGOU · INGLÊS 00:06/00:10'); .lc-htime '00:12' on #fdfdf8 → 2.15:1. Desktop 1269px board: .p7-blbl 'Ligou · Inglês' 9.14px on #f5f6ee → 2.02:1; .p7-cmuted 'Local'/'Fonte'/'Status' 9.77px on white → 2.19:1; .p7-ts/.p7-time 12.2px → 2.15:1. Only the desktop customer bubble is right (.p7-bub--mint .p7-blbl #0b655a = 6.05:1). Screenshot at 390 scrollY 1990–2004: labels are faint ghosts above 16px ink bubbles.

**Why:** These labels are the caption of the frozen EN/ES→PT visual — who is speaking, in which language. WCAG 1.4.3 needs 4.5:1; at 2:1 in daylight the console reads as an ordinary chat and the 'Resumo em português' drawer has nothing to contrast against. Five lenses flagged it independently.

**Fix:** index.html <style>, color only, no copy changes:
.lc-who,.lc-htime,.p7-blbl,.p7-cmuted,.p7-ts,.p7-time,.p7-foot{color:var(--text-secondary)} /* #48656e: 5.85:1 on #f7f8f1, 6.12:1 on #fdfdf8, 5.74:1 on #f5f6ee, 6.25:1 on #fff, 6.06:1 on paper */
.lc-row--cust .lc-who{color:var(--lg-teal-700)} /* mirrors index.html:490 desktop mint rule; #0b655a = 6.50:1 on #f7f8f1 */
Size (safe, auto-width nowrap bubbles): .lc-who{font-size:12px} and .p7-blbl{font-size:max(11px,.85cqw)} (.85cqw = 10.8px at 1269 -> floors to 11px).
Drop from the original fix: .lc-ev b (already --text-secondary at :532), .lc-ts (inherits from .lc-who, redundant), and the .p7-clabel/.p7-cmuted 11px floors (nowrap 'Urgência identificada' in a ~219px card risks clipping at 1440; handle in L01 with white-space:normal or reduced tracking). .p7-foot mobile is already --text-secondary at :549; only the desktop rule at :492 changes. --text-muted stays for .p7-dot/.lc-evline hairlines, .tdots, .rulechips .arr and the marquee.

**Verifier:** Evidence checks out against source. colors.css:9 sets --text-muted to --lg-ink-300 = #9db3b8. index.html:525 .lc-who (11px/800 uppercase, .12em) and :515 .lc-htime (12.5px) use it on .lc-shell #f7f8f1 / .lc-head #fdfdf8; :526 .lc-ts has no color and inherits it. Desktop :489 .p7-blbl .72cqw, :454 .p7-cmuted .77cqw, :468 .p7-ts and :436 .p7-time .96cqw, :492 .p7-foot 14px all use --text-muted. Recomputed WCAG ratios: #9db3b8 on #f7f8f1 2.05, on #fdfdf8 2.15, on #f5f6ee 2.02, on #fff 2.19, on paper #fbfcf8 2.13. At the measured 1269px board (landing-geometry.json p7board.w) .72cqw = 9.14px, .77cqw = 9.77px, .96cqw = 12.18px, matching the finding. Only .p7-bub--mint .p7-blbl (:490, #0b655a, 5.91:1 on #d9f2ec) passes; the mobile console has no such rule. So roughly a dozen text nodes in the primary proof section fail AA by more than 2x, on the surface the brand guide itself says must stay legible on a phone in daylight. Two errors in the finding: (1) .lc-ev b (index.html:532) is already color:var(--text-secondary), not muted, so it does not belong in the list or the fix; (2) .p7-foot on mobile is already overridden to --text-secondary at :549, so only the desktop rule at :492 changes. The 'why' is somewhat overstated: the EN->PT story is also carried by the English bubble text itself, the .lc-en/.p7-en 'EN' pill (ink-950 on paper), the teal .p7-langnote, and the JM/Ligou avatars, so conversion is not blocked and the visual survives; but who-is-speaking and the card field names (Local/Fonte/Status) are effectively invisible in daylight. P1 holds at its low end (a11y failure on the proof fold, one-line token swap); it would be P2 if only timestamps were affected. The fix as written also carries an implementation risk: flooring .p7-clabel/.p7-cmuted to 11px is inherited from L01 and .p7-clabel is white-space:nowrap inside a 17.3% (~219px) card with ~33px horizontal padding; 'URGÊNCIA IDENTIFICADA' at 11px/800/.12em is ~190px plus dot+gap and is likely to clip at 1440. That size change should stay in L01 with a wrap/tracking decision, not ride along here.


### L03 · P1 · ADJUSTED — Step '02 Suas regras' of the frozen second fold is invisible on mobile/tablet (1.05–1.2:1): a percentage gradient with an off-palette #40595a band lands under the label

**Where:** index.html:397 (.iv ≤1024 background:linear-gradient(180deg,#041a2b 0%,#07222f 26%,#40595a 36%,#dfe3d6 46%,#f6f7f0 56%)), :403 (.iv-col--2 .iv-lab{color:#0b655a}), :404 (.iv-col--2 .iv-line{color:ink-950}), :344 .iv-lab; jsx:230-231

**Viewports:** 390x844, 768x1024, 1024x768

**Evidence:** 390: section 2232px tall; .iv-col--2 .iv-lab at y 795–816 = 35.6–36.6% of section — exactly the #40595a 36% stop. Sampled bg rgb(62–73,87–97,89–97) vs fg rgb(11,101,90) → 1.05–1.11:1 at 13px caps. .iv-col--2 .iv-line 'Suas respostas viram regras de operação.' at 37–38.1% on rgb(80–98,103–119) → 2.87–3.65:1 at 16.5px/600. Same landing at 768 (label 729/2076 = 35%) and 1024 (782/2155 = 36%, label 1.08:1, line 3.71:1) because stops are percentages of a content-sized section. '01 CONVERSA' (7.7:1) and '03 EM OPERAÇÃO' (6.4:1) read fine. Screenshot at 390 scrollY 3760–3786 shows '02 SUAS REGRAS' as a ghost row. #40595a/#d9decf/#dfe3d6 exist in neither colors.css nor the brand guide.

**Why:** This is the hinge of the frozen 'Conversa → Suas regras → Em operação' chain and the one sentence that explains the product model. Outdoors on a phone the middle step disappears and fails AA for everyone; the failure moves with copy wrap. The smeared navy→grey-green→paper ramp is also the generic 'mesh gradient' tell (brand p.16: no colour exists only to decorate).

**Fix:** Stop placing text on the ramp; make the ≤1024 background a DOM-anchored two-tone instead of a percentage gradient. In index.html inside @media (max-width:1024px): replace `.iv{background:linear-gradient(180deg,#041a2b 0%,#07222f 26%,#40595a 36%,#dfe3d6 46%,#f6f7f0 56%)}` with `.iv{background:var(--surface-page);padding-top:0}` and add `.iv-head{max-width:none;margin:0 calc(50% - 50vw);padding:clamp(64px,8vw,104px) max(20px,calc(50vw - 50%)) clamp(28px,3vw,48px);background:linear-gradient(180deg,#041a2b,#07222f)}` (the section already has overflow:hidden; use the Container's own horizontal padding variable if one exists instead of the max()). Step 01 now sits on paper, so mirror the col-2/col-3 rules: `.iv-col--1 .iv-lab{color:var(--lg-teal-700)} .iv-col--1 .iv-line{color:var(--lg-ink-950)}` (teal-700 = #0b655a, 6.44:1 on #f6f7f0; the dark card #0d2536 already sits on paper like .iv-node does). While there, replace the raw #0b655a in :346/:403 with var(--lg-teal-700) so the section uses tokens only. Drop the pixel-anchored gradient alternative (fails at 768 where the label rests at ~728px). Long-term (desktop too): replace the 96deg gradient at :335 with a hard seam dark | var(--surface-page) and delete #40595a/#d9decf/#dfe3d6. Verify after change: at 360/390/430/768/1024, .iv-col--2 .iv-lab and .iv-col--1 .iv-lab computed contrast ≥4.5:1 against the surface directly behind them, measured after the Reveal transform has settled (scroll the column into view, wait ≥900ms, then getBoundingClientRect).

**Verifier:** Defect is real and reproduced on the worktree source (served statically from /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI, prebuilt assets/js/ligou-app9.js). Cited selectors/lines are exact: index.html:397 (.iv ≤1024 gradient with #40595a at 36%), :403/:404 (.iv-col--2 label #0b655a, line ink-950), :344, jsx:230-231. Computed style confirms the 180deg gradient is applied. Measured: 390x844 section 2232px, .iv-col--2 .iv-lab 795-816px = 35.6-36.5%, interpolated bg rgb(62-72,87-96,88-97) vs rgb(11,101,90) -> 1.03-1.12:1 at 13px/800; .iv-line 826-851px on rgb(80-98,103-118) vs ink-950 -> 2.87-3.61:1 at 16.5px/600 (below the 18.66px/700 large-text threshold, needs 4.5:1). 768x1024: label 1.00-1.22:1, line 3.38-4.33:1. 1024x768: label 1.26-1.53:1, line 4.22-5.26:1. Cols 1 and 3 pass (7.65:1 / 6.44:1 label). #40595a/#d9decf/#dfe3d6 exist only in index.html, not in _ds tokens/colors.css; brand p.16 'Nenhuma cor deve existir apenas para decorar' is verbatim in tmp/brand-guide-summary.md. On mobile .iv-rail is display:none (≤767px, index.html:498), so there is no fallback text for step 02. Two corrections: (1) the pixel figures are pre-Reveal — .iv-col--2 sits at translateY(26px)/opacity 0 until it enters view, so the resting label is ~769-790px (34.5-35.4%), on an even darker part of the ramp (label ≈1.15-1.23:1, line ≈2.3-2.9:1 — worse, not better); conclusion unchanged. (2) The 'alternative' pixel-anchored fix is unsafe as written: at 768 the label rests at ~728px, inside the proposed 720-780px #dfe3d6->#f6f7f0 ramp, and any width where the head wraps less pushes it into the #40595a band; it should be dropped in favour of the structural fix. The 'why' holds for the target user (phone, daylight, frozen three-step chain loses its middle label and the one sentence that states the model). Could not screenshot: the Browser pane does not composite in this non-interactive session, so the IntersectionObserver reveal never fired; numeric evidence is sufficient.


### L04 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Mobile demo choreography plays at page load, 2.5 viewports off-screen, and is finished before anyone reaches it — the proof always arrives static

**Where:** index.html:506-507 (.lc-i{animation:lcin .2s … var(--lcd) both}); src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:352-384 (--lcd 0/.1/.55/1/1.45/1.9/2.35/2.85s), :356 (.lc-again icon-only); docs/HANDOFF-NEXT-SESSION.md:97 (target 0,8–1,1 s/passo, ciclo 6–8 s)

**Viewports:** 390x844

**Evidence:** At scrollY 0 document.getAnimations() lists all 8 lcin CSSAnimations while .lc-shell sits at y 2093. After tapping the hero CTA (scrollY 1574, ~4s after load) all 8 report playState 'finished' (lc-sheet currentTime 3050). Replay frames: +1.0s customer bubble, 'Urgência identificada' AND the Ligou reply already in; +2.1s everything through 'Responsável avisado'; sheet at +3.05s — a 12s call (stamps 00:02/00:06/00:10) compressed to 3s at 0.45s/step; a 100-char English bubble gets 450ms. During replay the rule event (+424px) and PT summary (+666px) animate below the fold. Final CTA → #prova is a 7500px smooth fly-back to the same finished picture.

**Why:** The demo is the only proof and the primary conversion. Every CTA that promises a conversation (nav, hero, final) delivers a finished transcript; the causal chain cliente → regra → aviso → resumo em português is never observed. The handoff explicitly flags 0.45s/step as open.

**Fix:** Keep the gating part, split the cadence part out as RJ's decision, and fix two implementability details. (1) Gate on visibility, not mount: in CallDemo add `const [shellRef, shellOn] = useInView({threshold: .3});` (useInView is exported on window by assets/js/ligou-fx2.js:212), put `ref={shellRef}` and `className={'lc-shell' + (shellOn ? ' on' : '')}` on the .lc-shell div (jsx:351), and change index.html:506 to `.lc-shell.on .lc-i{animation:lcin .2s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1) var(--lcd,0s) both}` plus `.lc-shell:not(.on) .lc-i{opacity:0}` so rows stay hidden until the shell is 30% in view (keep the existing `@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.lc-i{animation:none}}` at :559 and add `.lc-shell:not(.on) .lc-i{opacity:1}` inside it; useInView already returns true under LGFX_REDUCED). No copy changes. (2) Replay from CTAs: `hashchange` does NOT fire when the hash is already #prova (hero tap then nav tap), so instead give the three `href="#prova"` Buttons (jsx:78, 186, 507) an onClick that dispatches `window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ligou:replay'))`, and in CallDemo listen for it to `setMrel(false); setMrun(m => m + 1)`; the existing `key={mrun}` on .p7m (jsx:350) already remounts the sequence. (3) Cadence: do NOT change --lcd silently; the handoff explicitly requires a new visual decision. Present RJ with the option `--lcd 0/.9/1.7/2.6/3.4/4.3/5.1/6.0s` + `animation-duration:.35s` (~6s cycle) alongside the current 2.85s, and only then apply. (4) 'Ver de novo' label: render the existing aria-label string as visible text inside .lc-again (`<span>Ver de novo</span>`) and change `.lc-again` (index.html:516) from a 44px circle to a pill `height:44px;padding:0 12px;border-radius:var(--radius-pill);gap:6px;font:700 12.5px var(--font-display)`; on the 360-399px branch (:553-557) keep the label but hide `.lc-wf` as already done. (5) Optional and only after RJ approves the cadence: mount `.tdots` (CSS exists unused at index.html:118-124) as an extra .lc-i row ~500ms before each Ligou bubble; no strings involved. Separate finding to log: the desktop `.p7-again` (jsx:298) is a non-interactive span, so 'Ver de novo' on desktop does nothing.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and reproduced: index.html:506 `.lc-i{animation:lcin .2s … var(--lcd,0s) both}` inside the max-width:767px block, with inline --lcd 0/.1/.55/1/1.45/1.9/2.35/2.85s (ligou-app9.jsx:352-384); CallDemo is mounted at load (App renders it outside the `ready` gate, jsx:536-561) and nothing ties .lc-i to `.play` or to useInView. Served the worktree at 390x844: at scrollY 0 the .lc-shell top is 2093px (2.48 viewports down, #prova at 1660), and 4.5s after load all 8 lcin animations report playState 'finished' with the exact end times 200/300/750/1200/1650/2100/2550/3050ms. `.lc-again` has textContent '' and only aria-label 'Ver de novo' (jsx:356). Customer bubble is 98 chars with a 450ms gap before the next event. The handoff (HANDOFF-NEXT-SESSION.md:93-96) confirms 0.45s/step vs the 0.8-1.1s / 6-8s target as an open item that 'requer nova decisão visual'. Final CTA sits at y 9488 -> #prova at 1574 (~7900px smooth scroll). Overstatements: (1) 'the causal chain is never observed' is false; the full chain (cliente -> Urgência -> regra -> Responsável avisado -> Resumo em português) is fully readable as a static, labelled example, which is what the desktop board (.p7-*) does by design (no p7 keyframes at all). What is lost is only the temporal unfolding. (2) Replay offsets measured at +431 (rule event) and +674 (sheet) from the shell top, not +424/+666; with the shell head at viewport top the sheet IS inside the 844 fold; it falls below the fold only when the head sits lower than ~170px (which is the realistic tap position, so the sheet claim mostly holds; the rule event is below the fold only when the head is below ~413px). (3) 'Every CTA … delivers a finished transcript' is true but the mismatch is inherent to the illustrative demo (DEMO_PHONE placeholder) and the static desktop design; not attributable to this bug alone. Severity: the user perceives nothing broken; content, hierarchy and conversion path are intact; the defect is a designed motion feature that never executes in view plus a replay control that is meaningless to someone who never saw the first run. That is a real but non-blocking defect: P2, not P1.


### L05 · P2 · ADJUSTED — 'Falar com o Ligou' lands on 519px of heading; .p7 top padding stacks a 216px seam and a hard <br/> orphans the proof title into 4 lines

**Where:** index.html:497 (#prova scroll-margin-top 86px), :192 (section{margin-top:64px!important}), :195 (.dor-ct padding-bottom 60px), :422 (.p7 padding 54px 0 62px), :423 (.p7-title clamp 34px), :425-426 (.p7-bridge/.p7-langnote), :504 (.p7m margin-top 28px); ligou-app9.jsx:286-287 (empty eyebrow-mark + 14px; 'Quando a regra exige decisão,<br/>ele traz a exceção pronta.')

**Viewports:** 390x844

**Evidence:** After tapping the hero CTA: scrollY 1574; viewport tops — empty eyebrow gap 69→178, title 178–321 (350x143, 4 lines: 'Quando a regra exige / decisão, / ele traz a exceção / pronta.' — the <br/> forces a break the 350px column already makes), bridge 339–439, langnote 451–491, console head 520–590, customer bubble 606–749, first Ligou reply starts 811 and is cut by the fold at 844; 'Regra consultada' 943, 'Resumo em português' 1185, decision row 1526. Scrolling: Dor last line 1536 → Dor bottom 1596 → #prova 1660 → .p7-title 1752 = 60+64+54+12+14 ≈ 216px of paper (26% of viewport) with only a 22x2 dash; other seams are 104–130px.

**Why:** The CTA promises a conversation and delivers a headline; combined with L04 the user sees one static bubble and must scroll two screens to the Portuguese summary — the moment that proves the product. The blank quarter-screen sits exactly between pain and proof.

**Fix:** Mobile only (inside the existing @media (max-width:767px) block at index.html:496+, which comes after :192 so it wins). 1) ligou-app9.jsx:287: change `decisão,<br/>ele` to `decisão, <br/>ele` (adds only a space, invisible while the br renders; string unchanged) then CSS `.p7-title br{display:none}` -> natural 3-line wrap ('Quando a regra exige / decisão, ele traz a / exceção pronta.'); optional `.p7-title{font-size:30px}` to match the Dor h2 (saves 12px more) — otherwise keep 34px for parity with --size-display. 2) `.p7{margin-top:0!important;padding-top:24px}` -> Dor->title seam ~110px (60+24+12+14), in line with the other 104-130px seams; Dor keeps its border-bottom as the divider, same pattern as .iv at :411. 3) `.p7-bridge{padding:10px 14px}` and `.p7m{margin-top:16px}`. 4) `#prova{scroll-margin-top:69px}` (nav height). 5) Move the language note under the console without touching markup: `.p7 .ct{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start}` `.p7m{order:1;align-self:stretch}` `.p7-langnote{order:2;margin-top:14px}`. Measured result on arrival at 390x844: console head ~340px, customer bubble ~448-569, urgency ~581-619, Ligou reply ~653-750, 'Regra consultada' ~762. Do NOT apply a br guard to .iv h2 (frozen second fold, separate finding if any). Desktop (>=768) untouched; .p7-cq board unchanged.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and reproduces from the CSS: at 390px .p7-title clamp(34px,3.7vw,57px) floors at 34px (3.7vw=14.4px), line-height 1.05 -> 4 lines x 35.7 = 143px with 'decisão,' and 'pronta.' each alone on a line (measured 350x143 at doc y 1752 in the served worktree). Seam Dor->title = .dor-ct padding-bottom 60 + section margin-top 64 (index.html:192) + .p7 padding-top 54 + eyebrow-mark 12 + title margin 14 = ~204-216px; Dor bottom 1596, #prova 1660, title 1752 all match the finding. Arrival geometry after href='#prova' with scroll-margin-top 86 also matches: bridge 100px, langnote 40px, .lc-head 70px at ~viewport 520-528, customer bubble ~637-757, Ligou reply starts ~841 (cut). All .lc-i animations report playState 'finished' at arrival (mount-time, not in-view), so the console is static when the user gets there. Two adjustments: (1) Severity: the customer bubble IS on the arrival screen and the cut reply is a natural scroll cue; the Portuguese summary is ~1.4 screens down, not two; nothing is hidden or broken, so this is a composition/density defect on the primary CTA's destination, P2 (borderline P1 as a bundle), not P1. (2) The fix as written is defective: `.p7-title br{display:none}` makes the h2 render 'decisão,ele' because the JSX (ligou-app9.jsx:287) has no whitespace node around the <br/> (measured innerText 'Quando a regra exige decisão,ele traz a exceção pronta.'); it needs a space in the markup or the br stays. The .iv h2 br guard (jsx:211) is out of L05's scope, touches the frozen second fold's two-sentence stacking, and has the same missing-space problem, so drop it. Also padding-top:24 alone leaves the seam at ~170px; margin-top:0!important (mirroring .iv at :411) is needed to bring it into the 104-130px range of the other seams. Measured with the corrected CSS injected: title 3 lines (95px at 30px / 107px at 34px), console head at ~340px viewport on arrival, customer bubble, 'Urgência identificada', Ligou reply and 'Regra consultada' all on the arrival screen; langnote reflowed below the console via flex order works (its language cue is already carried inline by 'Cliente · Inglês').


### L06 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Five mockup buttons are dead <button>s with pointer/hover/active states; desktop 'Ver de novo' is a decorated <span> while its mobile twin works

**Where:** ligou-app9.jsx:394 (.lc-apr 'Aprovar encaixe'/.lc-adj), :347 (.p7-apr/.p7-adj), :256 (.iv-approve 'Aprovar e ativar'), :298 (.p7-again <span>) vs :356 (.lc-again button with onClick); index.html:546-548, :486-488, :380-382 (.iv-approve:hover/:active), :437

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** All five are <button type=button> with cursor:pointer, enabled and tabbable, and __reactProps carries no onClick/href, no disabled/aria-disabled. Programmatic click on .lc-apr, .lc-adj, .iv-approve: body.innerHTML, location and focus unchanged. .iv-approve darkens on hover and scales on :active. .p7-again is a SPAN (tabIndex -1, cursor auto) styled as a white pill with a refresh icon at 870,1893. Sizes: .lc-apr 151x48 orange, .p7-apr 133x44, .iv-approve 304x42 (100% width on mobile). They are the most button-like objects in #prova/#diferenca and the only orange fills between hero CTA and pricing CTA.

**Why:** 'Aprovar encaixe' is the largest orange surface inside the proof — orange is the brand's action colour — and tapping it does nothing (Principle 3: no invented affordance; the CTA must say exactly what happens). Screen-reader users tab into 'Aprovar encaixe, botão' and nothing happens; desktop users click 'Ver de novo' and nothing replays. Six extra tab stops with no outcome.

**Fix:** Keep every label and the current visuals; remove the false affordance only. (a) jsx:256, :347, :394 — change `<button className="iv-approve|p7-apr|p7-adj|lc-apr|lc-adj" type="button">` to `<span className="…">` (no aria-hidden, no role; the text stays readable as static content inside the labelled example). (b) index.html:380 drop `cursor:pointer` and the `transition`; delete lines 381-382 (.iv-approve:hover / :active); index.html:486 and :546 drop `cursor:pointer` (add `cursor:default` if the DS button reset applies). Orange fill on .lc-apr/.p7-apr (487/547) may stay — it depicts the dashboard's real approval button — but if RJ wants to honour Principle 1 strictly, swap to the tint pair background #ffe0d6 / color #b83216 on those two lines only. (c) Desktop 'Ver de novo': jsx:298 make it `<button className="p7-again" type="button" onClick={() => setMrun(m => m + 1)}>` (same handler as .lc-again, keep the visible text so no aria-label is needed), add `key={mrun}` to the `.p7-cq` wrapper (jsx:291) and give the board a staged entrance so the replay is visible: in index.html add `.p7-card,.p7-brow,.p7-drawer{animation:lcin .25s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1) var(--lcd,0s) both}` outside the mobile media query, set inline `--lcd` on .p7-c1/.p7-b1 (0s), .p7-b2/.p7-c2 (.5s), .p7-b3/.p7-c3 (1s), .p7-drawer (1.5s), and wrap the rule in `@media (prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference)`. Drop the 'swap Aguardando decisão for Regras ativas e validadas' alternative.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and reproduced in source: src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:256 (.iv-approve), :347 (.p7-apr/.p7-adj), :394 (.lc-apr/.lc-adj) are all `<button type="button">` with no onClick/href/disabled, and grep of assets/js/*.js and ligou-fx2 finds no delegated handler for those classes; index.html:380-382 gives .iv-approve cursor:pointer + :hover darken + :active scale, :486/:546 give the Aprovar/Ajustar pairs cursor:pointer, :487/:547 fill them with --lg-orange-500. .p7-again (jsx:298, css :437) is a `<span>` styled as a white pill while .lc-again (jsx:356) is a working button. Aggravating fact the finding missed: the hero and nav CTAs (jsx:78, :186 `href="#prova"`) drop the visitor exactly onto this board, and on mobile the working replay button teaches that the console is interactive right before the dead orange button. Overstatements: only .iv-approve has hover/active states (the four Aprovar/Ajustar buttons have cursor:pointer only); Principle 3 is about invented state (timers/metrics/testimonials), not affordances — the stronger rule broken is Principle 1 (orange = action, large orange only in hero signature, demo CTA, checkout), and both mockups are labelled as examples (`Chamada · Exemplo` on desktop, `Este exemplo está em inglês` above both). Severity inflated: nothing blocks reading or the conversion path; it is a trust/affordance annoyance plus six pointless tab stops → P2. Fix needs correction: (1) aria-hidden on the spans would hide visible copy from AT users (contradicts 'every string stays') and role="presentation" on a span is a no-op; (2) the 'swap Aguardando for Regras ativas' alternative invents a state change on a landing (Principle 3) and reuses an #diferenca element inside #prova; (3) the desktop board has zero motion (the only animation in the section is `.lc-i{animation:lcin…}` inside the max-width:767px block, index.html:506), so binding .p7-again to mrun replays nothing unless the board is also keyed and given an entrance.


### L07 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Demo approve buttons are white on #ff5a36 (3.10:1) — off-DS and failing AA on the demo's punchline

**Where:** index.html:487 .p7-apr{background:var(--lg-orange-500);color:#fff}; :547 .lc-apr{color:#fff} (14px/700, 48px tall); :568 .nojs-v9__button; vs components.css:9 .lg-btn--accent{color:var(--on-accent)} with --on-accent:var(--lg-ink-950) (colors.css:11)

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** Relative luminance #ff5a36 = 0.288, white = 1.0 → 1.05/0.338 = 3.10:1; AA needs 4.5:1 for 14px bold (large text starts at 18.66px bold). Computed .lc-apr rgb(255,255,255) on rgb(255,90,54). The hero CTA measures ink-950 on orange 5.59:1 — exactly the pair the brand guide certifies (p.17: #FF5A36/#041D27 5.59:1) and lists #FF5A36 as unusable for small white text. Three ad-hoc orange buttons ignore that token.

**Why:** The proof fold builds to '→ Aguardando sua decisão' followed by this button; a daylight phone user gets the weakest contrast on the one control that says 'you stay in control'. It also splits the orange button into two looks (ink text on hero/nav/pricing/final, white text in the demo).

**Fix:** Edit the three declarations in place (do not append a new rule: .lc-apr lives inside the @media (max-width:767px) block starting index.html:496, so a later combined rule must sit after line 550 to win): index.html:487 `.p7-apr{background:var(--lg-orange-500);color:var(--on-accent)}`; :547 `.lc-apr{background:var(--lg-orange-500);color:var(--on-accent)}`; :568 `.nojs-v9__button{...;color:var(--on-accent);...}`. --on-accent is defined on :root in tokens/colors.css (imported by _ds/.../styles.css, linked at index.html:15), so it resolves for the noscript fallback too. Result 5.59:1, identical pair to the hero/nav/pricing/final CTAs. Optional: font-weight:800 on .lc-apr for parity with .nojs-v9__button. Leave .iv-hang (index.html:359) as is: icon-only, 3.10:1 meets the 3:1 non-text threshold. If L06 converts the pair to chips, #b83216 on #ffe0d6 = 4.82:1 passes AA.

**Verifier:** Evidence verified against source: index.html:487 `.p7-apr{background:var(--lg-orange-500);color:#fff}`, :547 `.lc-apr{background:var(--lg-orange-500);color:#fff}` (inside @media max-width:767px, 48px tall, 14px/700), :568 `.nojs-v9__button{background:var(--lg-orange-500);color:#fff}`. No hover/focus overrides exist. Recomputed WCAG luminance: #fff on #ff5a36 = 3.10:1; #041d27 on #ff5a36 = 5.59:1 (matches brand-guide-summary p.17 table). Desktop board type is cqw-scaled and landing-geometry.json measures approveBtn at 13.7px, so both sizes are below the 18.66px bold large-text threshold and need 4.5:1. The four real CTAs (nav :78, hero :186, pricing :483, final :507) all render via DS Button variant="accent" -> _ds_bundle.js:126 'lg-btn--accent' -> components.css:9 color:var(--on-accent) = --lg-ink-950 (colors.css:11), so the 'two looks for the orange button' claim is true. Severity is inflated: .p7-apr/.lc-apr are inert mockup buttons (type=button, no handler) inside a labelled example, not conversion controls; the primary action buttons already pass 5.59:1; the label is a short bold word still legible at 3.1:1. That is an AA fail plus token drift with a one-line fix, i.e. P2 (annoyance/consistency), not P1 (major). The finding's paraphrase of the guide ('unusable for small white text') is a slight stretch — the guide warns about #FF5A36 text on paper — but contrast is symmetric, so the conclusion stands.


### L08 · P1 · ADJUSTED — 2.45s navy splash on every load: ~3.7–3.8s to a readable CTA, an empty hero frame at hand-off, the signature printed twice, click-only skip

**Where:** ligou-app9.jsx:17-40 (Intro4 timers 900/1750/2450ms; onClick={skip} role="presentation", no tabIndex/keydown/sessionStorage), :535-541 (main.play only after onDone); index.html:28-43 (.intro*, .intro.out .6s slide, visibility 0s .7s), :40 (.intro__cap 11px rgba(251,252,248,.45)), :44-48 (.hd opacity:0 / .ln translateY(118%) until .play), :63-65 (.hero-wave draws 1.2s after 1.15s), :177-189 (delays 40–680ms), :242-245
