Screenshot at ~2.3s: navy hero with zero copy (all .hd opacity 0) — only the robot video until 'Ligou?' rises at ~2.7s. Intro shows 'Ligou?' (0–0.9s) → orange 'Atendido.' at clamp(56px,9vw,110px) (0.9–1.75s); the hero animates the identical words 0.8s later. No sessionStorage guard: every reload/back-navigation pays 2.45s. Skip is an undocumented click on the veil; Tab focus moves under it to .hd{opacity:0} elements. Reduced-motion users skip it entirely (LGFX_REDUCED) — proof the page works without it. Caption paper@.45 on #04182a = 4.37:1 at 11px (under AA).

**Why:** The user is on a roof or in a van with a missed call in mind; four seconds of ceremony before the primary action, a visibly blank frame, and the frozen 'Ligou? Atendido.' pre-empted read as latency and 'deslumbramento tecnológico' (brand p.8, p.22; Principle 4). Nielsen H3/H7 fail; keyboard users cannot skip. Also the fourth large orange surface (L09).

**Fix:** Keep Intro4 (approved v9 element, keeps 'ligou.ai · agente operacional'), make it cheap and skippable; offer removal to RJ as a separate yes/no. In ligou-app9.jsx Intro4: (a) once per session: const seen = (()=>{try{return sessionStorage.getItem('ligou-intro')==='1'}catch(e){return false}})(); in useEffect `if (LGFX_REDUCED || seen) { fire(); return; }` and after the timers `try{sessionStorage.setItem('ligou-intro','1')}catch(e){}`; after the effect `if (LGFX_REDUCED || seen) return null;` (hooks order preserved). (b) Timers: t1 450ms -> setTxt(1); t2 900ms -> { setOut(true); fire(); } (one hand-off: .play lands while the panel slides .6s, no blank frame); t3 dropped. Skip: `const skip = () => { setOut(true); fire(); }`. (c) Real skip target: render the root as <button type="button" className={'intro intro4 '+(out?'out':'')} onClick={skip} onKeyDown={e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '||e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();skip();}}} aria-label="Pular introdução" autoFocus> with `.intro{appearance:none;border:0;padding:0;background:none;text-align:inherit}` added to index.html:28; in useEffect add window listeners for 'wheel' and 'touchmove' -> skip (passive), removed on cleanup. (d) app9:35: render <div className="intro__t" key="b">Atendido.</div> without <span className="acc"> so the intro adds no fourth large orange text (hero .acc at :181 untouched). index.html: (e) :40 `.intro__cap{...color:rgba(251,252,248,.62)}` (7.2:1). (f) :41 `.intro__bar` animation duration 1.75s -> .9s to match t2. (g) Scope hero stagger tightening to the hero only: add `.hero4 .play .hd,.play .hero4 .hd{transition-duration:.5s}` and `.play .hero4 .ln{transition-duration:.5s}` (do not edit :45/:47 globally; Cta at app9:503-504 keeps its rise); in app9:177-189 set --d to 0/60/160/260/380/480ms (copy, wrapper and order unchanged). (h) :64 `.hero-wave path{...transition:stroke-dashoffset .7s var(--ease-out) .3s}` so the orange line finishes (~1.0s) before the lede (.38s+.5s = .88s... set lede --d 420ms if the line must strictly land first). Resulting timeline: first-visit CTA readable ≈ mount+900+480+500 ≈ 1.9s; repeat visits/back-nav ≈ mount+480+500 ≈ 1.0s. Separate RJ question (not default): remove <Intro4/> and start `useState(true)` in App (:536), knowing it drops the string 'ligou.ai · agente operacional' from the page.

**Verifier:** Evidence holds against the source. ligou-app9.jsx:17-40 Intro4 uses setTimeout 900/1750/2450, onClick skip on a role="presentation" div with no tabIndex/onKeyDown, no sessionStorage/localStorage guard anywhere in the repo; App (:536-541) only adds .play to <main> when onDone fires at t3. index.html:44-47 keeps every .hd at opacity:0 and every .ln at translateY(118%) until .play; hero --d values are 40/60/160/290/440/560/680ms with .65s/.85s durations, so CTA fully visible = mount + 2450 + 560 + 650 ≈ mount+3660 (matches 3780 with a ~120ms mount), trust line ≈ mount+3780 (3881), wave 1.15s delay + 1.2s draw ≈ mount+4800 (4790). The blank hand-off frame is real: .out starts at t2 (1750) and the panel is gone by ~2350, but .play does not arrive until 2450, then 'Ligou?' only starts rising at +160ms -> ~700ms of navy hero with only the autoplaying video and zero copy. Intro font clamp(56px,9vw,110px) = 56px at 390 and 110px at 1440; .intro__cap rgba(251,252,248,.45) over #04182a computes to 4.36:1 (finding says 4.37) at 11px, under AA for small text (transient/decorative, so low weight). LGFX_REDUCED returns null and fires immediately (fx2.js:6, app9:23/29) so the page provably works without the intro. Brand cite verified: brand-guide-summary.md p.8 'não usa medo, urgência artificial ou deslumbramento tecnológico'; p.22 not found (imagery avoid-list is p.21). Severity P1 stands: primary action gated ~2.5s+ on every load and back-navigation for a phone user, plus an unskippable-by-keyboard overlay. Adjustments needed: (1) 'where' misattributes the 40-680ms delays to index.html:177-189; they live in ligou-app9.jsx:177-189 (index.html:177-189 is the <=920px media query). (2) The 'preferred' fix (remove <Intro4/>) deletes the string 'ligou.ai · agente operacional' which exists only in the intro (app9:36) and removes an element RJ shipped in the approved v9 checkpoint, colliding with 'every string stays' and 'melhorar, não refazer' -> must be a question for RJ, not the default. (3) The fallback fix is self-contradictory: 'add .play to <main> at mount' and 'call fire() at out so the headline rises while the veil lifts' cannot both be true (with .play at mount the headline is already up). (4) Tightening .hd/.ln durations at index.html:45/47 globally also changes the Cta section rise (app9:503-504 .ln via .wr-on); scope to .hero4. Frozen decisions and palette are untouched by either variant; dropping orange from the intro's 'Atendido.' does not affect the frozen hero fold.


### L09 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Orange budget blown: 6–8 large orange surfaces vs the contract's 3; internal headlines spend the accent and the accent word outsizes its h2

**Where:** ligou-app9.jsx:213 + index.html:340 .iv-word 'Em português.' clamp(52px,6.4vw,92px) color --lg-orange-500 (:414 mobile clamp(44px,12vw,58px) vs .iv h2 clamp(30px,8.4vw,38px) at :413); jsx:406 + index.html:131-132 .dorbig .acc 'não fica esperando.' --text-accent (30–52px); Intro4 jsx:35 + index.html:38-39 .intro__t .acc (56–110px); approve buttons index.html:487/:547; :359 .iv-hang orange; pricing CTA jsx:483; final CTA jsx:504-507 + index.html:157-158

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** Large orange in load order: (1) intro 'Atendido.' full-screen; (2) hero 'Atendido.' 50.7/77.8px + button 346x53; (3) .iv-word 92px; (4) demo approve fill; (5) pricing button; (6) final 'Atendido.' 58.5/144px + button; plus the sticky nav pill (always on) = 8 on mobile. In #diferenca the 44–58px accent word is larger than the section h2 (30–38px). Small orange: .prog bar, 7 eyebrow dashes, hero wave, 22 marquee dots, 5 FAQ '+', 5 arrows, 8 numerals, 2 connector blocks, hang-up pill, board dots. .impeccable Principle 1: 'no máximo três lugares: assinatura do hero, CTA da demo, checkout final… Headline interna não gasta acento'.

**Why:** Orange is defined as 'ação, telefonia e pontos de decisão' (brand p.16). When a 92px non-clickable phrase, a splash, a headline fragment and 22 scrolling bullets wear it, the eye stops reading orange as 'press here' and the hero CTA competes with six orange objects in its own viewport.

**Fix:** Two CSS edits, no string changes, no dependency on other findings. (1) index.html:340 .iv-word — change color:var(--lg-orange-500) to color:var(--lg-paper) (equivalently color:inherit, since .iv at :335 already sets paper). KEEP font-size:clamp(52px,6.4vw,92px) and the :414 mobile clamp(44px,12vw,58px): Principle 1 makes weight+scale the internal-hierarchy tool, and the recolour is legible on both gradients (verified: mobile word bottom at 16.6% of a 180deg gradient that is dark until 26%; desktop 92px text ends ~51% of the 96deg gradient). (2) index.html:132 .dorbig .acc — change color:var(--text-accent) to color:inherit. Optional brand-board idiom: in src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:406 and :213 wrap only the terminal '.' in <span className="acc">.</span> (markup only, string unchanged); if used on the Dor paper surface it must stay --text-accent (#b83216) per brand p.17, on the dark .iv it may be --lg-orange-500. DO NOT touch .iv-hang (46x32 small detail, brand telephony badge; ink-950 would vanish on the #0d2536 card). DO NOT touch .lg-btn--accent, .p7-apr, .lc-apr (demo CTA / checkout / decision point are the contract's sanctioned places; header repetition is Principle 5). Intro splash belongs to L08 independently. Result: large orange text outside signature/CTA/checkout goes from 2 to 0.

**Verifier:** Core defect is real and contract-explicit, but the count, the 'why', and half the fix are wrong. VERIFIED (source + live mirror 127.0.0.1:4174): index.html:340 .iv-word = #ff5a36 at 46.8px (390) / 92px (1440) vs .iv h2 32.76px / 56.16px (index.html:413/:337); index.html:132 .dorbig .acc = --text-accent = --lg-orange-700 #b83216 (tokens/colors.css:10) at 30px / 52px. Both are non-actionable internal text, and .impeccable.md Principle 1 literally says 'Headline interna não gasta acento — hierarquia ali se faz com peso e escala'. Two clear violations. INFLATED: of the '8 large orange surfaces', five are .lg-btn--accent (nav, hero, pricing, final) plus .lc-apr/.p7-apr — i.e. the demo CTA (Principle 5: the header repeats the same action), the checkout, and the demo's decision point (brand p.16 'pontos de decisão') — all sanctioned by the contract's own three places. FALSE: 'the hero CTA competes with six orange objects in its own viewport' — measured at 390x844 the first viewport holds exactly 3 large orange objects: frozen 'Atendido.' (50.7px), the CTA (346x53) and its nav twin (113x44), plus a 22x3 eyebrow dash and a 3px wave stroke; the two violations sit at y=1211 and y=3575 of a 9911px page, far from the hero. FIX DEFECTS: (a) it says 'the 92px scale carries the hierarchy' then caps font-size at the h2 clamp — contradictory and against Principle 1, which names scale as the sanctioned internal-hierarchy tool; (b) .iv-hang is 46x32 = 'detalhe pequeno… é livre', the orange handset is the brand's telephony channel badge (p.14/p.16), and an ink-950 pill on the #0d2536 card over an rgba(.05) callbar would be near-invisible; (c) the fix depends on L06/L07/L08 instead of standing alone. LEGIBILITY OF THE PAPER RECOLOUR CHECKED: mobile .iv gradient is 180deg (index.html:397) dark to 26% of height; word bottom measured at 16.6% -> on #041a2b–#07222f. Desktop gradient 96deg; 92px text ends ~51% of width (between #062231 and #40595a) -> paper contrast >7:1. Severity: no reading or conversion harm for the phone/daylight user; it is a brand-contract fidelity issue fixed by two one-line CSS edits -> P2, not P1.


### L10 · P1 · ADJUSTED — Pricing CTA 'Quero aproveitar a oferta' links to its own section — a dead primary at the money moment

**Where:** ligou-app9.jsx:483 <Button variant="accent" href="#preco">Quero aproveitar a oferta</Button> inside <section id="preco"> (:468); compiled assets/js/ligou-app9.js contains href: "#preco"; index.html:19 html{scroll-behavior:smooth}

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** The section's own id is the anchor target of the only accent button inside it; a tap scrolls ~600–800px back to the section top and nothing else happens. It is the only orange primary in the price block.

**Why:** The contract lets checkout dominate only in the price block; here it is a no-op. Brand p.23: 'O CTA principal deve dizer exatamente o que acontece'. Casey experiences a misfire, Rafael reads 'the offer is not real'. IA rates P1 (money moment), fidelity P2 — the IA argument (highest-intent click wasted) is the better-evidenced one.

**Fix:** In src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:483 change href="#preco" to href="#prova" (rebuild so assets/js/ligou-app9.js:1751 follows). Keep the label 'Quero aproveitar a oferta', keep variant="accent" and the existing marginTop: 8. Nothing else moves; 'Plano mes a mes. Sem fidelidade.' can stay where it is. Until a real checkout URL exists, this makes the only orange control in the price block do the page's sanctioned primary action (the demo) instead of scrolling to its own heading. When a checkout/contract URL is available, swap the same href for it; a self-anchor must never return.

**Verifier:** Core defect is real and reproduced. Source: /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:468 opens <section id="preco"> and :483 renders <Button variant="accent" href="#preco">Quero aproveitar a oferta</Button>; the DS Button (_ds_bundle.js:118) emits a plain <a href> so nothing else fires; compiled assets/js/ligou-app9.js:1751 carries href: "#preco"; index.html:19 sets scroll-behavior:smooth. Live measurement on the local mirror (127.0.0.1:4174) at 390x844: the button (220x43, 15px) sits 924px below the section top; with the button at viewport y 574 a click moved the page only 324px up (scrollY 8246 -> 7922), left the button off-screen (top 898 > 844) and put the heading 'Contrate ate 31 de dezembro de 2026 por $299/mes.' at the top of the screen — a no-op that reads as a glitch. At 1440x900 the button is 763px below the section top, so the jump is smaller and the button stays visible. Two overstatements: the '~600-800px' scroll is really ~300px on mobile, and the fix direction is off. The evidence about orange is correct: it is the only .lg-btn--accent in the block (the four accent buttons on the page are hero, nav, this one, and the final CTA; the 'Oferta' badge is a pale tint). Severity P1 holds: the contract (.impeccable.md principle 5) says checkout 'so domina no bloco de preco', so this is the one place the page puts checkout forward, and brand p.23 requires the primary CTA to say exactly what happens; a dead primary at the money moment fails both, even though demo conversion elsewhere is intact (hence not P0). Fix adjusted: (1) #comecar is a static five-card list 1750px above with no action on arrival — landing there is another anchor no-op, just rationalised; the contract's own rule ('A demo e a prova. Ela e a acao primaria em qualquer ponto da pagina'; brand base layer 'Demo primeiro. Compra depois.'; the final CTA already routes 'Falar com o Ligou agora' to #prova) points to #prova. (2) Do not downgrade to variant="secondary": principle 1 explicitly reserves one of the three large-orange slots for 'o checkout final', so removing accent here spends nothing and loses the sanctioned emphasis.


### L11 · P2 · ADJUSTED — English demo dialogue has no lang="en" (WCAG 3.1.2 Language of Parts)

**Where:** ligou-app9.jsx:333-335 (.p7-bub text), :362, :371, :379 (.lc-bub), :449 (FAQ answer quoting "Hi, you've reached [Your Business]…")

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** DOM audit: the only [lang] attribute is <html lang="pt-BR">. Nine English sentences (six bubbles across both consoles + the FAQ quote) inherit pt-BR.

**Why:** TalkBack/VoiceOver read 'Hi, water is coming in near the chimney' with a Portuguese voice — gibberish exactly where the page promises proof; the demo's accessible rendering is broken for the users who need speech.

**Fix:** In /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx: (1) lines 362, 371, 379 — add lang="en-US" to each `.lc-bub` div (the `.lc-who` PT label is a sibling, so the whole bubble is English). (2) lines 333-335 — inside each `.p7-bub`, keep `<span className="p7-blbl">…</span>` as is and wrap the remaining text run (including the `<br/>`) in `<span lang="en-US">…</span>`; do not put lang on `.p7-bub` itself or the PT label gets marked English. An inline span inherits `.p7-bub`'s white-space:nowrap, cqw font-size and `.p7-b3 .p7-bub` override, so geometry is unchanged. (3) line 449 — the FAQ answer is a plain string rendered via `<p>{a}</p>`; convert that one qs entry's answer to JSX: `<>Ele se apresenta como assistente virtual da sua empresa: "<span lang="en-US">Hi, you’ve reached [Your Business]. I’m their virtual assistant — how can I help?</span>" A conversa é natural, mas a confiança do seu cliente não depende de fingir que existe uma pessoa do outro lado.</>` keeping the ’ apostrophes and straight quotes so the rendered text is byte-identical; `key={qa[0]}` uses the question and is unaffected. `lang="en"` is also compliant; en-US is preferred because the scene is a US business and selects a US voice. No CSS, no copy, no layout change.

**Verifier:** Evidence confirmed in source: index.html:2 carries the only lang attribute (pt-BR); English dialogue with no lang exists at ligou-app9.jsx:333-335 (.p7-bub, PT label .p7-blbl nested inside), :362/:371/:379 (.lc-bub, label .lc-who is a sibling), and :449 (FAQ string with the "Hi, you've reached…" quote). Noscript fallback (index.html:580-605) is PT-only, so it is not affected. But the impact is overstated: index.html:495 `.p7m{display:none}` and :501 `.p7-cq{display:none}` (max-width 767px) mean only one console is in the accessibility tree per viewport, so an AT user hits 3 mispronounced bubbles plus 1 FAQ quote, not nine sentences; the PT labels, event rows and the entire PT drawer/sheet still read correctly, so the demo's accessible rendering is degraded in three bubbles, not broken. For the brief's target user (sighted owner on a phone in daylight) there is no effect on conversion or reading. Real WCAG 3.1.2 (AA) miss, cheap to fix, but P2 on the brief's scale, not P1.


### L12 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Live <title> is an export artefact, no OG/twitter tags, favicon is the channel badge the brand guide says is not the corporate mark

**Where:** index.html:6 (<title>Ligou 2026 v9 — Responsive Hero Video</title>), :12 (rel=icon assets/crop-logo-mark.png 62x62 handset), :1-17 (no og:/twitter: meta); nav and intro correctly use AgentNodeMark (jsx:42-54, :69)

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** Tab, bookmark and screen-reader page announcement read 'Ligou 2026 v9 — Responsive Hero Video' (2.4.2 Page Titled). grep for og:/twitter: returns nothing. Brand p.14: the handset is the 'badge de canal' and 'esses dois papéis não devem ser confundidos'; p.12 descriptor 'Agente operacional de inteligência artificial para negócios de serviços'. Sibling Ligou-MVP already uses 'Ligou — Agente operacional de inteligência artificial'.

**Why:** The audience's channel is WhatsApp; a shared link previews a dev title, no image and the wrong mark. Fidelity rates P1 (share path), a11y P2 (title only) — P1 kept because the defect is visible to every recipient before the page loads.

**Fix:** index.html:6 -> <title>Ligou — Agente operacional de inteligência artificial para negócios de serviços</title> (MVP pattern + p.12 descriptor; page copy untouched). After :14 add: <meta property="og:type" content="website">, <meta property="og:locale" content="pt_BR">, <meta property="og:site_name" content="Ligou">, <meta property="og:title" content="Ligou? Atendido. — Agente operacional de inteligência artificial para negócios de serviços">, <meta property="og:description" content="[the existing :14 description string, verbatim]">, <meta property="og:image" content="https://<deployed-origin>/assets/og-ligou.png"> (ABSOLUTE URL, 1200x630, <=300 KB, PNG/JPG — not hero-poster.png, which is 1672x941 / 1.7 MB and a video frame), <meta property="og:image:width" content="1200">, <meta property="og:image:height" content="630">, <meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image"> + twitter:title/description/image mirroring the og values. Produce assets/og-ligou.png by regenerating the existing /Users/d1f/Desktop/Ligou.AI/og-card.svg: swap the top-left handset circle for AgentNodeMark (jsx:42-54 geometry), replace the eyebrow 'AGENTE OPERACIONAL BILÍNGUE' with the p.12 descriptor, keep 'Ligou? Atendido.' (Familjen 600, Atendido in --signal-dark on paper), keep the robot (chest badge on the character is allowed), light paper background per brand p.13. Favicon: export AgentNodeMark to assets/agent-node.svg (keep the rgba(251,252,248,.3) head outline so it reads on dark tab bars) plus 32/180/192/512 PNGs; the 180 apple-touch-icon on solid petrol #041d27. Replace :12 with <link rel="icon" href="assets/agent-node.svg" type="image/svg+xml"> + <link rel="icon" type="image/png" sizes="32x32" href="assets/agent-node-32.png"> + <link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="assets/agent-node-180.png">. Also replace the footer <img src="assets/crop-logo-mark.png"> at src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:519 with <AgentNodeMark size={34}/> so the wordmark+handset lockup (brand p.15 don't) disappears. After deploy, test the preview with a cache-busting query (WhatsApp/Facebook cache the first fetch).

**Verifier:** Evidence holds on every point I could check: worktree index.html:6 and the live origin (curl https://client-nine-taupe-24.vercel.app/) both serve <title>Ligou 2026 v9 — Responsive Hero Video</title>; grep for og:/twitter: in index.html returns nothing; :12 rel=icon points to assets/crop-logo-mark.png, which is a 62x62 orange circle with a dark handset glyph (viewed), and brand p.14 (tmp/brand-guide-summary.md:111-117) says the handset is the channel badge and 'esses dois papéis não devem ser confundidos'. /Users/d1f/Desktop/Ligou-MVP/index.html:6 really is 'Ligou — Agente operacional de inteligência artificial'. The finding also under-reports: Footer (src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:519) renders the same handset PNG beside the 'Ligou' wordmark, which is the exact don't listed on brand p.15 ('não combinar logo e badge sem regra', summary :122/:134). Where it is wrong: (1) the FIX is not implementable as written — assets/hero-poster.png is 1672x941, not 1920x1080, and 1.7 MB; WhatsApp drops og:image above ~300 KB and requires an absolute URL, so the proposed tag would yield no image, the very defect being fixed; (2) the repo already has a share-card pipeline (Desktop/Ligou.AI/og-card.svg -> og-ligou.png, 1200x630, 153 KB, Aug 10) that only needs the handset lockup and the stale eyebrow 'AGENTE OPERACIONAL BILÍNGUE' replaced; (3) severity: the page is noindex on a vercel preview URL, no copy, fold or conversion path is blocked, and the recipient who taps gets the full page — this is a share-path/fidelity annoyance, P2, not P1 'major'. The corporate mark itself is DECISÃO PENDENTE (Agent Node vs A Linha, summary :6/:111), so the finding's claim that AgentNodeMark is 'the' corporate mark overstates; it is the v9 choice RJ froze for the hero/nav, which is enough to justify using it, but it is a decision to name to RJ, not a settled rule.


### L13 · P2 · ADJUSTED — No type scale: 7 ad-hoc h2 sizes, 4 lede sizes and 11 running-text sizes between 12 and 17px; silent sections outsize the price h2

**Where:** index.html:191 (--size-display:34px ≤600), :131 .dorbig clamp(30px,3.8vw,52px), :423 .p7-title clamp(34px,3.7vw,57px), :337/:413 .iv h2 clamp(34px,3.9vw,58px)/clamp(30px,8.4vw,38px), jsx:12 & :471 var(--size-display)=48px; ledes :272/:318 .h4-lede clamp(17px,1.35vw,20px)→16.5px, :424 .p7-sub, :339 .iv-sub, :425 .p7-bridge 750, jsx:13/:407 var(--size-body-lg); small text :140 .startcard p 14.5, :154 .incitem 13.5, :364 .iv-row p 14, :533 .lc-ev em 13.5, :151 14, :426 .p7-langnote 13, :277 .h4-trust 13, :356 .iv-id i 13, :379 .iv-own i 12.5, :515 .lc-htime 12.5, jsx:492/:522 14

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** Measured h2 at 390: 30 (Dor) / 34 (Prova) / 32.76 (Diferença) / 34 (Faz/Começar/FAQ/Preço). At 1440: 52 / 53.28 / 56.16 / 48 — Dor, Prova, Diferença are 4–8px LARGER than the Preço h2, inverting 'Hero e preço recebem escala editorial; o restante é silencioso'. Ledes at 390: 16.5 (hero) / 17 (Dor) / 16 (Prova) / 16 (Diferença) — the hero lede is the smallest. Running text at 390: 12, 12.5, 13, 13.5, 14, 14.5, 15, 15.5, 16, 16.5, 17 all present. Lede weights: hero 500, Dor 500, Prova 800 (.p7-bridge).

**Why:** In daylight the reader relies on size steps to know heading / lede / fact / footnote; half-pixel differences cost nothing in hierarchy and everything in rhythm, and every future edit becomes a new clamp.

**Fix:** Two h2 steps that honor the contract (price editorial, rest quiet), plus a 4-step running-text scale. Copy untouched. In index.html <style> add: :root{--t-h2-quiet:clamp(30px,calc(20px + 1.7vw),44px);--t-h2-price:clamp(34px,calc(24px + 2vw),52px);--t-h3:clamp(21px,calc(16px + .6vw),24px);--t-lede:clamp(17px,1.35vw,19px);--t-body:16px;--t-small:15px;--t-caption:13px} (390: quiet 30 / price 34; 1440: quiet 44 / price 52 — price is always the larger step). H2: .dorbig (:131), .p7-title (:423), .iv h2 (:337) → font-size:var(--t-h2-quiet); delete :413 (.iv h2 mobile clamp). src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:12 SectionHead h2 fontSize:'var(--t-h2-quiet)'; jsx:471 price h2 fontSize:'var(--t-h2-price)'. H3: jsx:408 and jsx:491 fontSize:'var(--t-h3)' — only then delete :191 (otherwise keep `:root{--size-h3:21px}` there). Ledes: .h4-lede (:272), .p7-sub (:424), .iv-sub (:339), .iv-q → font-size:var(--t-lede); jsx:13 and jsx:407 fontSize:'var(--t-lede)'; delete :318; .p7-bridge{font-weight:600}. Body: .iv-line, .lc-darrow → var(--t-body). Small (15): .startcard p (:140), .incitem (:154), .iv-row p (:364), .price-mobile-normal (:151), .p7-langnote (:426), .footer-links (jsx:522), jsx:492 fontSize:15, .p7-foot, .iv-own b, .iv-approve, .lc-rel, .lc-apr/.lc-adj. Caption (13): keep .h4-trust (:277) at 13; .lc-ev em (:533) 13.5→13 (stays a clear step under the 16px .lc-bub); .iv-pill 13.5→13; .lc-htime (:515), .iv-own i (:379) 12.5→13; .iv-id i (:356) already 13. Leave .lc-ts inheriting 11px from .lc-who (same row as its label), leave the tracked uppercase micro-label tier (10–11px) and the nav compression rules (≤600/≤399) alone — they are a deliberate label tier, not running text. Keep .hero4 h1 and .cta h2 exactly as-is.

**Verifier:** Evidence checks out against the source: every cited line exists (index.html :131 .dorbig clamp(30,3.8vw,52), :423 .p7-title clamp(34,3.7vw,57), :337/:413 .iv h2, :191 mobile :root override --size-display:34px, DS token --size-display:48px used by jsx:12 SectionHead and jsx:471 price h2). Computed at 1440: 52 / 53.28 / 56.16 vs 48 for Faz/Começar/FAQ/Preço; at 390: 30 / 34 / 32.76 / 34 — matches landing-geometry.json exactly. The contract line is real (.impeccable.md:96-97 'Hero e preço recebem escala editorial; o restante é silencioso'), so on desktop the three silent sections really do outsize the price h2, and on mobile price is merely tied with the silent sections, not editorial. The 0.5px running-text ladder (12, 12.5, 13, 13.5, 14, 14.5, 15, 15.5, 16, 16.5, 17) is also real. But three parts are off. (1) Evidence errors: 'the hero lede is the smallest' is false by its own numbers (hero 16.5 > Prova 16 / Diferença 16); Dor lede (jsx:407) has no fontWeight so it renders at --weight-regular 440, not 500. (2) Severity is inflated: the user is a phone reader; at 390 the h2 spread is 4px and 13 vs 13.5 vs 14 is not perceptible in daylight — this is a rhythm/maintenance defect plus a contract violation visible mainly on desktop, not something that blocks reading or the demo. P2, not P1. (3) The fix contradicts the principle it cites: collapsing ALL h2 (including Preço) into one --t-h2 makes price equal to the silent sections (32/52 everywhere) instead of editorial; on mobile it actually shrinks the price h2 from 34 to 32. It also deletes :191 wholesale, which drops --size-h3:21px and silently grows jsx:408 and jsx:491 (the bold Dor/Pricing paragraphs) from 21 to 24px on mobile without mapping them to the new --t-h3. Pushing .h4-trust and .lc-ev em from caption size to 15px puts them one pixel under the 16px console bubbles / two under the 17px lede, weakening the exact caption-vs-message step the fix claims to restore. And 'one scale' is overstated: .iv-pill 13.5, .iv-own b / .iv-approve 14.5, .lc-rel / .lc-apr 14, .p7-foot 14, .lc-id b 15.5 are left untouched, so 6+ ad-hoc sizes survive. Selectors are otherwise implementable and copy is untouched.


### L14 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Uppercase micro-labels follow 14+ specs (9.1–13px, 700/800, .06–.20em, two typefaces); 10–10.5px labels sit under the DS 12px caption floor

**Where:** index.html:266 .h4-eyebrow clamp(10px,.78vw,11.5px)/700/.14em; components.css:32 .lg-eyebrow 12px/700/.16em; :126 .pt-cap__label 10.5px/.18em; :344 .iv-lab 13px/800/.18em FAMILJEN; :384 .iv-railitem 12.5px; :388 .iv-memory__label 11px/800/.17em; :391 .iv-memory__approval 10.5px; :525 .lc-who 11px/.12em; :532 .lc-ev b 11px/.13em; :535 .lc-dhead 11px/.16em; :483 .p7-decision 10px; :513 .lc-en 10px/.06em; components.css:22 .lg-badge 11px/.08em; jsx:501 12px/.18em; jsx:466 11px/.16em; :40 .intro__cap 11px/.2em; tokens typography.css:3 --size-caption:12px, --track-caps:.16em

**Viewports:** 390x844, 768x1024, 1024x768, 1440x900

**Evidence:** Measured at 390: hero eyebrow 10px (its clamp floors at 10px for every viewport under 1282px — 10px at 768 and 1024 too); 'Preço' kicker 10.5px; 'DECISÃO DE EXCEÇÃO' 10px; .lc-en 10px; 'MEMÓRIA PERMANENTE DO SEU NEGÓCIO' 11px over 2 lines; '01 CONVERSA' 13px Familjen while 'CLIENTE · INGLÊS' is 11px Archivo — same role, different face. Section kickers alone use four specs (hero #7fc7b9 10px + orange bar; Começar DS Eyebrow 12px teal-700 + bar; Preço .pt-cap__label 10.5px teal-400 + dot; CTA inline 12px teal-400, no mark). Tracking values in use: .06/.08/.1/.12/.13/.14/.16/.17/.18/.2em. Archivo 800 labels render true 800 while 'display 800' headlines render 700 (L36).

**Why:** Labels are the wayfinding layer for a reader skimming outdoors; a dozen variants read as noise, the 10px ones fall under the daylight floor, and the contract says labels are 'caixa-alta em Archivo com tracking' (impeccable.md:152) — .iv-lab in Familjen breaks that. Small-caps-everything is also a top AI tell.

**Fix:** All in index.html <style> unless noted. (a) Tokens, adopting the DS values instead of inventing new ones: :root{--t-label:var(--size-caption);--t-label-track:var(--track-caps);--t-label-weight:var(--weight-bold)} (12px / .16em / 700). (b) One shared rule for the page-specific label selectors: .h4-eyebrow,.pt-cap__label,.iv-lab,.iv-railitem,.iv-memory__label,.iv-memory__approval,.lc-who,.lc-ev b,.lc-dhead,.p7-decision,.intro__cap{font-family:var(--font-body);font-size:var(--t-label);font-weight:var(--t-label-weight);letter-spacing:var(--t-label-track);text-transform:uppercase} — leave .lg-eyebrow (components.css:32) untouched; it already equals the token. Keep the step index in the display face to match .iv-railitem b and .startcard .sn: .iv-lab b{font-family:var(--font-display)}. Then delete the now-redundant size/weight/tracking/family declarations from :40, :126, :344, :384, :388, :391, :483, :525, :532, :535 and change jsx:466 label to {fontSize:'var(--t-label)',fontWeight:'var(--t-label-weight)',letterSpacing:'var(--t-label-track)',textTransform:'uppercase'} and jsx:501 to the same tokens (colour stays var(--lg-teal-400)). (c) .h4-eyebrow :266: replace font-size:clamp(10px,.78vw,11.5px) with the token (12px at all widths); keep color #7fc7b9 (9.2:1 on #04182a) and the orange 22x3 bar; white-space:normal already applies under 1200px (:300, :317), so it wraps to two lines at 390 — verify the hero still keeps the CTA at ~y392 in the 844 fold. (d) Preço kicker: either keep <span className="pt-cap__label">Preço</span> and let rule (b) size it, or swap to <Eyebrow>Preço</Eyebrow> with a local override color:var(--lg-teal-400) (the DS default --text-eyebrow is teal-700 and fails on --surface-inverse); the 22x2 var(--accent) bar is a mark, not a large orange surface, so it is within the brand rule. String unchanged either way. (e) Mobile-only pill .lc-en :513: 10px -> 11px. Do NOT touch .p7-en or any other cqw-sized board label here; file the board scale (4.3-7.7px at 768-1024, .p7-decision wrapping to 31px inside a 103px card at 768) as a separate finding with its own fix (e.g. show .p7m instead of .p7-cq below ~1100px, or re-derive the cqw scale). (f) Delete dead CSS completely, not partially: .trustline (:66, :203), .scrollhint group (:68, :69, :70, :71, :172 and the .scrollhint i::after entry in the reduced-motion list at :232), .callchip/.callchip i/.callchip--accent i (:115-117); confirm with grep over src/runtime and assets/js that nothing but src/claude-v9/Ligou 2026 v9.html references them.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and reproduces on the byte-identical mirror (http://127.0.0.1:4174/, sha 2966050505c1cd8e = worktree index.html). Measured at 390x844: .h4-eyebrow 10px Archivo 700 (clamp(10px,.78vw,11.5px) floors at 10px for every viewport under 1282px; confirmed 10px at 768 and 1024, 11.23px at 1440), and it wraps into a 39px-tall block above the frozen h1; 'Preço' .pt-cap__label 10.5px; 'Decisão de exceção' .p7-decision 10px (fixed px, reaches both the desktop board and the mobile console); .lc-who/.lc-ev b/.lc-dhead 11px/800 with .12/.13/.16em; .iv-memory__label 11px over 2 lines; .iv-memory__approval 10.5px; .iv-lab 13px in Familjen Grotesk with computed weight 800 (only familjen-grotesk-600/700.ttf are declared in tokens/fonts.css, so it renders 700, confirming the L36 observation) while .iv-railitem/.lc-who are Archivo; .lg-eyebrow 12px/.16em; jsx:501 12px/.18em; jsx:466 label 11px/.16em; .intro__cap 11px/.2em; .lg-badge 11px/.08em (and 'Oferta por tempo limitado' also wraps to 2 lines at 390). That is 14 distinct specs and ten tracking values, as claimed. The contract breach is real: .impeccable.md:152 says editorial labels are 'caixa-alta em Archivo com tracking' and .iv-lab is set in var(--font-display). The dead selectors (.trustline :66/:203, .scrollhint :68-71/:172/:232, .callchip :115-117) exist only in index.html and the design export src/claude-v9/Ligou 2026 v9.html; nothing in src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx renders them. Corrections: (1) the 'where' field mislabels index.html lines (:126, :344, :384, :388, :391, :483, :513, :525, :532, :535) as components.css; only :22 .lg-badge and :32 .lg-eyebrow live in components.css. (2) 'Under the DS 12px caption floor' is overstated: --size-caption is a token, not a floor; the DS's own components ship .lg-badge at 11px and .lg-capsule__label (components.css:41) at 10.5px/.18em, which is exactly the spec .pt-cap__label copies. The legitimate argument is legibility for a phone-in-daylight reader plus the contract, not a DS floor. (3) Contrast of #7fc7b9 on the hero --h4bg #04182a computes to 9.2:1, not 8.8:1 (immaterial). (4) Severity is inflated: h1 (50.7px), lede (16.5px) and CTA (17px) carry the sales argument at 390; the labels are the wayfinding layer, and the lede, not the eyebrow, does the 'what is this' job. This is a consistency-and-legibility annoyance, P2, not a major reading/conversion blocker. (5) The fix invents a third tracking value (.14em) instead of adopting the DS token --track-caps:.16em that .lg-eyebrow and .lg-step already use, so as written it adds one more spec rather than converging. (6) The 'board labels max(11px,.9cqw)' and '.p7-en floor 11px' sub-fixes are not implementable as stated: at 768 the .p7-board is 597px wide, cards are 17.3% = 103px, labels are white-space:nowrap, and .p7-clabel renders at 4.6px, .p7-blbl 4.3px, .p7-en 4.8px (6.1 to 7.7px at 1024); jumping them to 11px inside 103px nowrap cards will overflow. The board's cqw type scale at 768-1024 is its own defect and should be filed separately, not bolted onto the label token.


### L15 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Mobile console carries no visible 'Exemplo' label and reads as a live call; both summary <aside>s are unnamed

**Where:** ligou-app9.jsx:352-357 (.lc-head: 'Ligou em chamada', EN pill, waveform SVG, '00:12', replay), :384 (<aside class="lc-sheet"> no aria-label), :336 (<aside class="p7-drawer"> no aria-label); :293 (.p7-blab 'Chamada · Exemplo' lives inside .p7-cq which is display:none ≤767 at index.html:501); :508-516 (.lc-head styles)

**Viewports:** 390x844

**Evidence:** The only 'exemplo' cue on mobile is .p7-langnote (13px) above the shell, outside the artefact and scrolled away by the time the orange 'Aprovar encaixe' is reached. The header asserts present tense ('em chamada'), a static timer that never moves and animated bars; the sheet shows 'Política v2.4', 'John Miller', 'Aguardando sua decisão'. Landmark list: two complementary regions (.p7-drawer, .lc-sheet) have no accessible name while .iv-memory does.

**Why:** Principle 3: 'Todo mockup carrega rótulo visível de exemplo — aria-label sozinho não resolve'; brand p.24 forbids invented states. Jordan cannot tell replay from live; Rafael reads a frozen timer as fake. Fidelity rated P1, IA and a11y P2 — P2 kept (labelling gap rather than a legibility/conversion break; majority of lenses).

**Fix:** In `src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx` `.lc-idsub` (line 354): keep `<i className="lc-en">EN</i>`, and add a sibling `<i className="lc-en lc-ex">Chamada · Exemplo</i>` (verbatim reuse of the existing desktop string; not uppercase, no extra tracking — measured 125.2px at 10px/800, fits the 138.9px column at 360, 153.9 at 375, 160.1 at 390). In the `@media (max-width:767px)` block of index.html: `.lc-idsub{flex-wrap:wrap;row-gap:4px}` so the chip drops to its own line under EN when space is short (head has min-height:66px, so it grows to ~92px without clipping), and move the existing `.lc-wf{display:none}` from the <=359 rule (index.html:554) up into the <=767 block — the waveform is aria-hidden decoration, this removes the 'live bars' reading and also fixes the pre-existing 360-374 overlap of `.lc-wf` on `.lc-htime`. Keep `.lc-htime` '00:12' (string stays; desktop keeps `.p7-wf` untouched). Do NOT place the chip in the header row next to `.lc-htime` and do not use `.lg-badge` (157-160px) — neither fits. Accessibility: add `aria-label="Resumo em português"` to both `<aside className="p7-drawer">` (jsx:336) and `<aside className="lc-sheet ...">` (jsx:384) (existing heading text; only one is rendered at a time, so no duplicate-landmark noise). Zero new copy, no orange/teal added, Archivo label per type system.

**Verifier:** Core claim CONFIRMED against source and a live 390x844 measurement (worktree served locally; :4174 mirror was down): at <=767 `.p7-cq` is display:none (index.html:501) so the only 'Chamada · Exemplo' label (ligou-app9.jsx:293) never renders on mobile; the shell header (.lc-head, jsx:352-357) shows 'Ligou em chamada' + EN pill + waveform + static '00:12'; `.p7-langnote` (13px, y 2025-2065) is the only visible 'exemplo' cue and is off-screen when `.lc-apr` (y 3100) is in view. `.iv-memory` has aria-label, `.p7-drawer` (jsx:336) and `.lc-sheet` (jsx:384) do not. Principle 3 in .impeccable.md literally requires a visible example label ('aria-label sozinho não resolve'), and brand p.24 'Não inventar estados' is quoted correctly. Two parts of the evidence are WRONG: (1) the bars are not animated — `.lc-wf` is a static SVG of <rect>s, computed animationName 'none' on svg and rects, no CSS/JS touches it (the `wfb` keyframe applies only to `.wf i`); (2) the two unnamed asides are never exposed together — one lives in `.p7-cq`, the other in `.p7m`, and exactly one is display:none at any width, so a screen-reader user meets one unnamed region, not two. The FIX is not implementable as written: the header row has 8.8px of slack at 390 (`.lc-id` right 248.1, `.lc-htime` left 267.9), while 'Chamada · Exemplo' renders at 157.5-159.5px as `.lg-badge` and 125.2-139.1px in `.lc-en` style (measured), so 'replacing/preceding .lc-htime' would crush the 128.5px 'Ligou em chamada' title; at 375 the column is already 153.9px and at 360 it is 138.9px with the waveform overlapping the timer by ~10px (pre-existing, because the hide rule starts at <=359). The `.wf--off` suggestion targets a different component (`.wf i` divs), not `.lc-wf`. P2 stands: it is a labelling/brand-principle gap, not a legibility or conversion break; the P1 fidelity sub-rating was inflated.


### L16 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Desktop board is a static poster — no motion, all three timestamps at once, a fake replay control — and the desktop hero CTA lands here

**Where:** ligou-app9.jsx:290-349 (.p7-board), :298 (.p7-again span), :300-309 (.p7-trail static), :295 (static waveform SVG), :297 ('00:12'); index.html:429-491

Anchor jump to #prova puts the board top at ~387px; the drawer's decision row (y 2426) is 35px below the 900px fold.

**Why:** Brand motion page: the orange line connects and cards organize; here nothing connects anything — a screenshot of a conversation. Mobile (once L04 lands) tells a sequence; desktop visitors get the weaker proof plus a control that pretends to replay it.

**Fix:** Desktop board staging, mirroring the mobile console's real cadence. (a) In CallDemo (src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:281-349) add `const [bref, bon] = useInView({threshold:.35})` (useInView is exposed on window by ligou-fx2.js:212) and put `ref={bref}` + `className={'p7-board' + (bon ? ' on' : '')}` on .p7-board; move `key={mrun}` from .p7m (:350) up to .p7-cq (:290) so both boards re-key. (b) Add a step class `p7-i` with `style={{'--lcd': ...}}` to .p7-b1 (0s), .p7-c1 (.45s), .p7-b2 (.9s), .p7-c2 (1.35s), .p7-b3 (1.8s), .p7-c3 (2.25s), .p7-drawer (2.7s) — same .45s stride as .lc-i (index.html:506); CSS: `.p7-i{opacity:0}` `.p7-board.on .p7-i{animation:lcin .2s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1) var(--lcd,0s) both}` (reuse @keyframes lcin :507). (c) Draw only the solid teal path (jsx:304, stroke #7fd0c2 — keep teal, do not recolor orange): add `pathLength="1"` and CSS `.p7-trail path:not([stroke-dasharray]){stroke-dasharray:1;stroke-dashoffset:1;transition:stroke-dashoffset 1.6s var(--ease-out) .6s}` `.p7-board.on .p7-trail path:not([stroke-dasharray]){stroke-dashoffset:0}` (same technique as .hero-wave :64-65); leave the three gray dashed connectors static or fade them with opacity alongside their card step. (d) Convert .p7-again (jsx:298) to `<button className="p7-again" type="button" onClick={() => { setMrel(false); setMrun(m => m + 1); }}>` keeping the visible text 'Ver de novo' (no aria-label needed); add `cursor:pointer;font:inherit` to .p7-again (index.html:437) and a :focus-visible ring like .lc-rel (:542). (e) Reduced-motion/no-JS: append `.p7-i{opacity:1;animation:none}` and `.p7-trail path{stroke-dashoffset:0}` to the existing `@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)` block (index.html:230-236) — and since the static board is the complete state, no noscript change is needed. (f) Optional, to bring the decision row (.p7-dbtns, vTop 935) above the 900 fold after the CTA jump: trim `.p7{padding:54px 0 62px}` (:422) to 36px top and `.p7-cq{margin-top:42px}` (:427) to 24px (−36px total → buttons at ~899); or accept it, since 'Decisão de exceção' (vTop 819) already shows. Keep '00:12', the waveform and all strings unchanged.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and reproduced on the local mirror at 1440x900 (anchor #prova): document.getAnimations() inside .p7-board = 0; .p7-again is a SPAN, cursor auto, no handler (index.html:437 has no cursor/animation rule; ligou-app9.jsx:298 renders a <span>); .p7-b1/.p7-b2/.p7-b3 stamped 00:02/00:06/00:10 render at once; .p7-trail is 4 static paths; board vTop 383, .p7-dbtns vTop 935 (35px under the 900 fold) — matches the finding exactly. Both desktop CTAs (nav :78 and hero :186) href=#prova, so the primary action really lands on this static board with a dead 'Ver de novo' pill; brand guide p.22 ('Movimento que explica… a linha conecta, cards organizam') supports the direction. Severity P2 is correct (content complete, labelled 'Chamada · Exemplo', desktop is the secondary device; not P1). Adjustments needed: (1) the 'why' says mobile only tells a sequence 'once L04 lands' — false, .p7m already stages via .lc-i with --lcd 0→2.85s and a .2s lcin animation (index.html:506-507, jsx:352-383), so the whole mobile run is ~3s, not '~6s cadence'; (2) the stroke-dashoffset/pathLength=1 draw only works on the solid teal path (#7fd0c2); the three gray connectors already carry strokeDasharray='3 7' (jsx:301-303) and dashoffset-drawing would scramble their dash pattern; (3) the fix omits the mandatory prefers-reduced-motion/no-JS fallback (Principle 4, brand p.22; existing pattern index.html:230-236 and :559); (4) the fallback branch 'remove .p7-again, 00:12 and the waveform' deletes the strings 'Ver de novo' and '00:12', violating KEEP THE COPY — drop it; (5) the trail must stay teal (teal = guarantee/confirmation; Principle 1 caps large orange at three places), even though the 'why' cites the orange brand line.


### L17 · P3 · ADJUSTED — Dark tail: Preço + CTA + Footer stack into 2.4 viewports of continuous dark, so the final 'Ligou? Atendido.' has no pause to land on; dark ≈37–42% of the page

**Where:** ligou-app9.jsx:468 (#preco background var(--surface-inverse)), index.html:156 (.cta var(--surface-inverse-deep)), jsx:516 (footer), :534 (.lc-sheet #082f3d), :246 (.hero4), :335 (.iv dark half), :247 (sticky .nav4)

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** Mobile: Preço 7922→9162 (ink-900), CTA 9162→9592 (ink-950), Footer 9592→9911 → 1989px dark = 2.36 viewports; desktop 5763→7889 = 2126px = 2.36 viewports; the only seam is #082f3d → #041d27. Page-wide dark share on mobile ≈37–43% (hero 912–981 + iv dark ≈800–1250 + tail 1989 + lc-sheet ≈450 of 9911); opening = splash + 69px nav + 912px hero fully dark. Contract: 'Tema claro… seções escuras como pausa e ênfase, não como default'; brand p.13: 'Seções escuras criam ênfase; não dominam'. The v9 override approves the dark hero, not a dark price block.

**Why:** Daylight legibility is the stated reason for the light theme; the decision moment (price → final CTA) is read against glare and the dark bookend stops being a pause because it follows 1240px of dark already.

**Fix:** Pricing surface only, edit the inline styles in /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx (CSS selectors alone cannot override them): (1) line 468 section style -> background:'var(--surface-sunken)', color:'var(--text-body)'. (2) line 470: replace <span className="pt-cap__label">Preço</span> with the DS <Eyebrow>Preço</Eyebrow> (.lg-eyebrow = teal-700 on paper), or add index.html rule `#preco .pt-cap__label{color:var(--lg-teal-700)}` (teal-400 on paper is ~1.9:1). (3) ghost card (desktop only): label color 'var(--text-inverse-secondary)' -> 'var(--text-secondary)'; $499 color 'var(--text-inverse)' -> 'var(--lg-ink-950)'; /mês and p -> 'var(--text-secondary)'; index.html:152 .pricecard--ghost{border:1px solid var(--border-strong);color:var(--text-body)}. (4) 'Plano mês a mês. Sem fidelidade.' paragraph: color 'var(--text-inverse-secondary)' -> 'var(--text-secondary)'. Leave the white .lg-card, orange Badge and 'Quero aproveitar a oferta' button, the dark .cta 'Ligou? Atendido.' fold and the footer untouched; the CTA then becomes the single dark closing fold. Drop the .nav4.scrolled light-bar change from this item; if wanted later, gate it on leaving #top (IntersectionObserver), not scrollY>12, and also restyle .nav4-client border and the mark head.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and reproduces from the measured geometry and source: mobile Preço 7922+1240, CTA 9162+430, Footer 9592+320 = 1990px contiguous dark = 2.36 viewports; desktop 1061+638+427 = 2126px = 2.36 viewports. The only seam is ink-900 (#082f3d, inline style at ligou-app9.jsx:468 via --surface-inverse) to ink-950 (#041d27, .cta index.html:156 and footer jsx:516), effectively invisible. Page-wide dark share ~40% is plausible (hero 912 + .iv 180deg gradient dark until ~36-46% of 2232px + .lc-sheet #082f3d + tail 1990). Contract quotes are accurate (.impeccable.md 'Tema: claro... seções escuras como pausa e ênfase, não como default'; brand guide p.13 'Seções escuras criam ênfase; não dominam'), and docs/CHANGELOG-CLAUDE-V9.md registers only the dark hero, not a dark price block. But the 'why' is overstated: the decision content (Badge, $299, inclusions, orange 'Quero aproveitar a oferta', and on mobile the $499 line via .price-mobile-normal) already sits inside the white .lg-card; the ghost $499 card is display:none!important at <=767px (index.html:498). Only the eyebrow, h2, ROI sentence and 'Plano mês a mês' are paper-on-ink, at 34px/21px/14px with ~13:1 contrast, so daylight legibility of the price decision is not materially harmed. The real defect is rhythm/contract fidelity (no pause before the frozen closing fold), which is polish-tier for this user, so P3 rather than P2. The fix is also not implementable as written: #preco background/color and the ghost-card text colors and 'Plano mês a mês' color are inline JSX styles, so the proposed CSS selectors would lose to them (and the 'Plano mês a mês' line, omitted by the finding, would render rgba(251,252,248,.72) on paper = invisible). The nav portion is scope creep with a side effect: .nav4.scrolled fires at scrollY>12, so a light bar would float over the frozen dark hero for the first ~840px and needs .nav4-client border and the AgentNodeMark #0d2b3f head handled too.


### L18 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Desktop 96° gradient parks '03 Em operação' (and the tail of col 2's line) on the mid-tone: 3.1–3.4:1 at 13px

**Where:** index.html:335 (.iv background:linear-gradient(96deg,#041a2b 0%,#062231 40%,#40595a 54%,#d9decf 70%,#f6f7f0 84%)), :346 (.iv-col--3 .iv-lab{color:#0b655a}), :345 (.iv-line rgba(251,252,248,.92))

**Viewports:** 1300x900, 1440x900

**Evidence:** Canvas replica of the exact gradient sampled at the text's Range rect: .iv-col--3 .iv-lab starts at x=67.3% where bg = rgb(167,178,169) → 3.17:1 at 1440, 3.09:1 at 1300 ('03' numeral 3.09–3.42:1); .iv-col--2 .iv-line (16.5px paper) ends at 59.4% on rgb(98,118,116) → 4.23:1 at 1300 (5.34:1 at 1440). Screenshot at 1300 (translateY -2833) shows the '03' label in the sage band. Layout lens estimated ≈#a5b1a7 / 3.2:1 from stops + rects; a11y pixel-sampled it — same result.

**Why:** Both fail AA (4.5:1) in the 1025–1400px band most laptops use; the step numerals are the wayfinding for the frozen three-step chain. Same root cause as L03 (percentage ramp under text) on a different element/viewport.

**Fix:** index.html:346 — change `.iv-col--3 .iv-lab{color:#0b655a}` to `.iv-col--3 .iv-lab{color:var(--lg-ink-950)}` (one existing selector, no layout change; verified >=7.27:1 from 1100 to 1440 at the "03" start, >=13:1 at the word's end). Do NOT adopt the proposed gradient shift (96deg 38/50/62/76% puts col-2's .iv-line tail on rgb(162,174,165) = 2.23:1 at 1300) nor `.iv-col--2 .iv-line{max-width:24ch}` (wraps the line, drops col-2's card top ~20px vs cols 1/3 and misaligns the orange connectors/.iv-node). Leave .iv line 335 as is. The residual col-2 tail issue (rgba(251,252,248,.92) at 3.08-3.78:1 only at 1025-1240px; passes at >=1300) cannot be solved by re-tuning percentage stops because its end point slides to 64.5% while col 3 starts at 67.4%; hand it to L03's hard-seam replacement (light zone starting as an edge at the col-3 track) rather than patching here.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and reproduced exactly. Served the byte-identical worktree (/Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI, node dev-server.mjs on 127.0.0.1:4174) in the internal Browser pane and replayed the computed backgroundImage `linear-gradient(96deg, rgb(4,26,43) 0%, rgb(6,34,49) 40%, rgb(64,89,90) 54%, rgb(217,222,207) 70%, rgb(246,247,240) 84%)` on a canvas sized to the real #diferenca rect, sampling at the real .iv-col--3 .iv-lab rect (Container maxWidth 1500, padding 0 32px, 3x1fr grid gap 30 puts col 3 at 67.3-67.6% at every desktop width). Results for #0b655a (rgb 11,101,90; 13px/800 Familjen = not WCAG large text): 1440x900 bg rgb(167,178,169) 3.17:1 (numeral end 3.58:1); 1366x768 3.17:1; 1300x900 bg rgb(165,177,167) 3.12:1; 1200 3.02:1; 1100 2.91:1. The word's tail reaches near-white (6.44:1), so only "03" and the first letters sit on the mid-tone. So the headline claim holds and is not limited to 1025-1400: it fails at every 3-column width including 1440. Two corrections: (a) the col-2 tail claim is overstated at 1300 — rgba(251,252,248,.92) at the Range end (59.7%) measured 4.56:1 (passes AA narrowly; the finding's 4.23 comes from a slightly different sample point); it does fail below ~1240 (1200: 61.9% -> 3.78:1; 1100: 64.5% -> 3.08:1). (b) The primary FIX is wrong: replaying the proposed stops (96deg 38/50/62/76%) at 1300 lifts "03" to rgb(222,226,213) = 5.27:1 but drops col-2's tail onto rgb(162,174,165) = 2.23:1, a new and worse AA failure; the compensating `max-width:24ch` (~209px at 16.5px) wraps "Suas respostas viram regras de operação." into two lines, which pushes col 2's card top ~20px below cols 1 and 3 and misaligns the orange ::before connectors (top:46%) and .iv-node (top:42%) across the row. The 90deg alternative (tested 40/60/66/84) fixes "03" (5.2:1) but fails col-2's tail at 1200 (4.11) and 1100 (1.95) because that tail migrates from 57% to 64.5% while col 3 stays at 67.4% — no percentage ramp can satisfy both across the band. The finding's own second alternative (recolor the 03 label to ink-950) is the correct fix: measured 7.92 (1440/1366), 7.81 (1300), 7.55 (1200), 7.27 (1100) at the numeral start, rising past 13:1 to the right. Why: real but secondary for this persona (phone user gets the 180deg mobile gradient at <=1024, a separate finding); on a laptop it is a legibility annoyance on a 2-character wayfinding numeral, not a conversion blocker — P2 stands, not higher.


### L20 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Sticky 'Falar com o Ligou' runs at 11.5–12px on phones — the smallest button text on the page for the primary action

**Where:** index.html:205 (.nav .lg-btn 12.5px ≤600), :207 (.nav4-cta .lg-btn font-size:12px!important ≤600), :332 (11.5px!important ≤399), :326 (.nav4-client 12px); jsx:78 size="sm" (components.css:17 .lg-btn--sm 13.5px)

**Evidence:** Measured at 390: nav CTA 11.5px/600 in a 113x44 pill; 'Área' 12px/700; logo 19px + 28px mark. Body is 16px, hero CTA 17px, trust line 13px — the repeat of the voice action is 72% of body size and smaller than its secondary neighbour. Width budget at 390: 390 − 28 padding − ~87 logo − 10 − 47 Área − 6 ≈ 212px; 13.5px text needs ≈123–144px. The ≤399 rule bites the modal iPhone width.

**Why:** Principle 5: the header repeats the voice action so it is available at every scroll position; on a phone in sunlight an 11.5px label on a 113px pill is a colour blob, not a command, though there is room.

**Fix:** In /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/index.html remove only the font-size declarations, keeping the padding: line 205 `.nav .lg-btn{padding:9px 13px;font-size:12.5px}` → `.nav .lg-btn{padding:9px 13px}`; line 207 `.nav4-cta .lg-btn{padding-left:11px!important;padding-right:11px!important;font-size:12px!important}` → drop `font-size:12px!important`; line 332 `.nav4-cta .lg-btn{padding-left:9px!important;padding-right:9px!important;font-size:11.5px!important}` → drop `font-size:11.5px!important` (optionally raise the ≤399 side padding to 11px; still fits at 320 with 24px spare). Result: .lg-btn--sm 13.5px applies at every width; min-height 44px from :257/:301 is unchanged. Do NOT move the .nav4-client-long/-short swap out of the ≤399 block — measured widths show 'Área do cliente' + 13.5px CTA fits at 400–600 (58px free at 414), and moving it would change the visible label on those phones. No JSX or string changes.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and the cascade produces it: components.css:17 sets .lg-btn--sm 13.5px; index.html:205 (.nav .lg-btn 12.5px, inside @media ≤600 at :190), :207 (.nav4-cta .lg-btn 12px!important, same block) and :332 (11.5px!important inside @media ≤399 at :328) override it, and 390 < 399 so the 11.5px rule applies at the iPhone width. Measured live at 390 on the byte-identical mirror: CTA 113x44 at 11.5px/600 Archivo, 'Área' 47x44 at 12px/700, logo 81.5px, nav-links display:none, container padding 14px, gaps 10/6px — matching the brief's numbers. It is the smallest .lg-btn on the page (other Buttons: hero 17px, pricing 15px, final CTA 15.5px). Principle 5 in .impeccable.md literally says the header repeats the same voice action, so the 'why' is grounded. Contrast ink-950 on orange-500 computes to ~5.6:1 (passes AA for small text), so the 'colour blob' phrasing overstates it — the label is legible, just undersized relative to body 16px and its secondary neighbour — which keeps this at P2, not P1. Simulating the fix in-page (font-size 13.5px, padding 11px) gives a 133x44 pill with no overflow at 390 (94px free), 360 (64px free), 320 (24px free), and at 414/600 with the long 'Área do cliente' label still shown (58px/244px free). Therefore the second half of the proposed fix — moving the 'Área do cliente' → 'Área' swap from ≤399 to ≤600 — is unnecessary for width and would change the visible label on 400–600px phones for no reason, brushing against 'KEEP THE COPY'. Adjusted fix drops that part and narrows the CSS edit to the font-size declarations only so the padding overrides stay.


### L21 · P2 · CONFIRMED — Anchor targets have no scroll-margin above 767px: #prova, #faq, #comecar headings land under the 69px sticky nav

**Where:** index.html:497 (#diferenca,#prova{scroll-margin-top:86px} only inside @media (max-width:767px)); ligou-app9.jsx:71 (nav links #prova/#diferenca/#faq/#preco), :457 (#faq), :422 (.p7 padding-top 54px)

**Viewports:** 1440x900, 1024x768

**Evidence:** Desktop 'Controle' → #faq: h2 'O que todo dono pergunta.' top is 65px below the section top, under the 69px nav (screenshot: heading clipped, eyebrow hidden); #comecar h2 offset 66; #faq/#preco/#comecar have scroll-margin 0 at every width; #prova's eyebrow dash at 54px sits under the nav on every desktop 'Falar com o Ligou'.

**Why:** Anchor jumps that hide the heading make the nav feel broken — on the one desktop nav that exists and on every hero CTA click.

**Fix:** #prova,#diferenca,#comecar,#faq,#preco{scroll-margin-top:calc(69px + 24px)} at all widths (replace the mobile-only rule at :497).

**Verifier:** Source check (worktree index.html / ligou-app9.jsx / _ds tokens) reproduces the defect. .nav is position:sticky;top:0;z-index:40 with a 68px Container + 1px border = 69px (matches landing-geometry navH 69). There is no html{scroll-padding-top} anywhere (index.html, DS styles.css, components.css) and the only scroll-margin rule is index.html:497 `#diferenca,#prova{scroll-margin-top:86px}` inside @media (max-width:767px). Desktop nav links (visible only >920px, line 71) target #prova/#diferenca/#faq/#preco; hero and final CTAs target #prova. Computed landing at 1440 for #faq (line 457): section top at y=0 under the nav; Container has 0 top padding; SectionHead is a flex column gap 14 with an eyebrow line box (~25px: 12px inline-flex .eyebrow-mark inside a 16px/1.55 line) then the h2 → h2 box top ≈ 39-41px, 48px display type at line-height 1.04 → the top ~28px of the heading box (eyebrow fully, upper part of the glyphs) sits under the 69px nav. The finding's '65px' is the pre-reveal number (.rv{transform:translateY(26px)} before .on fires), so after the 0.7s reveal the heading settles even further under the nav — the defect is slightly understated, not inflated. #prova: .p7 padding-top 54 → eyebrow dash ~y60-66 hidden, .p7-title top ≈ 93 (54+25+14) visible with 24px clearance — matches 'eyebrow dash under nav' exactly. #preco: .price-ct padding-top 64!important → 'Preço' label 64-77 partly under the nav, h2 at ~95 visible. #diferenca: .iv padding clamp(64px,8vw,104px)=104 at 1440, 82 at 1024 → fine even without margin. Mobile (≤767) is already covered by the 86px rule, and nav links are hidden <920px, so the user on a phone is not hit; the desktop (RJ's review surface, 'Controle' → FAQ) is. Severity P2 (annoyance, not blocking) is right. Fix respects every constraint: pure CSS scroll offset, no string touched, no frozen fold or palette involved, selectors exist. Minor overstatement: nothing on the page links to #comecar (grep: no href="#comecar"), so it only matters for direct URL hashes — harmless to include.


### L22 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Everything is client-rendered: no-JS path drops the second fold and the price and tells a different story; a bundle exception yields a blank page

**Where:** index.html:560 (#root:empty{display:none}), :561 noscript, :580-605 (fallback: hero + 4-step proof + client link only), :586 vs jsx:184 (different lede), :587 ('Ver demonstração ilustrativa' exists only here); ligou-app9.jsx:561 createRoot

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** No-JS render (noscript injected): 2 sections, docH 1711 at 390; hasScenes false, hasFaq false, hasPrice false — none of Conversa → Suas regras → Em operação, A dor, O que ele faz, Como começar, FAQ, Preço, footer. Contract Principle 4: 'As duas provas aparecem em sequência sem JS; as três cenas operacionais ficam empilhadas'. With scripting on, a failure in _ds_bundle.js/app9.js leaves #root empty → display:none → white screen, and <noscript> does not render. On slow connections the page is blank paper until three scripts run, then the 2.45s intro starts.

**Why:** 'Tela em branco é o pior bug possível numa landing'; the current fallback covers only disabled-JS, not failed-JS, and its lede/CTA strings diverge from the page.

**Fix:** Keep the existing noscript strings untouched (they are pinned by tests/frontend-contract.test.mjs:13-21). Two additive changes in index.html only: (1) Inside <noscript> after #nojs-proof, add a third section `.nojs-v9__proof` reusing the `.nojs-v9__flow`/`.nojs-v9__step` grid (grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr) desktop, 1fr <768) with the three scene headings from ligou-app9.jsx Scene() — 'Conversa', 'Suas regras', 'Em operação' — and their existing row copy verbatim (e.g. 'Serviços — Instalação, manutenção e reparos.', 'Cidades atendidas — San Francisco, Los Angeles, San Diego e região.', 'Emergências — 24h para vazamentos e falta de água.'), keeping the 'Exemplo visual' label above (mockups labelled, no invented state); then a fourth section with the Pricing strings verbatim from jsx:471/476/478/482 ('Contrate até 31 de dezembro de 2026 por $299/mês.', '$299' + '/mês', 'Ativação isenta. Quem contratar…', 'Para novas assinaturas após a oferta: $499/mês + ativação de $499.'), and the footer 'Área do cliente' is already covered (test expects exactly one /dashboard/ link in noscript — do not add another). Also add a 'Como começar' step list from Comecar() if the list is short; no new copy. (2) Failed-JS fallback without duplicating markup or touching the noscript contract: add before the three body <script src> tags an inline script `window.addEventListener('error',function(){var r=document.getElementById('root');if(r&&!r.firstChild){var n=document.querySelector('noscript');if(n)r.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',n.textContent);}},true);` — with scripting enabled the parser stores <noscript> children as text, so textContent is the fallback HTML; capture:true catches both <script> load errors and uncaught runtime exceptions, so a 404 on _ds_bundle.js or a TypeError at app9.js:7 renders the same fallback instead of a white page. Optionally add `defer` to the two vendor React scripts in <head> (index.html:16-17) so the DS stylesheet and body parse are not blocked by ~170 KB of JS; leave the Intro4 overlay and .play gating as they are. Re-run `bun test tests/frontend-contract.test.mjs`; the noscript regex still matches and the pinned strings are unchanged.

**Verifier:** Evidence checks out against the worktree source, but the fix is mostly wrong and the failure mechanics are partly misattributed. REAL: (1) index.html:580-605 <noscript> contains exactly two sections (hero + 4-step proof); no Conversa/Suas regras/Em operação, Dor, Faz, Comecar, FAQ, Pricing or Footer — .impeccable.md Principle 4 ('as três cenas operacionais ficam empilhadas' sem JS) is not met, and docs/CHANGELOG-DEMO-HERO-RESTORE.md:81 shows an earlier build did keep 'hero, prova e três cenas' without JS, so v9's 'fallback editorial' (CHANGELOG-CLAUDE-V9.md:42) is a regression. (2) Lede divergence is literal: index.html:586 'Seu cliente liga em inglês, espanhol ou português…' vs jsx:184 'Seu cliente liga. O Ligou consulta…'; 'Ver demonstração ilustrativa' (index.html:587) exists only in noscript. (3) Failed-JS blank is real: React 18.3.1 UMD loads from <head> (index.html:16-17), then _ds_bundle.js/fx2/app9 run synchronously at body end; assets/js/ligou-app9.js:6-7 destructures window.LigouDesignSystem_a33905, so a failed _ds_bundle throws a TypeError before createRoot, #root stays empty, <noscript> never renders, no error boundary exists → white page. First paint is also blank until the head scripts finish (body holds only an empty #root), then the 2.45s intro (app9.js:46-48). OVERSTATED: '#root:empty{display:none}' (index.html:560) is not causal — an empty div has zero height regardless; built JS contains no ?. / ?? so old-browser SyntaxError blank is not a live path; JS-disabled phones are a fraction of a percent, so the daily user impact is the rare bundle failure, not the noscript path — P2 stands, not higher. FIX PROBLEMS: (a) renderToString+hydrateRoot is not implementable as a surgical change — Hero output depends on matchMedia bands at render (jsx:120-155, `band !== 'mid' ? art : null`, mobile-only ghost CTA), so a viewport-less prerender guarantees hydration mismatches, and the build is a bare Bun.Transpiler pass (scripts/build-claude-v9.mjs) with vendored UMD React, no SSR pipeline; prerendered content would also flash before the Intro4 overlay mounts, altering the frozen dark intro. (b) The 'html.js .hd/.ln/.rv' guard is moot: those classes exist only inside React-rendered DOM; if the bundle throws before mount there is nothing to guard, and if it throws during render/effects React 18 unmounts the whole root anyway. (c) Rewriting the noscript CTA to 'Falar com o Ligou' and the lede to the page string changes copy and breaks tests/frontend-contract.test.mjs:15, which pins `href="#nojs-proof">Ver demonstração ilustrativa</a>` verbatim. (d) Extending noscript with the three scenes + price using the page's exact strings is valid and passes the test (line 36 only forbids 'A dor'/'Prova do produto' eyebrows).


### L23 · P2 · ADJUSTED — No in-page wayfinding on a 9911px mobile page: section links hidden below 920px

Header grows from 69px to ~104px, so change line 497 to `#top,#prova,#diferenca,#comecar,#faq,#preco{scroll-margin-top:112px}` and scope it to <=920px (this also resolves L21). Severity P2 is correct (primary action #prova stays one tap away; this hurts secondary intent). The 'why' overstates distance (7922/844 = ~9.4 viewports, not 20 screens). The FIX is not implementable as cited: the <nav> is a child of a flex-row Container with inline height:68, gap:24, marginLeft:'auto', so .nav-links{display:flex;overflow-x:auto} alone jams the chips into the 68px row between logo and actions instead of under the bar; .lg-tabs is a light --surface-sunken button/aria-selected tab tray and is wrong on the dark nav4; 'Preço next to Área' does not fit at 320-360px and adds a third pill beside the single orange primary; Comecar cards as anchors (y 6172) do not reach price; the taller sticky header also breaks the 86px scroll-margin-top at line 497, which only covers #diferenca/#prova.


### L24 · P2 · CONFIRMED — Footer 'Termos' and 'Privacidade' are href="#": they smooth-scroll the reader back to the hero

**Where:** ligou-app9.jsx:525-526 <a href="#">Termos</a> / <a href="#">Privacidade</a>; index.html:19 html{scroll-behavior:smooth}

**Viewports:** 390x844, 1440x900

**Evidence:** href="#" resolves to document top from y ≈9600 on mobile; both are announced as links (46–108 x 21.7px, see L65).

**Why:** The skeptic checks Terms/Privacy last as a legitimacy test; a dead link that throws him to the hero reads as an unfinished site.

**Fix:** Until the pages exist render them as <span> with the same text in var(--text-inverse-secondary), or point both to mailto:suporte@ligou.ai. No new copy.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real in the worktree that the local mirror serves byte-identically (md5 c4e9a435... for both). /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/src/runtime/ligou-app9.jsx:525-526 are literally `<a href="#" style={a}>Termos</a>` and `<a href="#" style={a}>Privacidade</a>`; index.html:19 is `html{scroll-behavior:smooth}` and the prefers-reduced-motion block (index.html:230-240) does not reset it. No onClick/preventDefault/hashchange handler exists anywhere in ligou-app9.jsx or assets/js/ligou-fx2.js, so the empty fragment performs a native smooth scroll to document top. landing-geometry.json puts the mobile Footer at y=9592 (finding says ≈9600) and desktop Footer at y=7462; the link box 21.7px tall = 14px font × --leading-body 1.55 (tokens/typography.css:4), so the L65 sizing is consistent. No /termos or /privacidade page exists in the repo (find over root, dist, dashboard) and there is no vercel.json rewrite, so the links are genuinely dead. The 'why' holds and is reinforced by the brand's own rule 3 ('Nada de estado inventado', .impeccable.md:120): a link that pretends legal pages exist is an invented state. Severity P2 (annoyance) is correct, not inflated: it does not block the demo CTA but it teleports a reader who reached the bottom of a ~9.9k px page back to the hero and reads as unfinished. Fix keeps every string, touches only the Footer, and the inline style object `a` already carries var(--text-inverse-secondary) + text-decoration:none, so a <span style={a}> is visually identical. Only refinement: drop the mailto alternative, because pointing 'Termos' at suporte@ligou.ai is itself a mislabelled affordance (the email link already exists one item to the left).

## Not verified (lower-ranked; treat as leads)

- **L25** (P2) Reveal-on-scroll blanks the viewport on fast landings: 30–42 wrappers at threshold .18, 26px/.7s plus staggers — _fix:_ In useInView for Reveal use rootMargin '0px 0px -8% 0px', threshold 0; shorten to .45s and translateY 14px; cap delays at 120ms; skip the delay for elements already intersecting on mount; wrap Scene's mobile columns in a single Reveal (the mobile ? <Reveal>… pattern Faz/Comecar/Faq use); one Reveal per SectionHead.
- **L26** (P2) prefers-reduced-motion block disables 8–10 dead selectors while leaving live motion on (smooth scroll, FAQ, nav, :active scales) and turns the replay button into a silent no-op — _fix:_ Inside the media block add html{scroll-behavior:auto} .faq-a,.pm,.nav,.lg-btn,.h4-ghostbtn,.iv-approve{transition:none} .lg-btn:active,.h4-ghostbtn:active,.iv-approve:active{transform:none} .lc-again{display:none} (or give .lc-again static pressed feedback); prune the dead selectors so the block documents what actually animates.
- **L27** (P2) Auto-playing hero loop, two marquees, 14 waveform bars and a 7s bob run with no in-page pause (WCAG 2.2.2) and keep running thousands of px off-screen — _fix:_ One 44x44 icon button anchored to .hero4-artlayer (bottom-right, aria-pressed, same glyph style as .lc-again) that calls video.pause()/play() and toggles .motion-off on <main> with .motion-off .mq__track,.motion-off .wf i,.motion-off .floaty{animation-play-state:paused}; persist in sessionStorage; hide under prefers-reduced-motion. Pause everything not in view via useInView({once:false}) toggling 
- **L28** (P2) Focus ring on accent buttons over light surfaces is 1.5:1; interactive elements use four different focus styles — _fix:_ --focus-ring:var(--lg-teal-700) (#0b655a, 6.7:1 on paper) plus a paper halo for dark surfaces: .lg-btn:focus-visible,.faq-q:focus-visible,.lc-again:focus-visible,.lc-rel:focus-visible,.lc-apr:focus-visible,.lc-adj:focus-visible,.iv-approve:focus-visible,.footer-links a:focus-visible,.nav-link:focus-visible{outline:3px solid var(--focus-ring);outline-offset:2px;box-shadow:0 0 0 6px var(--lg-paper)}
- **L29** (P2) FAQ collapsed answers stay in the accessibility tree; toggles lack aria-controls — _fix:_ <div className="faq-a" id={`faq-a-${i}`} aria-hidden={open!==i}> + aria-controls on the button; add .faq-item:not(.open) .faq-a{visibility:hidden;transition:max-height .4s var(--ease-out),visibility 0s .4s} so state and DOM agree after the animation (pairs with L62's grid-template-rows animation).
- **L30** (P2) Marquee ticker: 22 moving orange bullets under the hero CTA, 1.35–1.5:1 ghost words, a visible loop seam, and the honesty claims hidden on mobile and from AT — _fix:_ Keep both item lists, render static: .mq__track{animation:none;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;gap:10px 22px;width:auto}, .mq b{font:700 13px var(--font-body);letter-spacing:var(--track-caps);text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--text-secondary)}, .mq__dot{color:var(--lg-ink-300);font-size:13px}; drop items.concat(items) in ligou-fx2.js:134; on mobile render .mq-claims as a static wrapped .lg
- **L31** (P2) 'A diferença' cards show invented live/validated state and a fictional owner with no example label — _fix:_ Add the existing string 'Exemplo visual' (index.html:593) as a .lg-badge--outline chip top-right of .iv-card--dark and both .iv-card--light; set card 3 Waveform playing={false} (.wf--off); .iv-hang{background:var(--lg-ink-950)} so orange stays 'act'. Strings unchanged.
- **L32** (P2) Side-stripe borders (impeccable BAN 1): 3px orange on .iv-memory__copy, 2px teal on .lc-relato — _fix:_ .iv-memory__copy{border-left:0;padding-left:0} and put .iv-memory__approval on the DS eyebrow idiom (display:inline-flex;gap:10px + ::before{width:22px;height:2px;background:var(--accent)}) or border-top:1px solid var(--border-soft);padding-top:14px. .lc-relato{border-left:0;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;background:rgba(251,252,248,.06)}.
- **L33** (P2) Teal drift (8 off-token values) and a semantic collision: the customer's unresolved request is painted in the 'guarantee' colour — _fix:_ Customer bubbles → background:var(--surface-card);border:1px solid var(--border-soft); Ligou bubbles → background:var(--lg-teal-50). Map #7fc7b9,#7fd0c2→var(--lg-teal-200); #d9f2ec→var(--lg-teal-100); #e2f3ee→var(--lg-teal-50); #bfe4da→var(--lg-teal-200); #075f56,#0b655a→var(--lg-teal-700); .p7-bridge bg → rgba(11,101,90,.08).
- **L34** (P2) The connecting line is teal where the brand says the line is orange and the nodes teal; the motif appears as five disconnected fragments in two colours and two thicknesses — _fix:_ .p7-trail path stroke="var(--lg-orange-500)" strokeWidth="3" with node circles teal-700; .lc-evline{border-left:2px solid var(--lg-orange-500)} (a vertical connector, not a text stripe); .iv-dots{background-image:radial-gradient(circle,var(--lg-orange-500) 1.5px,transparent 1.6px)}; unify the eyebrow dash to one spec (22×3, --accent) and let it start the line in each section (e.g. width:44px); dem
- **L35** (P2) Buttons use two typefaces and six sizes; the demo's 'Aprovar' controls are set in the display face — _fix:_ .h4-ghostbtn,.iv-approve,.p7-apr,.p7-adj,.lc-apr,.lc-adj,.lc-rel,.faq-q{font-family:var(--font-body);font-weight:600}; sizes .lc-apr,.lc-adj,.iv-approve,.lc-rel{font-size:15px}; board buttons per L01's clamp; drop the 15.5px override at :202 so hero/final CTA stay 17px (.lg-btn--lg). Result: 13.5 (sm) / 15 (md) / 17 (lg), one face. Or replace custom controls with DS <Button> variants.
- **L36** (P2) Familjen Grotesk ships only at 600/700: every 'weight 800' headline renders 700, and body 440/750 resolve to 500/800 — the tokens describe a page that does not exist — _fix:_ typography.css:6 --weight-black:700; --weight-regular:500 (or ship Archivo as a variable font if 440 is wanted); index.html:425 .p7-bridge{font-weight:600}; cap label weight at 700 (L14) so display 700 stays the heaviest voice; optional html{font-synthesis:none}; remove the unused archivo-700.ttf duplicate and italic declarations from fonts.css.

---

# Dashboard — `#ligou` view


## Overall

The #ligou view is a faithful, disciplined build of the approved mock: the orange rail, the navy decision card, the safe-default approve flow and the composer mechanics are all specific to this product and documented in code. It fails at the one job the tab exists for: on a 390x844 phone at first paint the Aprovar/Ajustar/Recusar row is 100% under the fixed composer (row y 701-757 vs composer 701-757), and after the owner decides, the card silently becomes the next customer's under John's narrative with a fabricated "Pedido urgente · mesmo dia" kicker (a live defect, since the Supabase mapper never sets urgencyLabel). Around that core, the type system is not a system (11 sizes on the phone, causal chain at 7-8px, primary CTA text at 3.10:1) and a handful of states (focus, pressed, sending, resolved) were never finished. None of this needs a redesign; every fix is spacing, tokens, bindings and state modifiers on existing selectors, with all copy intact.


## AI-slop verdict

Not generic slop, but it inherited the mock's slop habits and shipped them at 1x: nine tracked-uppercase micro-labels between 7 and 10px on one screen, a causal strip whose type is smaller on a 390 phone than on a 320 one, ~11 near-identical petrol greys chosen per element, 40+ literal px font sizes bypassing the three tokens that exist, and a desktop that is the phone layout stretched into three columns with 13px body text and 286/458px voids. The verifier also refuted one slop accusation: the soft-orange tints (handoff, onboarding, pending icon) are a consistent "pendente" status grammar, not stray CTA orange, and the rail is brand continuity, not decoration. What keeps it out of slop territory is that the decisions that matter are deliberate: the operational line connects real events, the approve dialog defaults to "Somente este caso", the palette honours the p.17 contrast table everywhere except the one white-on-orange pair, and the prototype state is labelled on screen. The fix is finishing the system, not replacing the look.


## Nielsen heuristics — 23/50


| # | Heuristic | Score | Key issue |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Visibility of system status | 2 | 'Aguardando · sua decisão' is 8.3px (F04); after approving John the card reads Maria with an invented same-day kicker while the chain still narrates John (F02); the 'Respondendo…' signal is 9px and in Supabase mode is the only in-flight feedback (F18); the resolved node/icon stay action-orange (F19). |
| 2 | Match between system and the real world | 3 | Conversational pt-BR and real facts (17h, chaminé) match how the owner thinks; the three-column receipt strip and tracked uppercase kickers are dashboard idiom, not his. |
| 3 | User control and freedom | 2 | Cancel/Escape/Back work, but there is no undo after Aprovar, the resolved case vanishes from the timeline, and focus is handed to the next case's Aprovar (F07). |
| 4 | Consistency and standards | 2 | Rule lives in the strip on mobile and in the card on desktop; 11 rendered type sizes at 390 from separate declarations, 22 font-size values, 16 letter-spacings (F05); .approval-kicker 9px vs sibling .approvals-detail-kicker 10px (F16). The IA lens's 'handoff looks like a button' claim was refuted (it uses the file's status tint grammar) so that item is dropped, score unchanged. |
| 5 | Error prevention | 3 | Safe case-only default, Recusar confirmation and busy lock hold. Corrected from the IA lens: at first paint the composer text input sits where Aprovar should be (a mis-tap raises the keyboard) and the Voz pill covers Recusar's position (F01); a permanent rule can be saved without seeing the text that becomes the rule (F10). |
