| 6 | Recognition rather than recall | 2 | The approve sheet covers the card (case mode y 483-832, rule mode 319-832) and .approval-context is display:none on mobile, so approval.proposedAction, the string that is literally saved as the rule, is never shown in the dialog nor on the mobile card (F10). |
| 7 | Flexibility and efficiency of use | 2 | Default phone path is scroll ~150px -> tap -> sheet -> confirm; no jump from the status cell to the decision (F01 fallback); no programmatic path from the desktop chain to the inspector card (F21). |
| 8 | Aesthetic and minimalist design | 2 | Raised from the IA lens's 1: the 'orange on nine things' charge was partly refuted (rail = continuity, soft tints = status grammar) and the desktop voids were verified as P3 (F20). What stands: the same-day rule is stated three times on desktop, the heading block spends 115px vs the mock's 101 before the first bubble (F01), and 46% of the 1440 canvas is navy. |
| 9 | Help users recognize, diagnose, recover from errors | 3 | Raised from 2: the verifier showed the toast never covers the card or the newest message and the confirmation is duplicated inline as a .system-message; what remains is a top-anchored, motionless 4.2s toast over a static heading (F14, P3) and a failed draft restored to the field (good). |
| 10 | Help and documentation | 2 | Caption and inspector paragraph explain why approval is needed, but the inspector paragraph is 11px (F17) and the disabled attach button's explanation lives only in an aria-label Tab cannot reach. |

## Cognitive load — 6 failed

- 1 Single focus — at 390x844 first paint the eye lands on badge + h1 + rule + date + owner bubble; the decision row is at y 701-757 under the fixed composer (F01).
- 2 Chunking <=4 — five chunks (heading, owner question, agent summary, causal strip, agent prompt) precede the card; the heading block alone is 115px vs the mock's 101.
- 5 One thing at a time — the composer input and the teal Voz pill physically occupy the row where Aprovar and Recusar should be (F01); after approval the card becomes Maria's under John's story and focus lands on her Aprovar (F02, F07).
- 6 Minimal choices <=4 — 12 interactive choices at first paint (3 card + 3 composer + 5 nav + 1 profile); the bottom nav has 5 destinations, 2 of them locked in prototype.
- 7 Working memory — the approve sheet blurs and covers the four card facts; saving as a rule asks Escopo/Duração without echoing request, rule or proposedAction (F10).
- 8 Progressive disclosure — good inside the dialog (rule options on demand), but the page deletes the rule (.approval-context display:none) on mobile instead of disclosing it, leaving it only in the 8.3px strip cell (F04, F10).

## Working well (keep)

- The operational timeline is the brand made structural: 2px orange rail, hollow context node, filled approval node with elbow into the card, avatar masks over the rail — one grid that holds at 320-1440 with no horizontal overflow, and the strongest 'this is Ligou' device on screen.
- Dialog and decision mechanics are correct: 'Somente este caso' is the default, Escopo/Duração only appear on 'Salvar como regra', Recusar confirms, busy flag blocks double submit, Escape/backdrop/Back all close, focus returns or falls back to #main-content, body scroll locks, reduced-motion kill-switch zeroes every animation.
- Composer discipline: send and voice share one grid slot so the input width never changes while typing (189.69px measured before/after), 16px input prevents iOS zoom, a failed send restores the draft, auto-follow only fires when the owner is already near the tail — and every muted grey on paper except the placeholder passes AA by token design (--text-muted annotated 'fica AA').

## Priority issues


### [P1] The decision row is not on screen at first paint on 390x844, and the card itself is misregistered: action icons collapse to 3/8/1px slivers because the svg can shrink, and the orange connector overdraws 8px into the navy card.

**Why:** The single reason to open this tab is to approve/adjust/refuse. On the most common iPhone width the owner sees a navy card with facts and a composer; the object under his thumb where Aprovar should be is the composer input (keyboard rises) and where Recusar should be is the Voz pill. ~150px of scroll recovers it, but the mock's promise (decide without scrolling) is broken, and the primary control looks like a rendering bug when it does appear.

**Fix:** Chrome + rhythm, all CSS, copy untouched (styles.css): `:root{--nav-height:70px;--header-height:56px}`; `.mobile-header{min-height:56px;padding:5px 14px}`; `.bottom-nav{min-height:70px}`; `.composer{min-height:50px;padding:3px 6px 3px 8px;bottom:calc(max(var(--nav-height),48px + max(4px,env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px))) + 4px)}`; `.page-heading{padding:4px 16px 2px}` h1 `margin:8px 0 4px`, `.heading-rule{margin:6px 0}`; `.conversation{gap:12px;padding:8px 14px 16px}`; `.operational-timeline{--timeline-row-gap:10px}`; `.timeline-content--context{margin-top:-6px}`; prompt bubble time floated inline. Card rules scoped under `.mobile-approval` (padding 14/14/12, kicker mb 8, facts gap 6, actions 10/8) plus global `.approval-facts dt svg{display:block}`. Measured result: button row bottom 690.6 vs composer top 718 (27px clear prototype, 48px production). Icons: `.button svg,.approval-actions button svg{flex:0 0 auto}`; at <=767 `.timeline-content--approval{margin-left:-14px}` (card 306px = mock), `.mobile-approval .approval-card{padding-inline:12px}`, `.approval-actions button{padding-inline:6px;gap:5px;min-width:0}`, svg 16px; move the 2-column fallback from <=359 to a new <=389 block. Connector: add `--timeline-approval-inset` to `.operational-timeline`, use it in `.timeline-content--approval{margin-left}` and in `.timeline-approval-node::after{width:calc(var(--timeline-rail-column)/2 + var(--timeline-column-gap) - 6px + var(--timeline-approval-inset))}` so the elbow ends flush at the card edge at 390/768/1440. Small-viewport fallback (375x667 still -134px): wrap the 'Aguardando' cell in `<a className="causal-jump" href="#approval-card">`, put `id="approval-card"` on the `.mobile-approval` wrapper (not inside ApprovalCard, duplicated at >=1200), `.mobile-approval{scroll-margin-top:calc(var(--header-height) + 12px)}`. Gate: record in dashboard/design-qa.md a Browser-pane/VPS-headless measurement at 390x844 and 375x667, scrollY 0, 0 and 34px safe-area: `.mobile-approval .approval-actions` bottom <= `.composer` top - 6px. Re-measure after the F04 strip change (+38-43px).

Findings: F01, F06, F22 · skill: `/layout`


### [P1] After a decision the story does not close: the timeline keeps narrating John while the card swaps to Maria with fabricated facts ('Pedido urgente · mesmo dia', 'Novo cliente', 'San Rafael, CA' are hard-coded defaults, so every production case gets them), the confirmation lands below her card, keyboard focus is returned to her Aprovar button, and the approval node and 'Aguardando' icon stay action-orange under the text 'decisão registrada'.

**Why:** The approved hierarchy call -> rule -> decision for ONE case is the proof that the agent does not improvise. Showing another customer's decision under John's chain, with an urgency label the data never asserted, breaks the brand's 'nada de estado inventado' contract on the decision card itself — and in production it is a live defect, not fixture-only.

**Fix:** ChatView.jsx: `narratedApprovalId = initialMessages[2]?.relatedApprovalId || initialMessages[1]?.relatedApprovalId || null`; `timelineApproval = pendingApproval && (!narratedApprovalId || pendingApproval.id === narratedApprovalId) ? pendingApproval : null`; find `narratedResolution` (system message with that relatedApprovalId) and exclude it from recentMessages. Row 4 renders: ApprovalCard + caption if timelineApproval; else `<ChatMessage message={narratedResolution}/>` (existing .system-message, copy in model.js:445-446); else empty-state ApprovalCard only if pendingApprovals is empty; never 'Nenhuma decisão pendente' while a case is pending. Desktop handoff stays bound to `pendingApproval`. No second .mobile-approval block — the Aprovações badge (2->1) and inspector already carry Maria. ApprovalCard.jsx: render `.approval-kicker` only when `approval.urgencyLabel`, `.client-tag` only when `approval.isNewClient`, location row only when `approval.location`; move the strings into approval-john-urgent in src/data/fixtures.js so the approved mock card is pixel-identical; gateway.supabase.js mapCase sets urgencyLabel from `c.urgency` only when same-day. App.jsx:496 helper interpolates `${approval.clientName}`. Focus (Dialog.jsx:22/58-60): store `{el, key: el.dataset.approvalId}`; on close return focus only if `el.isConnected && el.dataset.approvalId === key`, else focus the last `.conversation .system-message` (add `tabIndex={-1}` to it, it already has role=status) or #main-content; add `data-approval-id={approval.id}` and `aria-describedby={clientId}` (useId) on the three card buttons. Colour: ChatView.jsx:73 `causal-icon--pending` only when status is pending/aguardando else `causal-icon--done { color: var(--teal); background: var(--teal-soft) }`; line 103 `timeline-approval-node` + `is-resolved` when `!approval` -> `border-color/background: var(--teal)`; leave rail and connectors orange (continuity motif). Optional 240ms transition on node/icon/handoff (reduced-motion block already covers it). Extend tests/chat-view.test.mjs: narrated case resolved + another pending -> no card in timeline, system message in row 4, no 'Nenhuma decisão pendente'; approval without urgencyLabel -> no 'Pedido urgente'.

Findings: F02, F07, F19 · skill: `/harden`


### [P1] The primary action's label is the lowest-contrast text on the screen (white on #ff5a36 = 3.10:1 for 13px/700 'Aprovar', 'Confirmar aprovação', 'Restaurar' and the 10px nav badge), and the composer placeholder is the only sub-AA running text on paper (#798b92 on white = 3.55:1).

**Why:** This owner reads in daylight with one thumb; the two strings he must be able to read are 'Aprovar' and 'Fale com o Ligou...'. The brand guide p.17 already bans the inverted pair (#FF5A36 text on paper, 3.01:1) and prints '#FF5A36 / #041D27 — 5.59:1'; the landing DS defines --on-accent as ink-950. The dashboard's white glyph is the deviation.

**Fix:** styles.css, base rules, orange fill and copy untouched: `.button--primary { color: var(--ink-950); }` (:1085) covers all eight primary buttons; `.nav-badge { color: var(--ink-950); }` (:475); `.memory-card-actions .memory-approve-button` :1687 `color: var(--ink-950)` and rewrite its hover (:1693) to keep the fill on --orange (`filter: brightness(0.94)`) because ink on --orange-dark is 2.90:1; Aprovações view needs `.approvals-actions .approvals-approve-button { color: var(--ink-950) }` or `!important` in the existing block (:2038) since `.approvals-actions button { color:#f5f8f9 }` outranks a lone class. Do NOT switch fills to #b83216 (reserved for small text on paper, and it is the .button--danger fill). `.composer-send` may stay white (icon-only, 3.10 >= 3:1) or go ink for the handset-badge motif. Placeholder: `.composer input::placeholder { color: var(--text-secondary); opacity: 1; }` (6.25:1; --text-muted 4.92:1 is the floor). One line to RJ: the approved mock shows WHITE 'Aprovar' and badge; this is a deliberate brand-backed one-attribute change, named, not slipped in.

Findings: F03, F15 · skill: `/colorize`


### [P1] There is no type scale: 11 rendered sizes at 390 (nine between 7 and 13px in 3-16% steps), chat body 11.3px, the causal chain call -> rule -> decision at 7.1-8.5px on every phone 360-430 (a 320 phone gets it 40% larger via the <=359 override), dialog consequence text 10-11px, card kicker 9px, chrome labels 8-11px, and nothing grows between 768 and 1440 while the 28px wordmark sits 0.8px from the 28.8px inspector h2.

**Why:** At arm's length in sunlight 11px Archivo blurs, and because every secondary element sits 1-2px from it nothing pops except the h1. The strip is the only place the rule is shown as a rule on mobile, and 'Aguardando · sua decisão' — the one status he cares about — is the smallest text on the page. The brand contract lists typography as 'livre para melhorar'.

**Fix:** Add role tokens alongside the existing ones (do not delete --type-small or re-value --type-micro, which feed 26 and 13 out-of-view declarations): `--type-label: 11px; --type-body: clamp(13px, calc(11.98px + 0.2625vw), 15px); --type-chat: var(--type-body); --type-lead: clamp(15px, calc(13.9px + 0.28vw), 18px); --type-subhead: clamp(20px, calc(18.4px + 0.4vw), 24px)`. Re-point ONLY #ligou selectors: label -> .message-label, .approval-kicker, .inspector-kicker, .approval-context span, .business-card span, .choice-grid legend, .comparison-grid span, .message-bubble time, .client-tag, .sending-status, .prototype-badge, .bottom-nav .destination-link; body -> .message-bubble p (line-height var(--leading-body)), .approval-facts dd (delete the 13px override :2871), .approval-context p, .approval-caption, .page-heading > p, .dialog-heading p, .choice-grid small, .inspector-content > p (max-width 40ch), .voice-demo-button, .system-message, .sidebar .destination-link, .reset-button, .profile-menu-panel span/button, .button, .approval-actions button; lead -> .desktop-timeline-handoff strong, .choice-grid strong, .approval-empty strong; subhead -> .dialog-heading h2, .inspector-content > h2 (replacing clamp(22px,2vw,30px)). Delete dead rule :812-815. Causal strip (measured: three columns cannot hold 9px labels below ~430): `.causal-strip small { font-size: clamp(9px, calc(8.1px + 0.23vw), 10px); font-weight:700; letter-spacing:0.04em; white-space:normal; overflow:visible }`, `.causal-strip strong { font-size: clamp(11px, calc(9.9px + 0.28vw), 13px); -webkit-line-clamp:3 }`; at <768 `.causal-strip { grid-template-columns: minmax(0,.8fr) minmax(0,1.6fr); padding:8px 6px }` and `.causal-strip > div:last-child { grid-column:1/-1; margin-top:4px; padding-top:4px; border-left:0; border-top:1px solid var(--border) }` (use :last-child, the first two cells render conditionally); at >=768 restore three columns 0.75fr 1.25fr 1fr and replace :2867 `letter-spacing:0.08em` with var(--tracking-label); delete only the two font-size overrides at :2638-2644. Strip grows to ~99px (+38-43px) — F01's budget absorbs it; re-run scrollWidth/scrollHeight checks at 360/375/390/430. Verify no Memory/Approvals/Settings selector changes computed size; expected #ligou set at 390: 11 / 13 / 15 / 20 / 24.7.

Findings: F04, F05, F09, F16, F17 · skill: `/typeset`


### [P2] The decision sheet asks the owner to choose case vs rule while blurring and covering the facts (case mode y 483-832, rule mode 319-832 over a card at 517-769), never shows approval.proposedAction — the string that is literally saved as the rule — and, when 'Salvar como regra' is tapped, jumps 164px upward in one frame so the Duração select lands where his finger was; the sheet opens with the ring on the 'Fechar' X.

**Why:** Recognition over recall at the one moment a hurried tap becomes permanent business memory. The default path is safe (case-only), so not P1, but a blind permanent write plus a teleporting sheet on the product's core loop is more than polish.

**Fix:** App.jsx approve dialog only: inside `.dialog-form`, before `.choice-grid`, echo facts with the existing paper-warm pattern: `<div className="comparison-grid"><div><span>Solicitação</span><p>{approval.request}{approval.note ? ' · ' + approval.note : ''}</p></div><div><span>Regra consultada</span><p>{approval.rule}</p></div><div><span>Nova proposta</span><p>{approval.proposedAction}</p></div></div>` (all three labels already exist in the product; sheet ~660px < 743px cap; do not touch .dialog-backdrop). Motion: render `.rule-options` unconditionally inside `<div className="rule-options-reveal" data-open={approvalMode === 'rule'} inert={approvalMode !== 'rule'}>`; CSS `.rule-options-reveal { display:grid; grid-template-rows:0fr; overflow:hidden; margin-top:-18px; padding-inline:4px; margin-inline:-4px; transition: grid-template-rows var(--dur-med) var(--ease-out) } [data-open="true"] { grid-template-rows:1fr } .rule-options-reveal > .rule-options { min-height:0; padding-top:18px }` (the -18/+18 fold keeps today's gap in the default state); add border/background transitions on `.choice-grid label`. Focus: Dialog.jsx:25 `panel?.focus()` (panel has tabIndex -1 + aria-labelledby/describedby), extend the Shift+Tab trap at :42 to include `document.activeElement === panel`, add `.dialog-panel:focus-visible { outline:none }`; keep the X first in DOM (sticky heading), optionally `.icon-button { border-radius: var(--radius-sm) }`. Reduced motion already handled by the global block.

Findings: F10, F13, F11 · skill: `/clarify`


### [P2] Interactive states were never finished: the only text field has its focus indicator removed (`.composer input { outline:0 }` beats the global ring, no :has/focus-within on the pill), there is zero :active anywhere while the OS tap highlight is suppressed, the confirmation toast pops in at the top with no motion for 4.2s over the inspector heading on desktop, and 'Respondendo…' is 9px with no entrance (never painted in prototype; the only in-flight signal in Supabase mode).

**Why:** Individually these are polish, together they make the surface feel inert exactly when the owner needs confidence that his tap and his message registered. Keyboard users lose position at the composer (weak-indicator inconsistency: every button gets a 3px ring, the field gets a 1px caret).

**Fix:** Focus: after `.composer input` add `.composer:has(input:focus-visible) { border-color: var(--teal); box-shadow: var(--shadow-float), 0 0 0 3px var(--focus-ring-color); }` (opaque token, ~6.9:1; :has already used at :388; scoping to the input avoids a double ring on Anexar/Voz/Enviar). Press: after the `(hover:hover)` block (post :3301) and before reduced-motion, outside any media query: `.button:active:not(:disabled), .approval-actions button:active:not(:disabled), .composer-send:active, .voice-demo-button:active, .composer-attachment:active, .icon-button:active, .profile-button:active, .toast button:active, .profile-menu-panel button:active { transform: translateY(0) scale(0.97) }`, `.button--primary:active:not(:disabled) { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--orange), #000 14%) }` (never --orange-dark, which is the danger fill), `.button--outline-dark:active { background: rgba(255,255,255,.10) }`; inside the hover block extend the transition list to the composer/icon/toast/profile controls and add `.button--primary:hover:not(:disabled) { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--orange), #000 10%) }`. Toast: in `@media (min-width:1200px)` `.app-shell.has-inspector .toast { top:auto; bottom:24px; right:24px; left:auto; width:376px }` (into the empty navy under the card, never above the mobile composer where auto-scroll parks the newest message); `@keyframes toast-in { from { opacity:0; transform:translateY(-8px) } }` and `.toast { animation: toast-in var(--dur-med) var(--ease-out) both }` (translateY(8px) in the has-inspector rule); App.jsx:42 3200ms for kind success only. Sending: replace `.sending-status` with a 28px white chip, `font-size: var(--type-small)`, `animation: status-in var(--dur-med) var(--ease-out) 120ms both` (appears only if still pending after 120ms) and `svg { animation: status-breathe 1.6s ease-in-out infinite }`; define both keyframes next to dialog-rise; no artificial hold in sendMessage (invented state).

Findings: F08, F12, F14, F18 · skill: `/polish`


## Single biggest opportunity

Put the decision where the thumb already is: reclaim ~40px of chrome and rhythm so the Aprovar/Ajustar/Recusar row clears the composer at first paint on 390x844 (F01, absorbing the causal-strip growth from F04), and make that row belong to the case being narrated (F02). Those two changes turn the tab from 'read, scroll, hunt, decide about the wrong customer' into the approved promise — call -> rule -> decision on one screen — without touching a single string or the look RJ approved.


## Minor observations

- F20 (P3): desktop inspector is a full-height navy column with ~458px empty under the card (card on ink-950 = 1.23:1 separation; at 1920 it stretches to ~856px). Baseline fix inside >=1200: `.inspector-content { display:grid; align-content:center; min-height:100%; width:min(100%,428px); margin-inline:auto }`. Option A (paper-warm inspector so the navy card regains figure/ground) is RJ's call — the brand guide's own p.23 PAINEL thumbnail is dark.
- F21 (P3): the desktop handoff 'Exceção pronta / Decida no painel à direita' is a 520x62 inert div repeating the inspector h2 ~170px to its right with an arrow that links to nothing. Render as `<button>` focusing `.context-inspector .approval-actions button` when pending (keep the resolved div with role=status), compact to min-height 44 with the arrow inline at 18px; keep the orange-soft/orange-dark 'pendente' status tint (consistent with .onboarding-cta and .approvals-status).
- Checked and fine: the soft-orange tints on handoff/onboarding/pending icon are a consistent status grammar in this stylesheet, not stray CTA orange; the rail staying orange after a decision is brand continuity (p.13), not a defect.
- Checked and fine: the mock itself renders chat at ~11-12px equivalent, strip labels ~7px and 35px buttons, so the shipped type is faithful and already larger than the mock in every role; the type-scale work is an evolution the contract permits, not a fidelity failure.
- Checked and fine: dialog initial focus on the first focusable was already accepted in design-qa.md:60; touch/mouse opens never show the ring (only script-driven focus in the internal browser did).
- Out of scope, flagged for a separate ticket: in Supabase mode App.jsx:274-275 discards the sendMessage result and re-fetches, so the owner's text and the canned agent reply appended in gateway.supabase.js:178 never appear after 'Respondendo…' — a correctness defect, not a design one.
- Out of scope, copy: the 'Somente este caso' helper hard-codes 'Autoriza o pedido de John sem alterar a memória.' (App.jsx:496) and reads wrong for Maria/David; interpolating clientName keeps the copy shape but is RJ's call.

## Personas — red flags


**Casey — distracted, one-handed, mobile 390x844**

- At first paint the Aprovar/Ajustar/Recusar row (y 713-757) is entirely under the fixed composer (701-757); the composer text input sits where Aprovar should be (mis-tap raises the keyboard) and the 'Voz · demo' pill (x 263-371) covers Recusar's position; ~150px of scroll to discover the buttons (F01).
- Aprovar's check icon renders 3px wide, Ajustar's 8px, Recusar's 0.8px — the primary control looks broken on every phone 360-430 (F06).
- After approving John the identical navy card reappears reading 'Maria Alvarez · Novo cliente / PEDIDO URGENTE · MESMO DIA' under John's story; the kicker and tag are fabricated defaults, not her data (F02).
- The rule and 'Aguardando · sua decisão' are 7.2/8.3px, the smallest text on the screen, while walking in daylight (F04).
- No press feedback on any control (tap highlight removed, no :active) and the confirmation toast lands at y 72 with no motion for 4.2s while eyes are at y ~700 (F12, F14).

**Sam — screen reader / keyboard**

- After 'Confirmar aprovação', Dialog.jsx returns focus to the same DOM button, which now reads 'Aprovar' for Maria Alvarez; nothing announces the case switched (F07).
- Tabbing from Recusar into #chat-input produces no visible change: `.composer input { outline:0 }` beats the global 3px ring and the pill has no focus state (F08).
- 'Você aprovou o pedido de John Miller…' is appended after Maria's card, so the confirmation is heard right after Maria's facts (F02).
- Dialogs open with 'Fechar, botão' focused before the title; Shift+Tab from a freshly focused panel would leak behind the backdrop if the panel is focused without extending the trap (F11, P3).
- Desktop: 'Decida no painel à direita' is a visual reference only; the handoff div is not focusable and not linked to the inspector card (F21, P3).

**Rafael — Brazilian roofer, phone in sunlight, one missed call on his mind**

- 'O que aconteceu com o John?' is answered with 115px of heading and a 5-line 11.3px paragraph; the thing he needs — Aprovar — is under the composer (F01, F05).
- 'Aprovar' is white on #ff5a36 at 13px = 3.10:1, less contrast than the 8px labels on the same card; the brand's own table says ink on orange = 5.59:1 (F03).
- Inside the card he cannot see WHY he is being asked: .approval-context is display:none on mobile, the rule exists only in the 8.3px strip cell and the agent bubble (F04, F10).
- When he taps 'Salvar como regra' the sheet jumps 164px and asks Escopo/Duração without showing the text that becomes the permanent rule (F10, F13).
- Maria's transfer request arrives labelled 'PEDIDO URGENTE · MESMO DIA' — urgency theater the data never asserted, the opposite of relief with control (F02).
- 'Fale com o Ligou...' is #798b92 on white = 3.55:1, faint in daylight (F15).

## Verified findings (detail)


### F01 · P1 · ADJUSTED — Decision card action row (Aprovar/Ajustar/Recusar) is 100% under the fixed composer at first paint on 390x844; the approved mock puts it above the fold

**Where:** /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/styles.css:1173-1191 (.composer position:fixed, bottom = nav+10px, min-height 54, padding 5px 6px 5px 8px), :399-411 (.bottom-nav min-height 77), :87-88 (--nav-height 77px, --header-height 62px), :240-252 (.mobile-header 62), :492-540 (.page-heading, h1 margin 10px 0 5px, .heading-rule margin 7px 0 7px), :577-585 (.conversation gap 14 / padding 14), :587-600 (--timeline-row-gap 12), :701-705 (.timeline-content--context), :747-749 (agent bubble max-width 270px), :951-958 + :1054-1061 (.approval-card / .approval-actions); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/views/ChatView.jsx:104-114; /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/components/ApprovalCard.jsx:53-62

**Viewports:** mobile 390, mobile 375x667 / iOS Safari (~664 innerHeight)

**Evidence:** Measured live at first paint (scrollY 0, fresh fixtures) by three lenses: .approval-actions y 701.6/713-757 (44px buttons), .composer fixed y 701-757, .bottom-nav y 767-844 -> not one pixel of the action row is visible, the divider above it (701.6) is hidden so the card reads as informational, caption at 776.6 sits under the nav; documentHeight 996 -> ~152px of scroll to discover the buttons. The object physically under the thumb where Aprovar should be is the 'Voz · demo' pill (x263-371, y707-751). Approved mock (853x1844 = same 0.462 aspect as 390x844) at 390 scale: header 0-56, owner bubble 157-206, agent 218-332, strip 333-394 (0.5px below the bubble), prompt 408-461, card 470-677, button row 631-665, caption 688-695, composer 719-772 in flow, nav 777-844; occluded band 125px vs 143 implemented. Drift vs mock: header +6, heading block 115 vs 101, agent->strip 13 vs 0.5, prompt bubble +14 (time on its own line; mock inline), card +46 (facts rows 20px because the inline dt svg adds a strut; actions block 22 above buttons vs 14), composer+nav 143 vs 122. Layout lens injected a transient budget (header 56, conversation padding-top 8, h1 margin 8/4, strip padding 6 + min-height 48, prompt time floated inline, dt svg display:block, kicker mb 8, actions 10/8, card padding-top 14, composer 52/pad 4, nav 70) -> action row bottom 712.1 vs composer top 710, 2px short; a11y lens' injected cuts (below) moved the row to 659-712 (33px visible). Previous QA (design-qa.md) validated reachability at 320x700 scroll-end only, never first paint at 390. Note the mock achieves one-screen fit with ~10px text and 35px buttons, which are not acceptable on a phone, so the budget must come from spacing and chrome, not type. All four lenses rated P1.

**Why:** The owner is on a phone, outdoors, hands busy, one missed call in mind; the single reason to open this tab is to approve/adjust/refuse. On the most common iPhone width he sees a navy card with facts and a composer, nothing signals that buttons exist below, and a tap meant for Aprovar opens the voice dialog. The mock's core promise (call -> rule -> decision, decide here without scrolling) is broken.

**Fix:** Keep the finding's chrome + rhythm changes, minus one item, with scoping fixes. All copy untouched.

Chrome (styles.css :87-88, :240-252, :399-411, :1173-1191): `:root{--nav-height:70px;--header-height:56px}`; `.mobile-header{min-height:56px;padding:5px 14px}` (profile button 44px at y 5.5 fits); `.bottom-nav{min-height:70px}` (links 61px, nothing clips); `.composer{min-height:50px;padding:3px 6px 3px 8px;bottom:calc(max(var(--nav-height),48px + max(4px,env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px))) + 4px)}` -> composer 52px tall, top 718 at 390x844, 4px gap to nav (mock 5px). `--header-height` is also consumed by the toast at :2257 and follows automatically.

Rhythm (:492-540, :577-600, :701-705, :757-760): `.page-heading{padding:4px 16px 2px}`, `.page-heading h1{margin:8px 0 4px}`, `.heading-rule{margin:6px 0}` (.page-heading is used only by ChatView, no cross-view effect); `.conversation{gap:12px;padding:8px 14px 16px}`; `.operational-timeline{--timeline-row-gap:10px}`; `.timeline-content--context{margin-top:-6px}` (measured: strip lands 4px under the agent bubble; mock 0.5px); `.timeline-content--prompt .message-bubble{padding-top:8px}`, `.timeline-content--prompt .message-bubble p{display:inline}`, `.timeline-content--prompt .message-bubble time{float:right;margin:2px 0 0 12px}` (measured prompt bubble 64.1 -> 52.1px, time sits at x 270-296 on the last line, inside the bubble, as in the mock). DROP `.timeline-content .message-row--agent .message-bubble{max-width:min(100%,300px)}` - measured no height change (115.3px at 270 and 300) and it drifts from the mock's ~229px bubble.

Card - SCOPE to mobile so the desktop inspector card (App.jsx:311) is untouched: `.mobile-approval .approval-card{padding:14px 14px 12px}`, `.mobile-approval .approval-kicker{margin-bottom:8px}`, `.mobile-approval .approval-facts{gap:6px}`, `.mobile-approval .client-tag{padding-block:1px;line-height:1.1}`, `.mobile-approval .approval-actions{margin-top:10px;padding-top:8px}`. Keep `.approval-facts dt svg{display:block}` global (measured fact rows 20 -> 17px; it is a correct fix on both surfaces).

Measured result @390x844 with fixtures: button row bottom 690.6 vs composer top 718 (27px clear in prototype mode, 48px in production with no badge; caption 701.6 clears composer 718 and nav 774). This absorbs F04's +~17px strip height; re-measure after F04.

Small-viewport fallback (375x667 / iOS Safari ~664: still -134px with the fix): in ChatView.jsx CausalStrip, render the 'Aguardando' cell as `<a className="causal-jump" href="#approval-card">` wrapping the existing icon + `<p>` (strings unchanged). Put `id="approval-card"` on the `.mobile-approval` wrapper in ChatView.jsx:106 (NOT inside ApprovalCard, which is also rendered in the inspector -> duplicate id at >=1200). Use `.mobile-approval{scroll-margin-top:calc(var(--header-height) + 12px)}` (fragment jumps align the target top; scroll-margin-bottom is inert here). At 375x667 with the fix: docH 905, max scroll 238 -> card top 217, buttons 437 < composer 541, reachable in one tap.

Gate: not a local Playwright test (repo has only node --test unit tests; local browsers are banned). Record in dashboard/design-qa.md a required measurement 'at 390x844 and 375x667 with fixtures, scrollY 0: `.mobile-approval .approval-actions` bottom <= `.composer` top - 6px (390) / anchor jump reaches it (375)', executed in the internal Browser pane or VPS headless Chrome, at 0 and 34px safe-area-inset-bottom.

Correct the 'why' text: a mis-tap where Aprovar should be focuses the composer input (keyboard rises); the voice pill covers Recusar's position.

**Verifier:** Core claim CONFIRMED by live re-measurement at 390x844 first paint (localhost:4180, fixtures): .mobile-approval .approval-actions y 701.6-756.6, buttons 712.6-756.6, .composer fixed 701-757, .bottom-nav 767-844, caption 776.6, docH 996. CSS arithmetic agrees: composer bottom = max(77px, 52px)+10 = 87 -> top 844-87-56 = 701. The row and its divider are 0% visible; prior design-qa.md only validated 320x700 at scroll-end and 430x932, never 390 first paint. Two evidence errors: (1) the Voz-demo pill (x 263-371) covers RECUSAR (x 267-354), not Aprovar; over Aprovar (x 82-169) is the composer text input, so a mis-tap raises the keyboard rather than the voice dialog; (2) 'nothing signals buttons exist' is overstated - a 10px navy band of the card shows between composer bottom (757) and nav top (767). Fix efficacy verified by injecting the proposed CSS: button bottom 690.6 vs composer top 718 = 27.4px clear (prototype), 48.4px with badge hidden (production), caption clears nav - so the fix over-delivers its own >=9px estimate and absorbs F04's +17px. Fix defects: `.timeline-content .message-row--agent .message-bubble{max-width:300px}` is a no-op (agent bubble height 115.3px at both 270 and 300; no 5->4 line change) and widens the bubble away from the mock (~229px at 390 scale); the .approval-card/.approval-kicker/.approval-facts/.approval-actions/.client-tag rules are shared with the desktop inspector card (App.jsx:311, .context-inspector .approval-card at styles.css:3134) so they change an approved desktop surface unscoped; `id='approval-card'` inside ApprovalCard would be duplicated in the DOM at >=1200 (mobile card is display:none but present); fragment navigation aligns the target TOP, so `scroll-margin-bottom` does nothing - `scroll-margin-top` is needed to clear the sticky header; and the repo has no browser test harness (node --test unit tests only), so the 'design-qa gate' cannot be a local Playwright test under RJ's no-local-browser rule. At 375x667 the fix still leaves the row 134px under the composer, so the anchor fallback is justified. Severity P1 stands: the single primary action of the primary tab is invisible at first paint on the most common width, contradicting the approved one-screen mock; not P0 because ~150px of scroll recovers it.


### F02 · P1 · ADJUSTED — After a decision the timeline still narrates John while the card silently swaps to Maria with invented facts and the confirmation lands below her card

**Where:** /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/views/ChatView.jsx:80-121 (OperationalTimeline takes messages[1..2] + any pendingApproval), :73-74 (Aguardando icon class), :116-121 (desktop handoff bound to the same value), :210-218 (passes approval={pendingApproval} regardless of messages[1].relatedApprovalId); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/App.jsx:239-243 (pendingApproval = pendingApprovals[0]); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/components/ApprovalCard.jsx:26, :31 (defaults 'Pedido urgente · mesmo dia' and 'Novo cliente'); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/fixtures.js:47, 55 (both John messages carry relatedApprovalId 'approval-john-urgent'; 'approval-maria-transfer' has no messages)

**Viewports:** all (measured 390x844 and 1440x900)

**Evidence:** Measured after 'Confirmar aprovação' for John at 390x844: timeline bubbles still read 'John ligou em inglês…' and 'Quer aprovar este encaixe para o atendimento hoje?'; causal strip reads 'Aguardando: decisão registrada' with the icon still orange (bg rgb(255,241,236), color rgb(184,50,22), class causal-icon--pending); the card below reads 'Maria Alvarez · Novo cliente' with kicker 'PEDIDO URGENTE · MESMO DIA' although Maria's fixture has no urgencyLabel and her request is a transfer, not a same-day job; the system message 'Você aprovou o pedido de John Miller…' renders at y 591, below Maria's card (y 319-555); the toast covers the heading at the top. Desktop: handoff still says 'Exceção pronta / Decida no painel à direita' while the narrated case is closed. The card's name is 12.3px and the kicker style is identical for both cases, so nothing signals the swap. Both lenses rated P1.

**Why:** The approved hierarchy is call -> rule -> decision for ONE case; it is the proof that the agent 'does not improvise'. Showing Maria's decision under John's chain breaks that proof, glues a same-day urgent label to a transfer request (invented state, which the brand contract forbids) and invites a wrong-target tap — the busy lock stops a double RPC, not a decision on the wrong customer. Relief-with-control depends on the story closing where it started.

**Fix:** 1) ChatView.jsx: `const narratedApprovalId = initialMessages[2]?.relatedApprovalId || initialMessages[1]?.relatedApprovalId || null; const timelineApproval = pendingApproval && (!narratedApprovalId || pendingApproval.id === narratedApprovalId) ? pendingApproval : null;` — do NOT use the relatedCallId/callContext.id variant (breaks production cases with null call_id). 2) `const narratedResolution = narratedApprovalId ? messages.find(m => m.role === 'system' && m.relatedApprovalId === narratedApprovalId) : null;` and exclude that message from recentMessages. In OperationalTimeline row 4 (`.timeline-content--approval` / `.mobile-approval`): if timelineApproval -> ApprovalCard + caption (unchanged); else if narratedResolution -> `<ChatMessage message={narratedResolution} />` (existing `.system-message`, copy already exists in model.js:445-446); else if !pendingApproval -> existing ApprovalCard empty state ('Nenhuma decisão pendente'); else render nothing (never claim 'nenhuma pendente' while pendingApprovals.length > 0). Add `.timeline-approval-node.is-resolved, .timeline-approval-node.is-resolved::before, .timeline-approval-node.is-resolved::after { background: var(--teal); border-color: var(--teal) }` when the row shows a confirmation (orange = action, teal = confirmation). 3) Desktop handoff stays bound to `pendingApproval` (its text 'Exceção pronta / Decida no painel à direita' remains true because the inspector shows the next case); `is-resolved` only when pendingApproval is null (current logic). Rendering the narratedResolution above it in row 4 closes John's story on desktop too. 4) Do NOT add a second `.mobile-approval` block for the next case; the Aprovações badge (2->1), the Aprovações tab and the desktop inspector already carry it. 5) ApprovalCard.jsx:24-27 render `.approval-kicker` only when `approval.urgencyLabel` is truthy; :31 render `.client-tag` only when `approval.isNewClient`; :33-36 render the location row only when `approval.location`; drop the 'San Rafael, CA' / 'John Miller' string defaults. Move the strings, don't delete them: add `urgencyLabel: "Pedido urgente · mesmo dia", isNewClient: true` to approval-john-urgent in src/data/fixtures.js:150-164 so the approved mock card is pixel-identical; in gateway.supabase.js mapCase, set urgencyLabel from `c.urgency` only when it denotes same-day, else omit. 6) ChatView.jsx:73: `className={pending ? 'causal-icon causal-icon--pending' : 'causal-icon causal-icon--done'}` with `pending = ['pending','aguardando'].includes(context?.status)`; either add `.causal-icon--done { color: var(--teal); background: var(--teal-soft) }` after styles.css:906 or simply omit the modifier so it inherits the default teal-soft `.causal-icon`. 7) Same family, one line: App.jsx:496 helper 'Autoriza o pedido de John sem alterar a memória.' -> interpolate `${approval.clientName}` (copy shape unchanged). 8) Extend tests/chat-view.test.mjs: narrated approval resolved + another pending -> no ApprovalCard in the timeline, system message present in row 4, no 'Nenhuma decisão pendente'; approval without urgencyLabel -> no 'Pedido urgente'. Pair with F14 (toast position).

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and deterministic from source, not just from the measurement notes: App.jsx:239-243 takes pendingApprovals[0] (John -> Maria once John is 'aprovada'; fixture order John, Maria, David); ChatView.jsx:141/211 freezes the narrated case to messages[1..2] (both carry relatedApprovalId 'approval-john-urgent', src/data/fixtures.js:47,55 — note the finding's path 'src/fixtures.js' is wrong) while :213 passes pendingApproval unconditionally; model.js:439-440 flips callContext.status to 'aprovado' so CausalStrip prints 'decisão registrada' but :73 hard-codes `causal-icon--pending` (styles.css:903-906 = #fff1ec/#b83216, exactly the rgb values measured); model.js:137-149 pushes the system message to the END of messages, and ChatView:143-146/220-222 renders index>=3 after the timeline, so 'Você aprovou o pedido de John Miller…' necessarily lands below Maria's card; ApprovalCard.jsx:26/31 defaults 'Pedido urgente · mesmo dia' and an unconditional 'Novo cliente' tag, and Maria's fixture has neither — the transfer request is labelled urgent same-day. Desktop handoff (:116-119) reads 'Exceção pronta' because approval=Maria. design-qa.md item 7 only tested the handoff after BOTH approvals, so this intermediate state was never QA'd. Extra weight: in production gateway.supabase.js mapCase sets location:null and no urgencyLabel/isNewClient, so every real case gets the fabricated kicker, 'Novo cliente' and 'San Rafael, CA' — the invented-state part is a live defect, not fixture-only. Severity P1 holds (brand contract 'nada de estado inventado' broken on the decision card; production impact), although the wrong-target-tap argument is weaker than stated: Aprovar opens a dialog whose description names the client (App.jsx:487) — but its radio helper hard-codes 'Autoriza o pedido de John' (App.jsx:496), same family. The FIX needs corrections: (a) the 'equivalent' `relatedCallId === callContext.id` variant is NOT equivalent — in production callContext.id = pending[0].relatedCallId ?? pending[0].id, so a case with null call_id would hide its own card; (b) binding the desktop handoff to timelineApproval renders 'Decisão registrada / Nenhuma exceção pendente' while Maria is still pending, the badge shows 1 and the inspector shows her card — a new false statement; likewise ApprovalCard(null) on mobile would print 'Nenhuma decisão pendente' while a case is pending; (c) step 4 as written strips the kicker and 'Novo cliente' from John's card in the approved mock unless fixtures gain the fields; (d) step 3 (second `.mobile-approval` block) adds a second exception card to the approved single-card hierarchy — prefer the existing Aprovações badge/inspector path.


### F03 · P1 · ADJUSTED — Primary action text is the lowest-contrast text on the screen: white on ORANGE 500 = 3.10:1 (Aprovar, Confirmar aprovação, Restaurar, nav badge)

**Where:** /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/styles.css:1085-1088 (.button--primary { color: var(--white); background: var(--orange) }), :475-490 (.nav-badge 10px/800 white on orange), :1228-1231 (.composer-send, non-text icon); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/views/ChatView.jsx:200 (onboarding .button--primary); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/components/ApprovalCard.jsx:54 ('Aprovar')

**Viewports:** all

**Evidence:** Computed WCAG: #ffffff on #ff5a36 = 3.10:1. 'Aprovar' is 13px/700 (styles.css:1073) and the badge '2' is 10px/800 — both are small text and need 4.5:1. The brand guide p.17 already bans the same pair inverted (#FF5A36 text on #FBFCF8 = 3.01:1, 'não usar em texto pequeno'); the dashboard ships the mirror image of that ban on its one mandatory action. Alternatives computed: #041d27 on #ff5a36 = 5.59:1 (the guide's own handset badge is ink glyph on orange); #ffffff on #b83216 = 5.99:1. The primary CTA currently has less contrast than the 8px labels on the navy card. Both lenses rated P1.

**Why:** This owner reads on a phone in daylight with one thumb; the only thing he must be able to read and hit is 'Aprovar'. Orange stays as the action colour — the failure is the glyph colour on top of it (WCAG 1.4.3 fail on the decision button).

**Fix:** Keep all copy and the orange fill; switch glyphs to ink, mirroring the landing DS (--on-accent = ink-950) and the p.14 handset badge. Edits in /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/styles.css (all base rules, none inside a media query): (a) :1085-1088 `.button--primary { color: var(--ink-950); background: var(--orange); }` — covers Aprovar, Confirmar aprovação, Restaurar and the five other primary buttons in one shot; (b) :475-490 `.nav-badge { color: var(--ink-950); }` (only `right`/`border-color` are overridden later, so no specificity issue); (c) :1687-1691 change `color: #fff` to `color: var(--ink-950)` IN PLACE, and rewrite the hover at :1693-1696 so the fill does not go to --orange-dark: `.memory-card-actions .memory-approve-button:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); filter: brightness(0.94); }` (ink on ~#f0552f ≈ 5.0:1; ink on #b83216 would be 2.90:1) — or simply delete that hover rule and rely on the existing translateY(-1px) lift in the (hover: hover) block at :3291; (d) :2038-2041 the selector must beat `.approvals-actions button` — use `.approvals-actions .approvals-approve-button { color: var(--ink-950); }` (0,2,1) or add `color: var(--ink-950) !important` to the existing !important block; (e) drop `.button--primary svg { color }` — Tabler icons inherit currentColor; (f) `.composer-send` stays white (icon-only, 3.10 ≥ 3:1 under 1.4.11) OR switch to ink for consistency with the handset badge motif — optional, not required. Result: #041d27 on #ff5a36 = 5.59:1 on every text-bearing orange control. Do NOT use the white-on-#b83216 fill alternative: .impeccable.md reserves the deep orange for large text on light backgrounds, and #041d27 on #b83216 fails (2.90). After applying, re-check the three approval buttons and the nav badge at 390px and on the desktop sidebar (ink glyph on orange disc on navy — same look as the brand badge).

**Verifier:** Evidence verified against source: .button--primary (styles.css:1085-1088) is white on var(--orange)=#ff5a36 at 13px/700 (1072-1073); .nav-badge (475-490) is 10px/800 white on orange; .composer-send (1228) same pair. Usage sites confirmed: ApprovalCard.jsx:54 'Aprovar', App.jsx:523 'Confirmar aprovação', App.jsx:616 'Restaurar', AppShell.jsx:51 badge, plus five more .button--primary instances (Tentar novamente, Salvar ajuste, Criar nova versão, Criar evento de teste, MemoryView:167) the finding did not list. Contrast recomputed: #fff/#ff5a36 = 3.10 (fail 1.4.3 for 13px bold; large-text exemption starts ~18.66px bold), #041d27/#ff5a36 = 5.59, #fff/#b83216 = 5.99, #ff5a36/#fbfcf8 = 3.01. 'Lowest-contrast text on the screen' holds: every other text pair on #ligou computes above 3.10 (kicker #ff7959 on ink-900 5.48, 8px .approval-context span 6.68, --text-muted 4.92; --text-faint 2.13 is defined but unused). Brand precedents are real and stronger than claimed: p.17 table prints '#FF5A36 / #041D27 — 5.59:1' and '#FF5A36 / #FBFCF8 — 3.01:1 não usar em texto pequeno'; p.14 handset badge is orange disc + ink glyph; and the landing DS that styles.css:65 claims alignment with defines --on-accent: var(--lg-ink-950) and .lg-btn--accent{background:var(--accent);color:var(--on-accent)} — the dashboard's white glyph is the deviation, not the fix. design-qa.md never checked contrast. Severity P1 is fair (axe rates color-contrast 'serious'; sunlight glare compounds it; at 390px the check icon collapses to a dot so the label carries all meaning) — the nav badge alone would be P2 since it has aria-label and is supplementary. However the fix as written has three defects: (1) '.approvals-approve-button { color }' (0,1,0) LOSES to '.approvals-actions button { color: #f5f8f9 }' (0,1,1) at styles.css:2020-2036 — the existing rule already needs !important for the same reason — so the Aprovações view stays white; (2) '.memory-card-actions .memory-approve-button:hover' (1693-1696) switches the fill to --orange-dark, so an ink glyph on hover = 2.90:1, a NEW failure worse than today's 5.99; (3) .composer-send is listed in the ink selector group and then told it may keep white, and '.button--primary svg { color }' is redundant because @tabler/icons-react renders stroke=currentColor. Also the approved mock (exec-779a40d7…png) shows WHITE 'Aprovar' and a white '2' badge on orange, so the implementation is faithful to the mock and the fix changes one attribute RJ saw — surgical and brand-backed, but it should be surfaced to him, not slipped in.


### F04 · P2 · ADJUSTED — Causal chain (call -> rule -> decision) renders at 7.1-8.5px on every phone from 360 to 430 — the smallest text on the page, and a 320px phone gets it 40% larger than a 390px phone

**Where:** /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/styles.css:867-941 (.causal-strip grid-template-columns 0.85fr 1.2fr 0.95fr; :921-930 `.causal-strip small { font-size: clamp(7px, calc(6.2857px + 0.2232vw), 8px) }`; :932-941 `.causal-strip strong { font-size: clamp(8px, calc(6.5714px + 0.4464vw), 10px) }`; :892-911 .causal-icon 23.4px ring, svg clamp 14-17px), :2638-2644 (@media max-width 359px override -> 10px / 12px), :2867-2869 (letter-spacing 0.08em), :1035-1037 (.mobile-approval .approval-context display:none); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/views/ChatView.jsx:57-78

**Viewports:** mobile 390, mobile 360, mobile 320, tablet 768 (8/10px), desktop 1440 (8/10px)

**Evidence:** Measured 360x740: `.causal-strip small` 7.09px (800, uppercase), `.causal-strip strong` 8.18px, line-height 10.2px; 'Encaixe no mesmo dia exige sua aprovação.' in an 80px column, 3 clamped lines, 31px tall. Measured 390: 7.16 / 8.31px in 84/118/94px cells, strip 308x56. 320: 10 / 12px via the ≤359 stacked override. 768 and 1440: 8 / 10px. Computed curve: 320 -> 10/12, 360 -> 7.09/8.18, 390 -> 7.16/8.31, 430 -> 7.25/8.49, 768+ -> 8/10 — the smallest phones are the only ones that can read it. Values are smaller than the 10px timestamps, the 11px caption and nav labels and the 9px kicker; value/chat ratio 0.73 vs the mock's 0.87-0.9 (mock values ≈8.6-10px at 390 scale against ≈10px chat). Because `.mobile-approval .approval-context` is display:none, 'Regra consultada / Encaixe no mesmo dia exige sua aprovação.' exists on the phone only in this 8.3px cell and the 11.3px bubble. Live experiment (a11y lens): small 8px -> no cell overflows ('Regra consultada' 89/89px, zero slack); strong 10px -> rule wraps to 3 lines inside the existing `-webkit-line-clamp: 3` with scrollHeight = clientHeight, strip 56 -> 70px; 11px -> 73px. Width budget with the shipped Archivo 600 (mock lens): column-2 text area 90.0px; 'Encaixe no mesmo dia' = 85.8px @8.31, 98.1 @9.5, 103.2 @10; 'sua decisão' 52.5px @9.5 vs column-3 area 65.3px. Severity: typography and IA lenses P1, a11y and mock lenses P2 (the bubble carries the same facts); P1 kept because on mobile this is the only place the consulted rule is shown as a rule, and the size curve inverts the intent of the fluid tokens.

**Why:** This strip is the approved hierarchy's proof that the agent consulted a rule instead of improvising; it is the reason the owner is being asked to decide. At 7-8px in daylight it is texture, not information — he will approve or refuse without reading the rule, and 'Aguardando · sua decisão', the one status he cares about, is the smallest text on the screen.

**Fix:** Copy unchanged; CSS only (JSX dl/dt/dd optional). 1) Base type, styles.css :921-941: `.causal-strip small { font-size: clamp(9px, calc(8.1px + 0.23vw), 10px); font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 0.04em; line-height: 1.2; white-space: normal; overflow: visible; text-overflow: clip; }` and `.causal-strip strong { font-size: clamp(11px, calc(9.9px + 0.28vw), 13px); line-height: var(--leading-compact); -webkit-line-clamp: 3; }` (390 -> 9/11 = chat size; 768 -> 9.9/12.05; 1440 -> 10/13). 2) Phone layout (<768, add inside the existing `@media (max-width: 767px)` block at :387 or as base): `.causal-strip { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, .8fr) minmax(0, 1.6fr); padding: 8px 6px; }` `.causal-strip > div:last-child { grid-column: 1 / -1; margin-top: 4px; padding-top: 4px; border-left: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); }` — use :last-child rather than nth-child(3) because CausalStrip (ChatView.jsx:60-70) renders the first two cells conditionally. Measured 360/375/390/430: no label overflow, no clamp, rule 2 lines at 11px, 'Aguardando / sua decisao' reads as a status bar; strip 99px vs 56-61 today. 3) >=768 (inside `@media (min-width: 768px)`, near :2859-2869): restore three columns `.causal-strip { grid-template-columns: 0.75fr 1.25fr 1fr; padding: 9px 6px; }` `.causal-strip > div:last-child { grid-column: auto; margin-top: 0; padding-top: 0; border-top: 0; border-left: 1px solid var(--border); }` and replace :2867 `letter-spacing: 0.08em` with `var(--tracking-label)`; the 690px chat column has room for 9.9/12px in 3 cells. 4) Delete only the two font-size overrides at :2638-2644 (keep the <=359 stacked grid/padding/icon rules) so 320 gets 9/11 and the curve is monotonic 9->10 / 11->13. 5) Drop the icon-ring loosening and the 3-column-on-phone variant from the original fix — the ring is 23.4px at 390 either way and the 3-column variant fails measurement at 360/375/390. 6) Fold cost is +38-43px on 360-430 (not +17); reconcile with F01 (at 390x844 the approval buttons already sit under the fixed composer at first paint, so F01's fix must absorb this). Re-verify after F01 by re-running the scrollWidth/scrollHeight check on `.causal-strip small/strong` at 360, 375, 390, 430.

**Verifier:** EVIDENCE CONFIRMED. The clamps at styles.css:924 and :935 compute exactly as claimed and I re-measured live on the prototype: 360 -> 7.09/8.18px, 375 -> 7.12/8.25, 390 -> 7.16/8.31, 430 -> 7.25/8.49; the <=359 block (:2557-2677, overrides at :2638-2644) forces 10/12px, and >=768 (:2692+, incl. the 0.08em tracking at :2867) yields 8/10. The inversion (320 gets ~40% larger type than 390) is real, and these are the smallest visible glyphs on the phone (owner in-bubble time is 8px; .approval-context span 8px is display:none on .mobile-approval, :1035-1037, so the rule appears as a labeled rule only in the strip). WHY PARTLY OVERSTATED: the agent bubble 60px above narrates the same rule at 11.3px ('Consultei nossa regra: para encaixe no mesmo dia e necessaria a sua aprovacao.') and 'sua decisao' is redundant with the card and its buttons, so the decision task is not made majorly harder; two of the four lenses already said P2. Also, the approved mock (853px ~ 2x of ~427) itself has strip labels ~7px and values ~8.2px at 1x, so the implementation is faithful to the mock's absolute sizes; the fix is a legibility improvement over the mock, which .impeccable.md explicitly permits ('Livre para melhorar: tipografia'). FIX FAILS AS WRITTEN. I injected the exact proposed CSS (0.75fr 1.25fr 1fr, padding 9px 6px, labels clamp 9-10px/700/0.04em/white-space normal/overflow visible, values clamp 11-13px, clamp 3) and measured scrollWidth vs clientWidth and scrollHeight vs clientHeight per cell: 360 -> 'LIGACAO' 44>41px overflow, 'AGUARDANDO' 70>62px overflow, rule at 11px CLAMPED (4th line hidden); 375 -> both labels overflow (44>43, 70>65); 390 (the finding's primary viewport) -> 'AGUARDANDO' 70>69 overflow; only 430 passes. With overflow:visible those single words spill into the cell border. Strip height goes 56 -> 89px at 390 (+33px, not the ~17px estimated). A 9/10px variant on the current grid also fails ('REGRA CONSULTADA' 97>89, 'AGUARDANDO' 67>64 at 390). Conclusion: three columns cannot hold 9px labels plus >=10px values below ~430px; the 7-8px type is what makes the grid fit. The finding's own IA alternative (2-row grid, Aguardando spanning under the two facts) is the only variant that passes at 360/375/390/430: zero label overflow, zero clamp, rule on 2 lines at 11px, strip 99px (+38-43px). Extending the shipped <=359 stack costs 146px (+86-90px) and is rejected. Severity lowered to P2 because the content is duplicated at readable size and the task completes without it; it stays high-P2 because an approved hierarchy block (the chain as proof of control) is non-functional at its own type size and the fix is cheap CSS.


### F05 · P1 · ADJUSTED — No type scale: 11 distinct sizes on the phone (nine between 7 and 13px in 3-16% steps, then a 54% jump), chat body 11.3px, and nothing grows between 768 and 1440 while the wordmark (28px) collides with the inspector h2 (28.8px)

**Where:** /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/styles.css:89-94 tokens (`--type-micro: 0.625rem`, `--type-small: 0.75rem`, `--type-body: clamp(0.75rem, calc(0.6607rem + 0.4464vw), 0.875rem)`, `--type-chat: clamp(0.6875rem, calc(0.5982rem + 0.4464vw), 0.8125rem)` — both hit their max at exactly 768px), :806-810 (`.message-row--owner .message-bubble p, .message-row--agent .message-bubble p { font-size: var(--type-chat); line-height: 1.42 }`), :2871-2873 (`.approval-facts dd { font-size: 13px }` hard-coded at ≥768), :3120-3125 (`.inspector-content > h2 { font-size: clamp(22px, 2vw, 30px) }`), :3023-3026 (`.sidebar .brand-lockup strong { font-size: 28px }`), :3066 (`.sidebar .destination-link` 12px), plus 40+ literal px declarations (13× 13px, 12× 10px, 9× 12px, 8× 9px, 7× 11px, 3× 8px — e.g. :350, :355, :370, :414, :504, :539, :812-815, :966, :1041, :1050, :1114, :1138, :1258, :1825, :2064, :2071, :2138, :2162, :2196, :2203, :2224, :2232, :3039, :3114, :3130)

**Viewports:** all (measured 360, 390, 720x450 @200%, 768, 1440)

**Evidence:** Measured distinct rendered sizes at 360x740 (visible text): 7.09, 8.18, 9, 10, 11, 11.18, 12, 12.18, 13, 20, 24.38px; at 390: 7.16, 8.31, 9, 10, 11, 11.31, 12, 12.31, 13, 20, 24.67. Four sizes live inside a 1.3px band (11 caption / 11.31 chat / 12 date+voice / 12.31 facts) from four different declarations; step ratios 1.16, 1.08, 1.11, 1.10, 1.03, 1.06, 1.03, 1.06, 1.54, 1.23. Chat 11.31px (line-height 16.06px) in both bubbles, still 12.8px at 720x450 (200% zoom); `--type-body` is 12.3px at 390. Desktop 1440x900 distinct sizes: 8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 28, 28.8, 34 — chat 13px in a 720-780px column (~55 chars/line), nothing between 14 and 28px; `.sidebar .brand-lockup strong` 28px/800 Archivo vs `.inspector-content > h2` 28.8px/700 Familjen Grotesk — two 'largest' elements 0.8px apart, different faces, different columns. The fluid tokens deliver their entire range between 320 and 768 (10.571 + 0.004464×768 = 14.0); from 768 to 1440 (1.9× wider) only the h1 grows (28.3 -> 34). styles.css grep: 22 font-size values, 16 letter-spacing values, 12 line-height values; `--leading-compact` and `--track-display` defined and never used; `--tracking-label` used once (:3179). Severity: typography lens P1 (0.8) for the scale, a11y lens P2 for the chat-size facet; P1 kept because chat body is the primary content and this single root cause drives F04, F09, F16 and F17.

**Why:** The owner reads one thing here — what the agent said and what it wants. 11.3px Archivo 500 on a white bubble is comfortable at a desk and blurs at arm's length in sunlight; because everything secondary sits 1-2px away from it, nothing pops except the 24px title. On a laptop he sits farther from the screen yet the text is barely larger than on the phone while the empty paper and navy grow — the composition looks sized for a 6-inch screen inside a 15-inch layout. Fewer, larger, clearly separated steps are what let him find the message, the rule and the card in a two-second glance.

**Fix:** Same five-role scale, but implemented without breaking other views and with the full #ligou mapping. Tokens: ADD alongside the existing ones (do not delete --type-small or re-value --type-micro — they feed 26 and 20 declarations, most in Memory/Approvals/Settings/Voice-live/Login): `--type-label: 11px; --type-body: clamp(13px, calc(11.98px + 0.2625vw), 15px); /* 390->13.0 · 768->14.0 (never below today's 14) · >=1150->15 */ --type-chat: var(--type-body); --type-lead: clamp(15px, calc(13.9px + 0.28vw), 18px); --type-subhead: clamp(20px, calc(18.4px + 0.4vw), 24px);` keep --type-title, --leading-*, --tracking-label as they are. Re-point ONLY #ligou selectors: label/uppercase (keep current weights): .message-label, .approval-kicker, .inspector-kicker, .approval-context span, .business-card span, .choice-grid legend, .comparison-grid span, .desktop-timeline-handoff span, .causal-strip small (:924, replace the 7-8px clamp; if the 3-column strip at 390 cannot hold 'REGRA CONSULTADA' at 11px/0.1em, stack it as the <=359 layout already does — or defer to the finding that owns the causal strip and say so). label/400: .message-bubble time, .owner-message-time, .business-compact span, .business-card small, .approval-facts dd small, .client-tag, .sending-status, .prototype-badge, .bottom-nav .destination-link (replaces --type-small at :434; keep the <=359 10px exception). body: .message-bubble p (:801 and :806-810, line-height var(--leading-body)), .causal-strip strong (:936, replaces the 8-10px clamp), .approval-facts dd (delete the 13px override :2871-2873), .approval-context p, .approval-caption, .page-heading > p, .dialog-heading p, .choice-grid small, .inspector-content > p, .voice-demo-button (:1238 itself, not a span), .system-message (:858), .business-compact strong, .sidebar .destination-link (:3066), .reset-button, .profile-menu-panel span/button, .button (:1063) and .approval-actions button (:1064 and :2674). lead: .desktop-timeline-handoff strong, .choice-grid strong, .approval-empty strong (new rule; markup exists at ApprovalCard.jsx:16). subhead: .dialog-heading h2, .inspector-content > h2 { font-size: var(--type-subhead); line-height: var(--leading-tight); } replacing clamp(22px,2vw,30px). title: .page-heading h1 unchanged; brand stays 28px. Delete the dead rule :812-815. Expect the agent bubble at 390 to grow ~+33px (4 -> 5 lines at 19.5px), so pair with the F01 width change. Verify after: at 390 the visible #ligou set should be 11 / 13 / 15 / 20 / 24.7 (+ the 20px mobile wordmark), and no Memory/Approvals/Settings selector changes computed size.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and reproducible from the CSS: --type-chat clamp(0.6875rem, 0.5982rem+0.4464vw, 0.8125rem) yields 11.18px@360, 11.31px@390, 12.79px@720 and maxes at vw=768.1 exactly as claimed; --type-body 12.31@390; h1 24.67@390 -> 34@1440; inspector h2 clamp(22px,2vw,30px)=28.8@1440 vs .sidebar .brand-lockup strong 28px/800 (:3023). Grep inventory matches to the digit (22 font-size values; 13x13px, 12x10px, 9x12px, 8x9px, 7x11px, 3x8px; 16 letter-spacing; 12 line-height; --leading-compact and --track-display unused; --tracking-label used once at :3179). The brief's own geometry JSONs and flow-observations independently record chat 11.31, causal 7.16/8.31, kicker 9, facts 12.31, caption/nav 11, date 12, desktop 13/28/28.8/34. The :812 rule is truly dead: ChatView.jsx:47 renders the owner <time> outside .message-bubble. The 'why' holds for the stated user (phone, outdoors) and the brand contract lists typography as 'livre para melhorar'. BUT the fix is not implementable as written: (1) its :root block drops --type-small, which has 26 consumers — 4 inside #ligou (.destination-link :434, .system-message :858, .voice-demo-button :1238, .approval-actions button :2674) and ~20 in Memory/Approvals/Settings/Voice-live/Login — and re-valuing --type-micro 10->11px leaks into 13 out-of-view selectors; (2) the two smallest phone sizes, .causal-strip small/strong clamps at :924/:936 (7.16/8.31px), and .prototype-badge (10px) are absent from the mapping, so '11 sizes -> 5' is false; (3) the proposed --type-body (12.26px+0.19vw) is SMALLER than today's --type-body across 768-915px (13.72 vs 14.0 at 768); (4) .voice-demo-button span has no rule (size lives on the parent), .approval-empty strong has markup (ApprovalCard.jsx:16) but no rule yet, and the agent-bubble growth is ~+33px (4x16.06 -> 5x19.5), not +18. Severity: P1 is justified only by the chat/facts size for the primary content; the consolidation and the 28/28.8 collision alone are P2/P3. Note the approved mock (853px = 2.19x of 390) itself shows chat text at ~16px CSS line pitch, i.e. ~11-12px equivalent — the implementation matches the approval; the fix is still a legitimate evolution but should be framed that way.


### F06 · P2 · ADJUSTED — All three action icons collapse (Aprovar check ~3px, Ajustar ~8px, Recusar ~1px) at 390 because the svg has no flex-shrink:0 and the 3-column cell cannot hold icon + label

**Where:** /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/styles.css:1054-1083 (.approval-actions grid repeat(3, minmax(0,1fr)) gap 6px; .approval-actions button padding 0 13px, gap 6px, font-size 13px; .button svg / .approval-actions button svg width/height 17px without flex-shrink:0), :711-715 (.timeline-content--approval margin-left -8px), :2655-2675 (2-column fallback only ≤359px); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/components/ApprovalCard.jsx:53-63

**Viewports:** mobile 390, mobile 360-389

**Evidence:** Measured on .mobile-approval at 390: buttons 86.66-87px wide; label advances at 13px/700 Archivo: Aprovar 49.6-49.8, Ajustar 44.8, Recusar 51.9-52.1px; inner width 86.7 - 26 padding - 2 border = 58.7px < 17 + 6 + 51.9 = 74.9 -> the svg is the only shrinkable flex item and absorbs the deficit: rendered svg widths 3.0 / 7.9 / 0.75-0.8px (height still 17). Visible in the first-paint screenshot as a dot beside 'Aprovar' and no icon at all on 'Recusar'. At 320 (2-col, Aprovar full width) and in the 1440 inspector (108px cells) the icons are 17px. Below 381px the 3-col cannot hold icon + label at all. Card is 300px wide at 390 (x 68-368); the mock's card spans 62-368 (306px) with 90x35 buttons and legible 12.8-13.3px icons. Injected fix verified by the a11y lens: `svg{flex:0 0 auto}` + `button{padding:0 6px;gap:5px}` -> all three icons 17px, scrollWidth 85 = clientWidth 85, no overflow. Severity: layout lens P1 (0.98) vs interaction/IA/a11y/mock lenses P2; P1 kept because the defect is visible on the primary control across 360-430px (nearly every phone), removes the non-colour cue that distinguishes Aprovar from Recusar (brand guide p.17: colour is never the only indicator) and the layout lens' budget math also covers the 360-389 range the others did not.

**Why:** The icon is the fastest recogniser for a sunlit thumb-tap; a squeezed sliver looks like a rendering bug on the primary control and undermines trust in exactly the moment the product asks for a decision. Approve vs refuse is left to colour plus a 7-letter word at 13px.

**Fix:** Global, unconditional: `.button svg, .approval-actions button svg { flex: 0 0 auto; }` (styles.css:1079-1083). Inside `@media (max-width: 767px)` (new rules, or append to the existing block at styles.css:387): `.timeline-content--approval { margin-left: -14px; }` (card x 62-368 = 306px, matches mock); `.timeline-approval-node::after { width: calc(var(--timeline-rail-column) / 2 + var(--timeline-column-gap) - 12px); }` (connector keeps today's 8px plug-in into the card instead of growing to 14px; drop this line if F22 already re-derives the connector); `.mobile-approval .approval-card { padding-inline: 12px; }` (inner 282, cells 90px); `.approval-actions button { padding-inline: 6px; gap: 5px; min-width: 0; }`; `.approval-actions button svg { flex: 0 0 16px; width: 16px; height: 16px; }`. Budget at 390: 90 - 2 border - 12 padding = 76 >= 16 + 5 + 52.05 = 73.05 (2.95px slack). Then add a new `@media (max-width: 389px)` block containing only the approval rules currently at styles.css:2655-2675 (`.approval-actions { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); }`, `.approval-actions .button--primary { grid-column: 1 / -1; }`, `.approval-actions button { padding-inline: 8px; font-size: var(--type-small); }`) and delete them from the <=359 block (leave the header/nav/causal rules there). Do NOT use the 12px-label one-row alternative: at 360 the cell is 80px -> 66px content < 69.05 needed. Keep min-height 44px, colours, copy and button order unchanged; verify at 360/375/390/430 that every svg reports 16px and each button's scrollWidth equals clientWidth.

**Verifier:** Evidence is real and reproducible from the CSS alone. At 390: .conversation padding 14 -> 362; .operational-timeline rail 52 + gap 10 -> content column 300 starting x 76; .timeline-content--approval margin -8/+8 -> card x 68-368, 300px (matches mobile-390x844-geometry.json approvalCard.box). .approval-card padding-inline 14 -> 272 inner; repeat(3, minmax(0,1fr)) gap 6 -> 86.67px cells (JSON: 87); button padding 0 13 + 1px border -> 58.67px content. Advance widths from the shipped public/fonts/archivo-v25-700.ttf at 13px: Aprovar 49.82, Ajustar 44.81, Recusar 52.05 (matches the finding's 49.6-52.1). The label is a bare text node (ApprovalCard.jsx:55/58/61) whose min-content is the whole word, and no rule anywhere in styles.css gives .button svg / .approval-actions button svg flex-shrink:0 (grep of every flex:0 0 auto hit: none targets button svgs), so the svg absorbs the deficit: 58.67-6-49.82 = 2.85, -44.81 = 7.86, -52.05 = 0.62px -> the measured 3.0/7.9/0.75. With current CSS a 17px icon needs a cell >= 103px, i.e. viewport >= 439px, so even 430 renders a 14px icon; the 320 (2-col) and 1440 inspector (108px cells) exceptions are consistent. The mock (853px, checked) shows one row of ~90px buttons with ~12.8px icons and the card edge at ~x62 scaled, so the fix's card widening is fidelity, not redesign. What does not hold: (1) severity. Labels 'Aprovar/Ajustar/Recusar' at 13px/700 stay fully legible, 44px tall and tappable; the owner completes the decision with no difficulty. The claim that the collapse 'removes the non-colour cue' is wrong: the text label is the non-colour cue (WCAG 1.4.1 and brand p.17 'texto e forma' are carried by the text). This is a visible glitch on the primary control = P2 (annoyance/trust), as 4 of 5 lenses said; breadth of affected viewports does not raise impact to 'major difficulty'. (2) The one-row alternative (12px labels <=399) does not close the budget: at 360 with the full fix the cell is (360-28-62+6-24-12)/3 = 80, content 66 < 16+5+48.05 = 69.05, and its '76.7 -> 68.7' arithmetic mixes padding assumptions; only the 2-column extension works below ~381. (3) margin-left:-14px moves the card edge 6px left but .timeline-approval-node::after (width rail/2+gap-6px = 30px, ends x 76, z-index 3 over card z-index 1) is untouched, so the connector would overdraw 14px of the card instead of today's 8px; the fix depends on F22 or its own -6px correction. (4) .approval-card{padding-inline:12px} and the margin change should be scoped to <=767px; desktop is already overridden by .context-inspector .approval-card{padding:18px} but the 768-1199 inline card (564-654px) gains nothing from it.


### F07 · P2 · ADJUSTED — After a decision, keyboard/screen-reader focus lands on the NEXT case's Aprovar button with no announcement of the switch

**Where:** /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/components/Dialog.jsx:56-61 (return focus to trigger if isConnected); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/App.jsx:243 (pendingApproval = pendingApprovals[0]); /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/components/ApprovalCard.jsx:23-31, 54-62; /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/views/ChatView.jsx:27 (.system-message), :206 (aria-live on the whole .conversation, aria-relevant='additions text')

**Viewports:** all (measured 390x844 and 1440x900)

**Evidence:** Measured right after 'Confirmar aprovação' for John Miller: document.activeElement = BUTTON.button.button--primary (text 'Aprovar') — inside .mobile-approval on mobile, inside .context-inspector on desktop — and the card now reads 'Maria Alvarez'. React reuses the same DOM node for the new approval, so trigger.isConnected is true and Dialog hands focus to a button that now approves a different client. The live region announces the dd text diffs and the system message, never 'próxima decisão: Maria Alvarez'. After 'Recusar proposta' (last pending) focus correctly falls to MAIN#main-content. Severity: interaction lens P1 (0.92) vs a11y lens P2 (0.85); P1 kept — both lenses agree a double Enter approves a case the user has not read, which is a business outcome, not a cosmetic defect.

**Why:** A keyboard or switch user who presses Enter twice (Confirmar, then the returned focus) opens approval for a case he has not read; a screen-reader user hears 'Aprovar, botão' as the outcome of his decision. For an owner who acts fast this is the most dangerous focus outcome possible — the one a11y defect here that changes a business result.

**Fix:** 1) ApprovalCard.jsx: `const clientId = useId();` (React 19; must be per-instance because the card is rendered twice, .mobile-approval and .context-inspector, so approval.id alone would duplicate ids). Add `id={clientId}` to the client <dd> (line 31) and, on the three buttons (lines 54-62), `data-approval-id={approval.id}` and `aria-describedby={clientId}`, so "Aprovar, botão" is followed by "Maria Alvarez Novo cliente" — visible copy untouched. Drop the <article aria-labelledby/aria-describedby> idea. 2) Dialog.jsx:22 store `returnFocusRef.current = { el: document.activeElement, key: document.activeElement?.dataset?.approvalId ?? null }`. In the cleanup (lines 58-60): `const { el, key } = returnFocusRef.current || {}; if (el?.isConnected && (el.dataset?.approvalId ?? null) === key) el.focus?.(); else ([...document.querySelectorAll('.conversation .system-message')].at(-1) || document.getElementById('main-content'))?.focus?.();` — no requestAnimationFrame and nothing in App.jsx perform(): perform() awaits refresh() before setDialog(null) (App.jsx:251-253), so the new .system-message is already committed when the Dialog cleanup runs, and Ajustar (same case still pending, key matches) keeps returning to its own button. Avoid `:last-of-type`. 3) ChatView.jsx:27 add `tabIndex={-1}` to the .system-message div (it already has role="status", so focusing it reads the confirmation sentence). No new CSS needed: the default 3px ring is already --teal (#0b655a) on paper; if a rule is added for clarity keep it `outline-color: var(--teal)` (teal = confirmation), never orange.

**Verifier:** The mechanism is real and reproduced live (local prototype, 390x844, my own tab, localStorage restored afterwards): ChatView.jsx:107 and App.jsx:311 render <ApprovalCard approval={pendingApproval}> with no key, so when pendingApprovals[0] (App.jsx:243) switches from John to Maria React updates the same <article>/<button> nodes; Dialog.jsx:58-59 therefore sees trigger.isConnected === true and returns focus to BUTTON.button--primary "Aprovar" inside .mobile-approval, whose card now reads "Maria Alvarez Novo cliente" (badge 2 -> 1). The consequence that justified P1 is false, though: a second Enter does NOT approve anything, it opens the approve Dialog (App.jsx:484-487) titled "Aprovar esta decisão" with description "Defina se a decisão para Maria Alvarez vale só agora ou também vira uma regra." (announced via aria-labelledby/aria-describedby), initial focus lands on the "Fechar" X (Dialog.jsx:24-25), and a third Enter closes it; Maria remained pending (badge still 1). Approving the unread case needs Tab x3 + Enter after a dialog that names the client. Also overstated: the outcome IS narrated: Toast (App.jsx:49, role="status") says "Decisão aprovada somente para este caso." and a role="status" system message "Você aprovou o pedido de John Miller..." is appended inside the aria-live .conversation; what is missing is only the narration that the card switched cases. Keyboard/SR disorientation without a business outcome = P2, which the a11y lens itself assigned. The fix is directionally right but has three defects: (1) the unconditional requestAnimationFrame in App.jsx perform() would fight Dialog's own return-focus (perform also runs for memory edit/revoke, reset, voice phrase) and would steal focus even when the trigger is legitimately the same case (Ajustar keeps the case pending, so returning to "Ajustar" is correct); (2) `.system-message:last-of-type` is an element-type selector (div among div.message-row siblings): it matched in the prototype only because the system message is the last div before span.conversation-end, and fails whenever an agent reply follows; (3) `.system-message:focus-visible { outline-color: var(--teal) }` is a no-op since :root --focus-ring-color (#0b655a, styles.css:132) already equals --teal (styles.css:80), and aria-labelledby on the <article> is unreliable because screen readers do not announce an article's name when Tab lands on a button inside it.


### F08 · P2 · ADJUSTED — The only text field has its focus indicator removed (outline:0 on the input, no :focus-within on the pill)

**Where:** /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/styles.css:1193-1203 (.composer input { outline: 0 }) vs :215-218 (:focus-visible 3px ring); grep: no focus-within anywhere in the file

**Viewports:** all

**Evidence:** Measured with #chat-input focused: outlineStyle 'none', outlineWidth 0px, boxShadow none; .composer border rgba(4,29,39,.08) and box-shadow unchanged while :focus-within is true. Specificity: `.composer input` (0,1,1) beats `:focus-visible` (0,1,0). Tabbing from Recusar to the input produces no visible change; every button on the page does show the 3px ring. Severity: a11y lens P1 (measured, WCAG 2.4.7 fail on the primary input) vs IA lens P2; P1 kept on the measured evidence — a hard AA failure on the one text field of the view.

**Why:** A sighted keyboard user (and anyone using an external keyboard on a tablet, or the desktop inspector layout where keyboard is a normal path) loses the focus position at the composer.

**Fix:** Add one rule after `.composer input` in /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/styles.css (around line 1204), keeping `outline: 0` on the input: `.composer:has(input:focus-visible) { border-color: var(--teal); box-shadow: var(--shadow-float), 0 0 0 3px var(--focus-ring-color); }`. Use the opaque token (same #0b655a ring as every button, ~6.9:1 on paper, passes 1.4.11), not a 35% alpha halo; scope with `:has(input:focus-visible)` instead of `:focus-within` so Anexar/Voz/Enviar do not get a second ring around the pill on top of their own. The 3px shadow follows the 28px radius and layers over the existing --shadow-float without changing layout. Verify by tabbing into #chat-input at 390x844 and 1440x900 and reading getComputedStyle(.composer).boxShadow / borderColor.

**Verifier:** Evidence confirmed in source: /Users/d1f/.codex/worktrees/ligou-v0.2-browser-pilot/Ligou.AI/dashboard/src/styles.css:1193-1203 sets `.composer input { outline: 0 }` (specificity 0,1,1) which beats the global `:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid var(--focus-ring-color) }` at :215 (0,1,0); grep finds no `:focus-within`, no `:has(input` and no `caret-color` anywhere; the composer media overrides at :2686, :2818, :2875-2886 add no focus state. So the pill shows no ring/border/shadow change on focus, exactly as measured. But two things are overstated.
